### [1] [$g$MAGNUS: Fast SpGEMM on GPUs for Irregular Matrices via Hierarchical Multisplit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22866)
*Jordi Wolfson-Pou,Ahmed Helal,Fabrizio Petrini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出gMAGNUS算法，通过行内重排序和分层多分割策略解决GPU上不规则稀疏矩阵乘法中重行的全局内存溢出问题，在Intel和NVIDIA新架构上相比五种主流算法实现1.81-7.62倍几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则稀疏矩阵中存在大量重行（heavy rows），其大量中间积导致局部内存累加器溢出到全局内存，严重降低SpGEMM性能。

Method: 对中间积进行行内重排序，将重行细分为可在局部内存中完全累加的独立块；利用新颖的外积和分层多分割操作实现；算法自动根据输入矩阵维度和局部内存大小确定块数和多分割层级。

Result: 在两个大规模数据集上，相比MKL、cuSPARSE等五种领先算法，在Intel Ponte Vecchio和NVIDIA H200上实现1.81-7.62倍几何平均加速；核心kernel性能接近理论上限。

Conclusion: gMAGNUS有效解决了不规则稀疏矩阵SpGEMM中重行的全局内存瓶颈，具有输入感知和系统感知的自适应能力，性能显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We present $g$MAGNUS, a novel algorithm for sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpGEMM) of irregular matrices on GPUs. Such matrices often contain many \emph{heavy rows}, those with large intermediate products that force local memory accumulators to spill to global memory. $g$MAGNUS addresses this by computing an intra-row reordering of intermediate products, subdividing heavy rows into independent chunks that can be accumulated completely in local memory. This reordering uses novel outer product and hierarchical multisplit operations. The algorithm is input- and system-aware, automatically determining the number of chunks and multisplit levels based on the input matrix dimensions and local memory size. Experimental results on two extensive datasets show that $g$MAGNUS achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 1.81 to 7.62$\times$ over five leading algorithms (including MKL and cuSPARSE) on Intel Ponte Vecchio and NVIDIA H200. Additionally, the core kernels of $g$MAGNUS are evaluated, achieving near-peak performance compared to their theoretical upper bound.

</details>


### [2] [Application-Driven Architecture Exploration for Cross-Layer Heterogeneous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23042)
*Yuchen Fan,Minghong Sun,Jikui Ma,Yunpeng Xu,Shunyu Mao,Liu He,Shunan Dong,Jiahao Yang,Yu Zhu,Xinhao Yang,Tianyan Zhong,Haoran Sun,Daoqi Liu,Zongle Huang,Xinyuan Lin,Huazhong Yang,Maokun Li,Yongpan Liu,Yu Wang,Zhenhua Zhu,Hongyang Jia,Shuwen Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CHASE是一个应用驱动的跨层异构系统（XHS）架构搜索框架，通过解耦的两级循环（内层映射器+外层优化器）在满足物理部署约束的前提下，为稀疏计算和LLM工作负载搜索最优硬件架构，分别实现6.20×和2.12×的几何平均加速，同时降低成本和功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI和HPC基础设施需要支持包含密集张量计算、稀疏核、大内存占用和通信密集型集合的工作负载组合，而跨层异构系统（XHS）设计空间巨大且受物理约束（如功耗、交换基数、布线、成本），现有方法难以有效探索。

Method: 将候选架构表示为层次化类型图，采用解耦的两级循环搜索：(1) 内层映射器将硬件无关的工作流DAG转换为拓扑感知的事件轨迹，经校准的事件驱动模拟器评估；(2) 外层遥测引导优化器迭代进化硬件图，拒绝违反部署约束的设计。

Result: 映射器在6.06%内接近穷举最优，映射时间平均减少60.5%；计算模型误差4.4-7.5%；外层搜索64次迭代内达到近全局最优。端到端案例：稀疏工作负载偏好关键性感知异构pod（6.20×加速），LLM推理偏好scale-up岛（2.12×加速），同时降低成本和功耗。

Conclusion: CHASE有效解决了跨层异构系统设计空间搜索难题，证明了应用驱动的架构搜索能够在满足物理约束的前提下找到显著优于基线的高效XHS架构，且不同工作负载类型需要不同的异构组织策略。

Abstract: AI and HPC infrastructure increasingly serves workload portfolios that combine dense tensor computation, sparse kernels, large memory footprints, and communication-intensive collectives. Supporting these portfolios requires coordinated choices across accelerators, memory tiers, scale-up fabrics, and cluster networks. The resulting Cross-layer Heterogeneous System (XHS) design space is difficult to explore: hardware choices change legal task mappings, while rack power, switch radix, cabling, and cost constraints invalidate many candidates. We present CHASE, an application-driven framework that searches physically feasible XHS architectures through the workloads they must execute. CHASE represents candidates as hierarchical typed graphs and rejects designs that violate deployment constraints. It avoids intractable joint hardware-mapping search with a decoupled two-level loop: an inner mapper translates hardware-independent workload DAGs into topology-aware event traces, a calibrated event-driven simulator evaluates each mapping, and an outer telemetry-guided optimizer evolves the hardware graph. We evaluate CHASE on sparse-computing and LLM workloads. Its mapper remains within 6.06% of exhaustive optima while reducing mapping time by 60.5% on average relative to PEFT. Compute-model errors average 4.4-7.5%, and communication validation reproduces key trends across physical platforms. The outer search reaches near-global optima within 64 iterations. End-to-end case studies show that sparse workloads favor criticality-aware heterogeneous pods, whereas LLM inference favors scale-up islands; the resulting designs deliver 6.20$\times$ and 2.12$\times$ geomean speedups, respectively, while reducing cost and power relative to the baselines.

</details>


### [3] [Gleam: Adaptive Network-Efficient CUDA API Remoting for Cross-Device GPU Sharing over LANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23115)
*Zhihao Xu,Hao Zhong,Zeting Zhou,Yuhang Xu,Haoyu Tong,Wei Wang,Jinshan Chen,Keqiang He,Chong Zhu,Shengzhong Liu,Fan Wu,Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Gleam，一个面向局域网内异构个人设备的高效GPU共享框架，通过CUDA API远程调用实现分布式AI推理，重点解决网络带宽、高频API调用和任务争用三大瓶颈，实验表明在API远程调用效率和系统吞吐量上分别提升1.4-24.2倍和最高1.79倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU共享方案受限于局域网内有限的网络带宽、高频率的API调用开销以及跨任务间的资源争用，严重制约了异构个人设备间分布式AI推理的性能，亟需一种网络高效的通用GPU共享方案。

Method: 1) 通过自动模型权重缓存降低CUDA API远程调用的带宽开销，并采用异步执行缓解高频API调用带来的累积延迟；2) 设计运行时任务调度器，动态确定LAN客户端与服务器之间的API远程调用配对，同时考虑网络条件和并行工作负载下的GPU资源争用；3) 引入专用机制确保跨分布式执行的CUDA上下文一致性。

Result: 在异构NVIDIA GPU和多种AI工作负载上的实验表明，Gleam相比现有最优基线方法，API远程调用效率提升1.4-24.2倍，系统吞吐量最高提升1.79倍。

Conclusion: Gleam通过模型权重缓存、异步执行、动态调度和上下文一致性保障等机制，有效解决了局域网GPU共享中的网络瓶颈问题，显著提升了异构设备间分布式AI推理的效率和吞吐量。

Abstract: This paper aims to enable computation- and communication-efficient GPU sharing across devices within local area networks (LANs), facilitating ubiquitous AI inference on heterogeneous personal devices. We achieve distributed task offloading via CUDA API remoting. However, beyond raw computation, network constraints emerge as the primary bottleneck: limited bandwidth, high-frequency API invocations, and cross-task contention significantly hinder performance. To address these challenges, we propose Gleam, a novel and network-efficient framework for task-generic GPU sharing across local-area CUDA devices, with three key contributions. First, we reduce bandwidth overhead in CUDA API remoting through automatic model weight caching, and mitigate accumulated latency from frequent API calls by asynchronous execution. Second, we design a runtime task scheduler that dynamically determines API remoting pairs between LAN clients and servers, explicitly accounting for both network conditions and GPU resource contention under parallel workloads. Finally, we introduce dedicated mechanisms to ensure CUDA context consistency across distributed executions. Extensive experiments on heterogeneous NVIDIA GPUs and diverse AI workloads show Gleam consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving 1.4-24.2 times improvements in API remoting efficiency and up to 1.79 times higher system throughput.

</details>


### [4] [Libra: Taming Attention Workload Skew in Long-Context LLM Training with Bounded Sequence Pool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23250)
*Yan Wang,Xiulong Yuan,Kaiming Yang,Jiaxuan Peng,Pengju Lu,Mingzhen Li,Zhipeng Zhang,Chang Si,Zhixiang Ruan,Hongqing Chen,Linlang Jiang,Siyu Wang,Langshi Chen,Rui Men,Man Yuan,Guangming Tan,Yong Li,Weile Jia,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Libra提出基于大数定律（LLN）的负载均衡缩放原则，通过定长序列池和方差缩减的序列放置策略，解决长上下文LLM训练中注意力计算负载不均衡问题，在Qwen3-Turbo训练中实现最高2.54倍端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文LLM训练中，序列打包无法解决负载均衡问题：由于注意力计算成本与序列长度平方和成正比，长尾分布下等长打包序列的注意力工作量差异巨大，导致数据并行中的掉队者和流水线气泡问题。现有方法要么粒度过粗难以处理异常值，要么需要全局通信域随数据并行度增长。

Method: Libra方法包括：1）基于大数定律的缩放原则——注意力均衡池大小不随DP度增长；2）方差缩减的序列放置策略（VRSP），将互补注意力工作量的序列放在同一池中减少池间偏斜；3）平铺注意力池化（Tiled Attention Pooling），在池内将序列-头平铺块跨GPU调度，配合流水线运行时重叠块传输与注意力计算。

Result: 在Qwen3-Turbo的256K和1M token训练中，Libra相比Ulysses实现最高2.54倍端到端吞吐提升；微基准测试中最差步骤的掉队者注意力加速达3.14倍；已在生产环境中运行数十万GPU小时，支持32K到1M token任务，且保持训练语义不变。

Conclusion: Libra作为一种即插即用的上下文并行注意力算子和可插拔数据采样器，无需修改模型层、优化器或流水线调度，有效解决了长上下文训练中的注意力负载均衡问题，显著提升了训练吞吐。

Abstract: Long-context LLM training suffers from a load-balancing problem that sequence packing does not solve. Packing samples into fixed-token sequences balances memory and linear-cost operators, but the dominant attention cost scales with the sum of squared sequence lengths. Thus, equally sized packed sequences drawn from a long-tailed corpus can carry substantially different attention workloads, creating data-parallel stragglers and pipeline bubbles. Existing approaches either balance at the granularity of sequences or microbatches, where an outlier can dominate an assignment, or disaggregate attention over a global worker pool whose communication domain grows with the data-parallel (DP) degree.
  We present Libra, which operationalizes the law of large numbers (LLN) as a scaling principle for load balancing: the attention-balancing pool need not grow with the DP degree. Libra groups packed sequences and their CP groups into fixed-size sequence pools. As DP scales out, Libra adds pools rather than enlarging each one, bounding every attention exchange. Variance-Reduced Sequence Placement makes this effective for finite, long-tailed workloads by co-locating sequences with complementary attention workloads to reduce residual inter-pool skew. Within each pool, Tiled Attention Pooling dispatches sequence-head SH-Tiles across GPUs, while a pipelined runtime overlaps tile exchange with attention.
  Libra exposes a drop-in context-parallel attention operator and a pluggable data sampler, requiring no changes to model layers, optimizers, or pipeline schedules. On Qwen3-Turbo training with 256K- and 1M-token workloads, Libra improves end-to-end throughput by up to 2.54x over Ulysses, with up to 3.14x worst-step straggler-attention speedup in microbenchmarks. Libra has run for hundreds of thousands of GPU-hours in production on jobs spanning 32K to 1M tokens while preserving training semantics.

</details>


### [5] [X-Stage: An Overlooked Pipeline Stage for Communication-Computation Overlap in DiT Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23264)
*Jianwen Xian,Zhiyuan Xu,Yuchen Li,Ziliang Lai,Kang He,Zhen Huang,Aichen Feng,Jinyan Chen,Yilin Zhang,Qinqin Chen,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种软件可见的post-issue流水线阶段（X-Stage），通过Burst-Gap模型预测通信开销，并基于此重新设计了DeepGEMM MegaMoE和Ulysses序列并行注意力两个通信-计算融合内核，实现了最高1.62x和1.43x的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在分布式DiT推理中调度通信的发出和接收数据的可用时间点，但忽略了从发出到远端可见完成之间的post-issue进度，导致发送端背压难以预测。

Method: 识别X-Stage阶段，通过测量分析提出Burst-Gap模型（参数化无背压发出时间、有效排出速率和未完成容量），并据此重新设计两个通信-计算融合内核：交错Linear-1/Linear-2工作以分散突发存储，以及磁贴粒度融合All-to-All与FlashAttention。

Result: DeepGEMM MegaMoE在84个配置上几何平均加速1.18x、最大1.62x；Ulysses注意力中FlashAttention-3和-4分别达到1.43x和1.42x的发送端加速，长序列稳态时间接近纯FlashAttention。

Conclusion: post-issue进度是可测量的调度杠杆，可用于塑造突发、避免背压和隐藏发送端开销。

Abstract: Fine-grained, device-initiated communication lets persistent GPU kernels in distributed diffusion transformer (DiT) inference issue remote stores and overlap data movement with Tensor Core computation. Existing systems schedule when communication is issued and when received data becomes consumable, but omit post-issue progress before remote-visible completion, making sender backpressure hard to predict.
  We identify X-Stage, a software-visible post-issue pipeline stage. Measurements on an eight-GPU node with a recent NVIDIA architecture show that short remote-store bursts drain as the issuer resumes work, whereas sustained injection exhausts finite outstanding capacity and delays later issues. A lightweight Burst-Gap model parameterized by backpressure-free issue time, effective drain rate, and outstanding capacity predicts issue overhead, recovery between bursts, and the onset of backpressure.
  Guided by the model, we redesign two communication-computation fused kernels. For DeepGEMM MegaMoE, interleaving Linear-1 and Linear-2 work across expert waves places computation between concentrated remote-store bursts, yielding a 1.18x geometric-mean and 1.62x maximum kernel speedup over the Expert-Wave baseline across 84 configurations. For Ulysses sequence-parallel attention, tile-granular fusion of the post-attention All-to-All with FlashAttention lets an output-tile owner issue remote stores and resume computation without a dedicated communication warp or streaming multiprocessor. FlashAttention-3 and FlashAttention-4 reach maximum sender-visible speedups of 1.43x and 1.42x over serial execution, and at long sequences their steady-state times approach those of FlashAttention alone. These results establish post-issue progress as a measurable scheduling lever for shaping bursts, avoiding backpressure, and hiding sender-side overhead.

</details>


### [6] [SpecBox: Speculative Sandbox Scheduling for Efficient LLM Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23933)
*Yihui Zhang,Tianyu Wo,Jinghao Wang,Xiaoyang Sun,Menghao Zhang,Cangzhou Yuan,Li Li,Chunming Hu,Albert Y. Zomaya,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SpecBox 是一个基于推测性沙箱预分配的运行时系统，通过意图驱动的沙箱预预热、上下文感知的随机预取、语义结果缓存和零拷贝共享内存传输，解决了 LLM 代理中资源利用与交互延迟之间的矛盾，相比按需沙箱基线，P99 端到端延迟降低最多 2.9 倍，内存消耗相比永久预留方案减少 45.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理依赖 MCP 调用隔离的外部沙箱，但解耦式沙箱部署导致资源利用与交互尾延迟之间存在根本性矛盾：长时预留沙箱内存开销过大，而按需实例化会产生严重的冷启动惩罚，损害多租户、多轮代理工作负载下的响应性能。

Method: SpecBox 包含三个核心机制：(1) 意图驱动的沙箱预预热——通过关键词匹配和流式语义嵌入在 LLM 令牌生成过程中识别待执行的工具需求，使沙箱启动与模型推理完全重叠；(2) 上下文感知随机预取——基于沙箱依赖图概率性预测未来沙箱切换；(3) 两方面优化——语义结果缓存消除重复沙箱调用，以及专用带外共享内存传输平面实现零拷贝工件传输。

Result: 在高并发多轮代理轨迹评估中，SpecBox 的 P99 端到端延迟比按需沙箱基线降低最多 2.9 倍，峰值内存消耗比永久预留沙箱部署减少 45.9%。

Conclusion: SpecBox 通过推测性沙箱预分配有效解决了资源利用与延迟之间的矛盾，证明了在保持低延迟的同时大幅降低内存开销是可行的。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly rely on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to invoke isolated external sandboxes, disaggregated sandbox deployment introduces a fundamental tension between resource utilization and interactive tail latency. Persistent long-lived sandbox reservations incur excessive memory overhead at scale, while lazy on-demand instantiation generates severe cold-start penalties that degrade response performance under multi-tenant, multi-turn agent workloads. To resolve this dilemma, we present SpecBox, a runtime built around speculative sandbox preallocation tailored for dynamic LLM agent execution pipelines.
  At its core, SpecBox implements keyword matching and streaming semantic embedding to enable intent-driven sandbox prewarming, which identifies pending tool execution demands mid-LLM token generation and fully overlaps sandbox bootstrapping with model inference. To extend prewarming windows across sequential agent steps, the framework leverages context-aware stochastic prefetching atop a sandbox dependency graph to probabilistically forecast future sandbox switches ahead of execution. We complement these speculative mechanisms with two orthogonal optimizations: a semantic result cache that prunes redundant repeated sandbox invocations, and a dedicated out-of-band shared-memory transport plane that bypasses conventional network serialization to deliver zero-copy artifact transfers. Evaluated on high-concurrency multi-turn agent traces, our prototype demonstrates that SpecBox cuts P99 end-to-end latency by up to $2.9\times$ relative to the on-demand sandbox baseline, while slashing peak memory consumption by $45.9\%$ compared to permanently reserved sandbox deployments.

</details>


### [7] [Consensus In Asynchrony: Strictly Formal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24095)
*Ivan Klianev*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过严格形式化框架，解决了确定性崩溃容错共识在完全异步环境中可行性（Ben-Or等人）与FLP不可能性结果（Attiya等人）之间的表面矛盾，证明仅一个协议阶段就能从不可能性跨越到可能性，并提出了能容忍多崩溃故障的新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异步环境下确定性崩溃容错共识可行性（Ben-Or, 1983）与FLP不可能性结果（Attiya, Castañeda, Rajsbaum重述）之间存在的理论矛盾，弥补这一基础性理论空白。

Method: 采用严格形式化框架，扩展了Attiya等人的推理逻辑，证明仅通过一个协议阶段即可从不可能性推导出相反的结论（可能性），并设计了一个能容忍多个崩溃故障的新算法。

Result: 澄清了理论矛盾，提出了一种能容忍多个崩溃故障的确定性共识算法，并给出了严格的正确性证明。

Conclusion: 异步环境下确定性崩溃容错共识的可行性是成立的，FLP不可能性结果与该可行性之间的表面矛盾已被解决，新算法验证了理论结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we resolve the apparent contradiction between the proven possibility of deterministic crash-tolerant consensus in a fully asynchronous environment and the reconfirmation of the FLP impossibility result by Attiya, Castañeda, and Rajsbaum. With the use of a strictly formal framework that extends their reasoning, we close this fundamental gap in theory. Specifically, we demonstrate that a single protocol phase separates their findings from reaching the exact opposite conclusion. Another important outcome is a novel algorithm with ability to tolerate multiple crash faults. We provide a rigorous, strictly formal proof of correctness to validate our results.

</details>


### [8] [Certified Parallel-in-Time Sinkhorn for Dynamic Entropic Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24741)
*Xinyang Wen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出TemporalSinkhorn，一种时间并行执行器，通过批处理未来候选解和修复，无需牺牲输出精度即可加速动态最优传输问题的求解。在多种GPU和数据集上获得1.15x-4.315x加速，且无容差违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态应用（如最优传输流匹配）需要反复求解相关的熵最优传输问题，但传统分布式Sinkhorn过程采用顺序帧处理和迭代后同步，效率低下。

Method: 提出TemporalSinkhorn，包含中心行分片证书（只接受确定性安全前缀）、打包Sinkhorn更新、在线投影遗忘率设置审计里程碑、后验残差检查恢复深度低估。预测可改变工作放置但不授权不准确输出。

Result: 4块A100 GPU上，n=2048时遗忘引导里程碑比审计每次打包迭代加速1.15x-1.47x；比软c变换热启动加速1.42x-3.55x；流匹配小批量流加速3.054x-3.632x；RTX 4060笔记本GPU固定核测试几何平均加速4.315x。

Conclusion: TemporalSinkhorn在多种设置下显著加速分布式Sinkhorn过程，且无精度损失。端到端流匹配集成、优化求解器比较和多节点验证仍是开放工作。

Abstract: Dynamic applications, including optimal-transport Flow Matching, repeatedly solve related entropic optimal transport problems, yet conventional distributed Sinkhorn processes frames sequentially and synchronizes after every iteration. We present TemporalSinkhorn, a parallel-in-time executor that batches future candidates and their repairs without making output accuracy speculative. A centered, row-sharded certificate accepts only a deterministic safe prefix. The remaining candidates share packed Sinkhorn updates; an online projective forgetting rate places audit milestones, while a posteriori residual checks recover from every depth underestimate. Prediction can therefore change work placement but cannot authorize an inaccurate output.
  On 4 A100 GPUs, a 60-run, five-seed grid at n = 2048 shows that forgetting-guided milestones reduce wall time by 1.15x-1.47x relative to auditing every packed iteration in five statistically resolved regime cells. Against a sequential soft c-transform warm start, temporal execution is 1.42x-3.55x faster across six synthetic streams, with zero marginal-tolerance violations. On Flow Matching minibatch streams, temporal execution is 3.054x-3.632x faster than sequential carry at n = 2048, with no tolerance violations. A separate fixed-kernel test on an RTX 4060 Laptop GPU gives a 4.315x geometric-mean speedup. These are complementary deployment studies rather than a controlled hardware comparison. End-to-end Flow Matching integration, optimized-solver comparisons, and multi-node validation remain open.

</details>

### [9] [FedTaste: Topology-Aware Structural Transfer for Multimodal Federated Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23245)
*Haochen Liang,Jie Zhang,Hideya Ochiai*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTaste框架，通过拓扑感知的结构迁移（而非特征对齐）解决多模态联邦学习中模态缺失和数据Non-IID问题，利用冻结的基础模型提取全局拓扑蓝图，并引入模态自适应结构提示与谱一致性正则化，实现轻量级分支适应，在避免显式模态补全的同时显著降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖生成式补全、外部辅助数据或孤立单模态训练，面临通信/计算开销大和隐私风险等问题。

Method: 1) 利用冻结基础模型从完整模态客户端提取联合多模态拓扑；2) 服务器整合为全局结构蓝图；3) 引入模态自适应结构提示与谱一致性正则化，实现轻量级分支特定适应。

Result: 在多个数据集和Non-IID设置下持续取得优越性能，同时大幅降低通信开销。

Conclusion: FedTaste通过稳定群体级语义关系替代脆弱的一阶特征对齐，有效避免模态补全，实现高效的多模态联邦学习。

Abstract: Multimodal Federated Learning is often challenged by arbitrary modality missingness and Non-IID data distributions, which lead to severe representation drift and hinder effective collaboration across clients. Existing methods typically rely on generative imputation, external auxiliary data, or isolated unimodal training to bridge modality gaps, often incurring substantial communication and computational costs as well as potential privacy risks. To address these limitations, we propose FedTaste, a parameter-efficient framework for topology-aware structural transfer in Multimodal Federated Learning with missing modalities. Instead of aligning fragile first-order features, FedTaste focuses on more stable group-level semantic relations. Specifically, FedTaste leverages frozen foundation models to extract a joint multimodal topology from full-modality clients, which is then consolidated by the server into a global structural blueprint. To adapt clients with missing modalities, we introduce Modality-Adaptive Structural Prompts together with spectral consistency regularization, enabling lightweight branch-specific adaptation that aligns local partial representations with the shared blueprint. In this way, FedTaste avoids explicit modality imputation while preserving shared semantic structure across clients. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedTaste consistently achieves superior performance across multiple datasets and challenging Non-IID settings, while substantially reducing communication overhead compared with existing methods.

</details>

### [10] [Language-Routed RAG and Direct Option Scoring for Multilingual Financial QA: DS@GT at FinMMEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22841)
*Justice Ayela,Kabir Sahni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DS@GT提交了在FinMMEval 2026任务1中的方案，构建了一个基于LangGraph的多语言检索增强流水线，通过BGE-M3嵌入、FAISS索引、语言路由模型选择和检索增强直接评分（RADS）方法，在多语言金融考试问答任务中取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融认证考试（如CFA、EFPA、CPA）需要结构化领域推理，但标准NLP基准无法捕捉这类能力，且跨语言环境中检索与表示基础设施不完善，现有模型在多语言金融推理任务上面临严峻挑战。

Method: 构建基于LangGraph的检索增强流水线：使用BGE-M3嵌入和FAISS索引从30,209条多语言知识库中检索语义相关样本；采用检索增强直接评分（RADS）读取候选选项的下一token对数概率而非自由生成；对低资源语言使用加权倒数排名融合（RRF）合并单语言与跨语言索引；模型选择按语言路由（Qwen3-14B、Qwen2.5-14B、Llama-3.1-8B分别对应不同语言）。

Result: 研究表明思维链提示会显著降低希腊语准确率（从90.7%降至20.9%），Qwen3的默认思考模式也会使阿拉伯语RADS性能跌至接近随机水平。有效的多语言金融推理需要语言感知的检索、模型路由和精心选择的评分策略。

Conclusion: 多语言金融推理不能简单套用统一策略，必须根据语言特性进行检索、模型和评分策略的精细化路由与适配，才能实现跨语言的高性能表现。

Abstract: We present DS@GT's submission to FinMMEval 2026 Task 1, a multilingual financial exam question answering benchmark spanning English, Spanish, Greek, Chinese, and Hindi. Financial certification exams such as the CFA, EFPA, and CPA demand structured domain reasoning that standard NLP benchmarks do not capture, and this challenge compounds across languages where retrieval and representation infrastructure is underdeveloped. We build a retrieval-augmented pipeline on LangGraph that detects query language and retrieves semantically relevant exemplars from a 30,209-entry multilingual knowledge base using BGE-M3 embeddings and FAISS indexing. The system then scores answers via Retrieval-Augmented Direct Scoring (RADS), reading next-token log-probabilities over candidate option letters rather than generating free-form output. For low-resource languages, we fuse per-language and cross-lingual retrieval indices using weighted Reciprocal Rank Fusion. Model selection is language-routed: Qwen3-14B for Arabic, Chinese, and Hindi; Qwen2.5-14B for English; and Llama-3.1-8B for Greek, a routing derived from empirical ablations that reveal substantial language-asymmetric performance gaps. Notably, chain-of-thought prompting significantly degrades Greek accuracy (90.7% to 20.9%), and enabling Qwen3's default thinking mode collapses Arabic RADS performance to near-chance levels. Our results indicate that effective multilingual financial reasoning requires language-aware retrieval, model routing, and deliberate scoring strategy selection.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Exact Match: How Evaluation Methodology Dominates Model Choice in LLM-Based Product Attribute Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22949)
*Ayush Dwivedi,Ashvi Soni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验发现，在电商属性抽取任务中，评估方法和数据质量对F1分数的影响远大于模型选择和提示策略，MAVE基准存在23.2%的标注噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中不同模型、数据集和提示策略的性能差异很大，缺乏对评估方法和数据质量影响的系统性研究。

Method: 在MAVE基准上，对比四种提示策略（零样本、少样本、架构引导、定义增强）在GPT-4o-mini和Gemini 2.5 Flash上的表现，使用精确和模糊字符串匹配评估6,400个属性预测，并进行标注噪声审计和F1方差分解。

Result: 评估方法的方差贡献是模型选择的约23倍、提示策略选择的约5倍；MAVE基准存在23.2%的标注噪声；协议间F1差异高度显著（p<0.0001），Cohen's kappa为0.769。

Conclusion: 在生产级属性抽取流程中，评估方法和数据质量对性能的影响远大于模型选择和提示工程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become a default choice for structured product attribute extraction in e-commerce pipelines, with practitioners reporting widely varying performance across models, datasets, and prompting strategies. This paper presents a controlled empirical study comparing four prompting strategies -- zero-shot, few-shot, schema-guided, and definition-augmented -- across two production-grade LLMs (GPT-4o-mini and Gemini 2.5 Flash) on the MAVE benchmark. We evaluate 6,400 attribute-level predictions using both exact and fuzzy string matching, and conduct a rigorous noise audit of the ground truth labels. We formally decompose F1 variance across four experimental factors and find that evaluation methodology produces variance approximately 23 times larger than model choice and 5 times larger than prompting strategy choice. We further establish that the MAVE benchmark exhibits a 23.2% ground truth noise rate against modern LLM outputs. Paired permutation tests (B=10,000) confirm that the inter-protocol F1 gap is highly significant (p<0.0001) and Cohen's kappa of 0.769 between protocols indicates substantial agreement. We conclude that for production attribute extraction pipelines, evaluation methodology and data quality dominate the impact of model selection and prompt engineering.

</details>


### [12] [When Does Few-Shot Prompting Help? A Systematic Empirical Study of Shot-Count Effects Across Model Scale, Architecture, and Output Parsing Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22969)
*Ayush Dwivedi,Ashvi Soni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了少样本提示中示例数量与LLM分类性能的关系，发现两者并非单调或普适，且无法仅从模型规模预测。实验涵盖5种LLM、6种shot配置，揭示了四种截然不同的行为模式，并识别并修正了Llama 3.3 70B的解析伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本提示是NLP中广泛使用的推理技术，但示例数量如何与模型规模、架构及输出格式合规性交互影响分类性能，缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在AG News四分类基准（n=200）上，对5种LLM（Gemini Flash Lite、GPT-4o-mini、Llama 3.1 8B、Llama 3.3 70B、Llama 4 Scout 17B）进行6种shot配置（k∈{0,1,2,3,5,8}）的对照实验，报告宏平均F1、95% bootstrap置信区间、置换检验p值和Cohen's d效应量。

Result: 发现四种行为模式：零样本表现良好且增益不显著的模型；零样本灾难性失败但单样本大幅恢复的模型（Llama 3.1 8B, d=10.98）；零样本最优但随示例增加退化的模型（Llama 4 Scout）；以及U型曲线模型（Llama 3.3 70B）。此外识别并修正了Llama 3.3 70B性能被低估206%的解析伪影。

Conclusion: shot数量与分类性能的关系并非单调、普适，且不可仅从模型规模预测；存在多种定性不同的行为模式，需谨慎设计少样本配置。

Abstract: Few-shot prompting, the practice of prepending a small number of input-output demonstration pairs to a query before presenting it to a large language model (LLM), is among the most widely adopted inference-time techniques in NLP. Yet little systematic work investigates how shot count interacts with model scale, architecture, and output format compliance in determining classification performance. This paper presents a controlled study of five LLMs across six shot-count configurations (k in {0,1,2,3,5,8}) on the AG News four-class benchmark (n=200). Our models span proprietary and open-source families: Gemini Flash Lite, GPT-4o-mini, Llama 3.1 8B, Llama 3.3 70B, and Llama 4 Scout 17B. We report macro-averaged F1 with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals (B=10,000), permutation-test p-values, and Cohen's d effect sizes across all 30 configurations. Our findings reveal four qualitatively distinct behavioral regimes: (1) models already well-calibrated at zero-shot that show modest, statistically insignificant gains (Gemini, GPT-4o-mini); (2) models that undergo catastrophic zero-shot failure but recover dramatically with a single example (Llama 3.1 8B, d=10.98, p<0.0001); (3) models optimal at zero-shot that degrade monotonically with additional examples (Llama 4 Scout); and (4) models exhibiting a U-shaped curve (Llama 3.3 70B: 0-shot F1=0.907, 2-shot F1=0.635, 5-shot F1=0.785 with parser corrected). We additionally identify, diagnose, and correct a systematic parsing artifact that artificially deflated Llama 3.3 70B performance by up to 206%, constituting a methodological contribution to LLM evaluation practice. Our results demonstrate that the relationship between shot count and classification performance is not monotonic, not universal, and not predictable from model scale alone.

</details>


### [13] [VecTree-RAG: An Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework Combining Vector and Tree Retrieval for Efficiency and Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23006)
*Xinyan Zhong,Yuwei Shi,Yuqi Wei,Chen Shen,Tianhang Zhou,Zhenghao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VecTree-RAG提出了一种将向量检索与推理引导的树结构遍历相结合的智能体框架，分别处理论文检索和证据定位两个任务，在多个科学问答基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成方法通常通过固定长度片段的相似性搜索同时处理相关论文识别和证据定位两个问题，忽略了文档结构，割裂了科学主张与其方法论和论证语境之间的联系。

Method: VecTree-RAG采用双机制互补的智能体框架：向量搜索负责在语料库中对压缩的文档和章节表示进行排序，而推理引导的源验证树遍历则在候选论文内部定位证据。

Result: 在QASPER（0.800）、LitQA2（0.925）和MOSAIC（0.547）三个基准上均取得最高分；证据页面精确度达0.274，远超基线方法的0.046-0.071。

Conclusion: 向量检索缩小了语料库搜索空间，树导航将阅读集中在结构相关的证据上，该方法提供了结构感知且可追溯的科学文献问答架构。

Abstract: Scientific question answering requires a retrieval system to solve two distinct problems: identifying which papers are relevant and locating the supporting evidence within those papers. Conventional retrieval-augmented generation typically addresses both through similarity search over fixed-length passages, flattening document structure and separating scientific claims from their methodological and argumentative context. We present VecTree-RAG, an agentic framework that assigns these tasks to complementary retrieval mechanisms. Vector search ranks compact document and section representations across the corpus, whereas reasoning-guided traversal of source-verified section trees localizes evidence within shortlisted papers. Full text is retained in a page store and exposed progressively only after structural localization. We evaluate VecTree-RAG on 300 QASPER questions, an open-access subset of 54 LitQA2 questions, and 49 multi-document MOSAIC questions. Compared with Dense RAG, reranked Dense RAG, RAPTOR, and Search-o1, VecTree-RAG obtained the highest observed answer score on all three benchmarks, reaching 0.800 LLM-judge correctness on QASPER, 0.925 accuracy on LitQA2, and a 0.547 composite score on MOSAIC. On QASPER, its evidence-page precision was 0.274, compared with 0.046--0.071 for the baselines. LitQA2 ablations further showed that the complete vector--tree architecture required fewer inference tokens than variants without tree navigation or corpus-level vector routing. These results indicate that vector retrieval narrows the corpus-level search space and tree navigation concentrates reading on structurally relevant evidence. Although multi-turn inference remains more expensive than single-call retrieval, VecTree-RAG provides a structure-aware and traceable architecture for scientific literature question answering.

</details>


### [14] [EGR: Embedding-Native Generative Retrieval with a Shared LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23038)
*Xiaodong Liu,Congfei Zhang,Hsiang-wei Chao,Siman Wang,Tong Zhao,Xiao Bai,Vincent Zhang,Jingxiao Ma,Zhe Liu,Wenfeng Zhuo,Zichu Li,Jitin Krishnan,Yunzhi Zhou,Yajun Wang,Jinchao Li,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EGR是一个嵌入原生生成式检索框架，通过单一共享LLM统一学习物品和用户表示，在推荐与广告系统中显著提升检索质量和转化率，简化系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索方法存在量化、标识符词汇表变动、令牌到物品对齐等实践复杂性问题，且嵌入管道将物品编码器与查询生成器分离训练，限制了用户-物品对齐。

Method: EGR使用单一共享LLM，从物品元数据学习物品表示、从交互历史学习用户表示，物品直接索引为密集向量，用户历史编码为密集检索查询，通过联合对比训练聚类相关物品并对齐查询与目标物品。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews基准上超越现有方法；在Snap DPA上随数据规模扩展、处理冷启动物品、受益于多模态输入；生产部署实现+2.91%转化率提升。

Conclusion: EGR在简化系统设计的同时提升了检索质量和广告性能，验证了嵌入原生生成式检索在推荐与广告领域的有效性。

Abstract: Generative retrieval is increasingly popular in large-scale recommendation and advertising systems, yet current methods introduce practical complications. Semantic-ID methods rely on quantization, mutable identifier vocabularies, and token-to-item grounding; embedding-based pipelines train the item encoder separately from the query generator, which limits user-item alignment. We propose EGR, an Embedding-Native Generative Retrieval framework for recommendation and advertising. EGR uses a single shared LLM to learn item representations from item metadata and user representations from interaction histories in one embedding space. Items are indexed directly as dense vectors, and user histories are encoded as dense retrieval queries. Joint contrastive training groups related items and aligns queries with their target items. We evaluate EGR on public benchmarks, industrial data, and live deployment. EGR outperforms published baselines on Amazon Reviews; on Snap DPA, it scales with data, handles cold-start items, and benefits from multimodal input. In production, EGR delivers a +2.91% conversion-rate lift, simplifying system design while improving retrieval quality and ad performance.

</details>


### [15] [SMART: LLM-Augmented Hybrid Retrieval for Dynamic Product Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23121)
*Congfei Zhang,Jingxiao Ma,Xiaodong Liu,Hsiang-wei Chao,Siman Wang,Ge Liu,Shantanu Aggarwal,Vincent Zhang,Meghana Missula,Rachel Liao,Zichu Li,Xiao Bai,Yunzhi Zhou,Yajun Wang,Zhe Liu,Jinchao Li,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SMART提出了一种轻量级质量门控机制，仅将约10%的用户路由至LLM路径进行语义探索，在保持重定向性能的同时，以90%的LLM成本削减实现+27.6%的广告转化率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态产品广告需要在海量商品目录中实现重定向与探索两个目标的平衡，传统嵌入模型语义理解不足，而大规模LLM部署存在高昂推理成本和词汇不匹配问题。

Method: 通过实验发现规则生成查询在BM25词汇索引上擅长重定向，LLM生成查询在稠密ANN索引上擅长探索；基于此提出SMART，采用轻量级质量门控识别初始关键词结果覆盖不足的用户，自适应地将约10%受益用户路由至LLM语义探索路径。

Result: 离线评估中，门控方法在维持重定向性能的同时，以90%的LLM成本削减捕获了大部分语义探索增益；在线A/B测试中，广告转化率较强嵌入基线提升27.6%。

Conclusion: 通过检索分解与自适应门控，SMART以极小成本实现了语义探索与重定向的最佳平衡，为大规模广告系统提供了一种高效可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic Product Ads (DPA) require retrieving relevant items from multi-million product catalogs, balancing two competing objectives: retargeting (re-surfacing known interests) and prospecting (discovering new categories). While Large Language Models (LLMs) capture semantic intent better than traditional embedding models, deploying them at scale introduces prohibitive inference costs and lexical mismatch issues. Through controlled experiments on millions of users, we demonstrate a critical retrieval decomposition: rule-generated queries excel at retargeting on a lexical BM25 index, while LLM-generated queries excel at prospecting on a dense ANN index. Building on this, we propose SMART (SeMantic-aware Adaptive ReTrieval). To manage costs, a lightweight quality gate identifies coverage gaps in initial keyword results, adaptively routing only the ~10% of users who benefit from semantic prospecting to the LLM path. Offline evaluation demonstrates that this gated approach captures the bulk of semantic prospecting gains in Relevance Score while maintaining competitive re-targeting performance at a 90% reduction in LLM costs. Finally, in a 2-week online A/B test at Snap, SMART improved the ad conversion rate by +27.6% over a strong embedding-based baseline.

</details>


### [16] [Statistically Supported LLM Ingredient and Recipe Data Collection in Computational Nutrition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23273)
*James Izzard,Hassan Eshkiki,Fabio Caraffini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM的质量控制流水线，用于从食谱中提取营养成分数据，通过统计估计、不变量检查和网络回退机制，将营养比值的绝对百分比误差中位数从31.9%降至10.1%，每个食材API成本约1美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有营养数据库不完整、不一致，且是为人工参考而非自动化推理设计的，LLM单次输出不可靠且可能引入无声错误，需要一种可控的数据获取流水线。

Method: 构建LLM驱动的质量控制流水线：将重复LLM查询视为模型诱导的答案分布，采用鲁棒点估计和归一化置信度分数处理多种数据类型；设计不变量守卫层检查营养和逻辑自洽性；通过线性规划调和轻微数值不一致，严重违规则通过网络证据修复，最后才交由人工审核。

Result: 在30个食材参考集上，营养标志精确匹配率达98.4%，营养比值绝对百分比误差中位数从基线31.9%降至10.1%，降低21.8个百分点，每个食材API成本约1美元。

Conclusion: 将LLM辅助数据库构建定位为受控的数据工程工作流，使不确定性可操作化而非丢弃，为计算营养学中精确食材数据的获取提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Computational nutrition needs precise ingredient data, but current databases are incomplete, inconsistent, and built for human reference rather than automated reasoning. LLMs could help fill these gaps, but single-pass outputs are unreliable and can introduce silent errors into downstream computation. We present a quality-controlled LLM pipeline for ingredient data acquisition that combines robust statistical estimation, domain-specific invariant checks, and a web-fetch fallback. An illustrative Heap's Law fit to 233 recipes suggests that unique-ingredient growth is sub-linear and front-loaded: the projected ratio of unique ingredients to recipes falls from 1.74 at 100 recipes to 0.19 at 5,000. For each ingredient attribute, repeated LLM queries are treated as samples from a model-induced answer distribution, and we apply robust point estimators and normalised confidence scores across numerical, Boolean, multiple-choice, open categorical, and optional integer types. An invariant guard layer enforces nutritional and logical self-consistency within each ingredient record. Minor numeric inconsistencies are reconciled via a linear program that minimises worst-case percentage deviation while preserving semantic zeros, and major violations are escalated to web-evidence-grounded repair, then human review only if that fails. On a curated 30-ingredient reference set, the pipeline achieves 98.4% exact match on nutrient flags and cuts median absolute percentage error on nutrient ratios from 31.9% for the median-aggregated baseline to 10.1%, a reduction of 21.8 percentage points, at an API cost of about $1 per ingredient. This frames LLM-assisted database construction as a controlled data-engineering workflow that makes uncertainty operational rather than discarding it.

</details>
