### [40] [Queryable Self-Organizing Maps: A Database Abstraction for Topology-Driven Data Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22843)
*Denis Mayr Lima Martins,Gottfried Vossen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出MapDB，将自组织映射(SOM)作为可查询的数据地图嵌入数据库管理系统(DBMS)中，支持用户在数据库内直接探索高维数据的拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SOM在DBMS之外训练和可视化，与关系数据脱节，无法在数据库内直接利用SOM的拓扑探索能力。

Method: 提出可查询数据地图抽象，包含代表点、邻域关系、对象分配和派生摘要；通过轻量级原型MapDB实现，使SOM制品在数据库中可查询。

Result: 实验表明：SOM训练在中等分析规模下可行；物化后地图查询可交互；SOM区域为探索性SQL提供有意义的查询目标。

Conclusion: MapDB成功将SOM拓扑分析能力融入数据库，实现了数据探索与关系查询的无缝集成。

Abstract: Self-Organizing Maps (SOMs) have long been used as exploratory tools for high-dimensional data: they organize objects into a two-dimensional topology that reveals clusters, gradients, sparse regions, dense regions, and boundaries. Yet, in modern data systems, SOMs are typically trained and visualized outside the DBMS, disconnected from the relational data they summarize. We introduce the abstraction of a queryable data map: a learned topological artifact consisting of representatives, neighborhood relations, object assignments, and derived summaries. We instantiate this idea with MapDB, a lightweight prototype that makes SOM artifacts queryable so users can explore data topology without leaving the database. Experimental study shows that SOM training is feasible at moderate analytical scale, that map queries are interactive after materialization, and that SOM regions provide meaningful targets for exploratory SQL.

</details>


### [41] [iFVS: Towards Instance-Optimized Filtered Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22922)
*Abdullah Al-Mamun,Yeasir Rayhan,Walid G. Aref*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出iFVS，一种针对过滤向量搜索的实例优化量化方法，通过查询特定码本生成来提升精度与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过滤向量搜索中，传统量化方法（如PQ）在距离计算时存在精度损失，且过滤谓词和选择率变化会导致相关向量空间改变，进一步加剧召回率下降问题。

Method: iFVS采用查询特定码本生成方法，根据查询向量和过滤谓词动态调整码本，实现实例优化，替代传统的固定码本方案。

Result: 在多个过滤选择率区间内，iFVS相比固定码本量化基线方法，显著改善了QPS-召回率的权衡表现。

Conclusion: iFVS通过实例优化的查询特定码本策略，在保持紧凑向量存储的同时提升了压缩向量的排序精度，为过滤向量搜索中的量化问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Filtered vector search (FVS) is increasingly important in modern AI + DB systems, where vector similarity search is combined with relational predicates. Quantization plays a vital role in these systems by enabling query processing over large vector datasets. However, lossy approaches, e.g., Product Quantization (PQ), incur a precision penalty during distance calculation, thereby negatively impacting the query recall performance. This problem becomes more challenging in FVS because the relevant vector space can change with the relational predicate and selectivity. Motivated by the success of instance-optimized database system components, we introduce iFVS, an Instance-Optimized Filtered Vector Search technique. Given a fixed, quantized vector dataset, and a representative workload of filtered vector queries, iFVS adopts a query-specific codebook generation approach for FVS that is instance-optimized towards a certain dataset and query workload. Instead of using a fixed codebook for all queries, iFVS conditions distance estimation on both the query vector and the filter predicate. This enables more accurate ranking over compressed vectors while preserving compact per-vector storage. Experiments show that iFVS improves the Queries Per Second (QPS)-recall tradeoff across several filter selectivity bins compared with fixed-codebook quantized FVS baselines.

</details>


### [42] [Common-Neighbor-Count-Based Representative Possible World Finding on Uncertain Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23085)
*Chengjie Gu,Xiaoliang Xu,Yuxiang Wang,Kai Yao,Mengzhao Wang,Tianxing Wu,Yingjie Xia,Xiangyu Ke*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出面向不确定图的对等节点结构特征——共同邻居计数（CRPW）问题，即找到一个确定性图（可能世界），使其节点间共同邻居数量尽可能接近期望值，并证明了该问题的NP难解性。设计了基于两阶段迭代优化与自适应终止的高效算法，实验表明在共同邻居相关任务上性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代表可能世界（RPW）研究仅关注节点级特征（如度、三角度数），而许多数据挖掘任务（如链接预测）关键依赖节点间的成对特征（如共同邻居数），存在方法空白。

Method: 提出两阶段基础算法：第一阶段快速初始化一个可能世界；第二阶段迭代优化。优化阶段将浮点评估替换为整数计数策略加速，并设计了基于Beta分布的自适应终止方法自动控制迭代轮次。

Result: 在真实不确定图上的大量实验表明，该方法在各类挖掘任务上表现优异，尤其在共同邻居相关任务中效果最佳。

Conclusion: CRPW将RPW从节点级特征扩展到成对结构关系，所提算法在保持高质量的同时显著提升了计算效率，为不确定图上的确定性算法直接应用提供了有效代理。

Abstract: A representative possible world (RPW) is a deterministic graph derived from an uncertain graph $\mathcal{G}$ where a designated structural feature closely approximates its expected value in $\mathcal{G}$. Serving as a proxy for $\mathcal{G}$, the RPW allows conventional deterministic algorithms to be directly executed on it for mining tasks targeting this feature, thereby avoiding computationally expensive enumeration or sampling on $\mathcal{G}$. Existing studies on RPWs primarily focus on individual node features, e.g., degree or triangle degree. However, many mining tasks, such as link prediction, critically rely on the number of common neighbors between two nodes, which is a pairwise feature. To bridge this gap, we study the \underline{C}ommon-neighbor-count-based \underline{R}epresentative \underline{P}ossible \underline{W}orld (CRPW) problem, extending RPWs from preserving node-level statistics to preserving pairwise structural relationships. The problem seeks the possible world that best preserves the expected numbers of common neighbors between node pair, and we prove that is NP-hard. To address it, we develop a two-stage basic algorithm that quickly initializes a possible world and then refines it iteratively. We next accelerate the refinement by replacing its costly floating-point evaluation with an efficient integer counting strategy, as the refinement only requires determining whether a change is beneficial, rather than computing its exact magnitude. Moreover, we design a Beta-based adaptive termination method to automatically stop the refinement once the desired quality of the possible world is reached, preventing over- or under-execution. Extensive experiments on real-world uncertain graphs demonstrate the effectiveness of our algorithms on diverse mining tasks. Especially on common-neighbor-related tasks, we achieve the best performance among all compared methods.

</details>


### [43] [ABISS: Evaluating Text-to-SQL Systems Through Agent Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23340)
*Giovanni Sullutrone,Luca Sala,Sania Aftar,Georgia Koutrika,Sonia Bergamaschi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对现实场景中Text-to-SQL系统处理模糊与不可回答问题的三大缺陷（分类体系不完整、基准生成不真实、交互静态化），提出了统一分类法、多智能体生成管道和动态仿真环境ABISS。实验发现两个关键瓶颈：子类别分类困难和澄清条件后的SQL生成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL基准测试在精心筛选的数据集上表现良好，但真实用户常提出模糊或不可回答的问题，现有系统处理能力差。三个互相关联的缺陷阻碍了进展：不完整的分类体系、现实场景基准生成不足、以及静态的用户交互方式。

Method: (1) 提出包含8个类别的统一分类法，覆盖模糊和不可回答的问题；(2) 构建多智能体生成管道，采用两阶段流程（NLQ生成+SQL接地）和显式的类别一致性验证阶段，由本地开源模型委员会验证；(3) 提出ABISS动态仿真环境，让Text-to-SQL代理与风格感知的模拟用户进行多轮对话交互。

Result: 在ABISS-BIRD和ABISS-Spider上对8个开源模型的实验发现两个根本瓶颈：模型能检测到问题有问题但难以精确定位子类别；即使收到有用用户信息后，模型在最终解决步骤中仍然经常失败。提供真实的类别标签能大幅提升执行和反馈指标，但即使在Oracle类别标签下，模糊问题的执行率仍然偏低。

Conclusion: 当前Text-to-SQL系统在处理模糊和不可回答问题上仍存在显著不足，特别是在子类别分类和澄清后的SQL生成方面。研究提供了新的基准和生成管道，为未来改进方向提供了基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate high performance on curated Text-to-SQL benchmarks; nevertheless, real-world users frequently pose ambiguous or unanswerable questions that current systems handle poorly. Three interconnected gaps hinder progress: incomplete taxonomies, realistic benchmark generation for real-world settings, and static user interaction. We address all of the above issues through three contributions: (1) a unified taxonomy of 8 categories covering ambiguous and unanswerable questions; (2) a multi-agent generation pipeline with a two-stage process (NLQ generation followed by SQL grounding) and an explicit Category Conformance validation stage, producing questions from arbitrary databases validated by a council of local open-source models; and (3) ABISS (Ambiguity Benchmark using Interaction-Simulated Sessions), a dynamic simulation environment where Text-to-SQL agents interact with style-aware simulated users across multi-turn dialogues. Experiments with eight open-source models on ABISS-BIRD and ABISS-Spider reveal two fundamental bottlenecks. The first is subcategory classification: models detect that a question is problematic yet struggle to pinpoint the specific subcategory. The second is clarification-conditioned SQL generation: even after receiving useful user information, models often still fail in the final resolution step. Providing the ground truth category yields large gains in both execution and feedback across both datasets, yet ambiguous-question execution remains low even under oracle category labels. We release our code for data generation and benchmark on GitHub (https://github.com/giosullutrone/ABISS-Evaluating-Text-to-SQL-Systems-Through-Agent-Interaction).

</details>


### [44] [The Atoms of the Score: Record-Level versus In-Engine Composite Evaluation of Clinical Quality Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23619)
*Angelo Kastroulis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍Mercury引擎，通过将CQL评估作为数据库问题处理（使用患者优先的FHIR二进制编码、基于CQL检索的索引、查询计划器），采用外部聚合架构保留每条中间记录，在100,000患者数据集上比Blaze引擎更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CQL引擎在决策支持和质量测量两条轴上存在架构差异，内部聚合会丢失中间结果，而外部聚合架构既能计算复合分数又能保留每条个体判定结果用于检查、归因和行动。

Method: Mercury采用外部聚合架构，将CQL评估视为数据库问题：使用患者优先的紧凑二进制编码存储FHIR，基于CQL检索条件派生索引，使用查询计划器选择访问路径，以CQL作为查询语言。

Result: 在100,000患者（112.3M资源）的Synthea语料库上，Mercury 2.0.1在相同AWS硬件上使用Blaze自有基准测试套件进行评估。

Conclusion: Mercury作为第二种架构（外部聚合）的专用CQL数据库引擎，通过将CQL评估视为数据库问题而非内存解释，在性能上可与最快的CQL评估器Blaze竞争。

Abstract: Clinical Quality Language (CQL) engines serve two axes: decision support (evaluate one patient, now) and quality measurement (score a population). The composite score is rarely the end product, because the individual determinations behind it are what let a score be inspected, attributed, and acted on. Two architectures follow from where aggregation happens: one computes the composite inside the engine over the whole store (no data movement); the other evaluates individual records and lets the composite be totaled externally, keeping every intermediate available.
  Mercury is a purpose-built CQL database engine of the second kind. It treats CQL evaluation as a database problem -- FHIR stored in a compact binary encoding keyed patient-first, indexes derived from what CQL retrieves filter on, a planner selecting access paths, CQL as the query language -- rather than as in-memory interpretation over a generic FHIR store. We evaluate Mercury 2.0.1 against Blaze 1.10.1, among the fastest CQL evaluators, using Blaze's own published benchmark suite on identical AWS hardware over a 100,000-patient Synthea corpus (112.3M resources).

</details>


### [45] [Order in Desbordante: Techniques for Efficient Implementation of Order Dependency Discovery Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23632)
*Yakov Kuzin,Dmitriy Shcheka,Michael Polyntsov,Kirill Stupakov,Mikhail Firsov,George Chernishev*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本研究针对数据画像中的顺序依赖（OD）发现问题，通过C++重实现FASTOD和ORDER算法，并结合多种优化技术，实现了最高10倍的性能提升和2.9倍的内存降低，推动了OD向工业应用的转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OD发现方法仅从算法角度出发，忽略了实现层面的优化，导致计算开销巨大，难以满足工业应用需求。本文旨在通过工程优化手段提升OD发现的效率和实用性。

Method: 首先用C++重实现FASTOD和ORDER两种算法以提升速度和降低内存；然后分析算法瓶颈并提出多种优化技术；最后在开源高性能数据画像工具Desbordante中进行评估。

Result: 重实现版本性能提升最高3倍，应用优化技术后最高达10倍；内存消耗降低最高2.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过工程层面的重实现和针对性优化，可显著提升OD发现算法的性能，使其更接近工业级应用标准。

Abstract: Science-intensive data profiling focuses on discovery and validation of various patterns in datasets. This study considers discovery of one such pattern - order dependency (OD). Simply put, OD states that some list of columns is ordered according to another one. It is of use for database query optimization, data cleaning and deduplication, anomaly detection, and much more.
  Existing discovery methods have approached this problem solely from the algorithmic standpoint, without focusing on the implementation side. At the same time, this problem is very computationally intensive, and therefore this part should not be ignored, as it brings ODs closer to industrial use.
  In this paper, we study two algorithms for OD discovery which target different OD axiomatizations - FASTOD and ORDER. We start by reimplementing these algorithms in C++ in order to speed them up and lower their memory consumption. We then analyze their bottlenecks and propose several techniques which improve their performance even further.
  To perform evaluation, we have implemented these algorithms inside Desbordante - a science-intensive, high-performance, and open-source data profiling tool developed in C++. Experiments have demonstrated a performance improvement of up to 3x obtained by reimplemented versions, and, with the application of our techniques, up to 10x. Memory consumption has been lowered by up to 2.9x.

</details>


### [46] [Extending Desbordante with Probabilistic Functional Dependency Discovery Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23636)
*Ilia Barutkin,Maxim Fofanov,Sergey Belokonny,Vladislav Makeev,George Chernishev*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了概率函数依赖（pFD）与近似函数依赖（AFD）的区别，并在开源数据剖析工具Desbordante中实现了pFD发现算法，对比了两者在运行时间、内存消耗及输出结果上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统函数依赖（FD）对脏数据不鲁棒，而pFD相比AFD研究较少，本文旨在填补这一空白，深入理解pFD的特性及其与AFD的关系。

Method: 首先从理论上分析pFD与AFD的区别，然后实现pFD发现算法，通过实验评估其运行时间和内存消耗，并与AFD发现算法进行对比，最后研究两者的输出结果是否可互相转换。

Result: 分析了pFD相对于AFD的优势场景；实现了pFD发现算法；对比了两类算法在时间和内存上的表现；发现AFD与pFD的输出并不能直接互换使用。

Conclusion: pFD与AFD虽然有相似之处，但在理论和实践上存在显著差异，不能简单用AFD算法替代pFD发现，需要专门针对pFD进行研究与实现。

Abstract: Data profiling aims to extract complex patterns from data for further analysis and use that data in domains such as data cleaning, data deduplication, anomaly detection, and many more.
  Functional dependencies (FDs) are one of the most well-known patterns. However, they are poorly suited for these tasks, as real data is usually dirty, and the rigid definition of FDs does not allow algorithms to locate them. For this reason, there are several formulations aimed at relaxing FDs to support dirty data, with approximate functional dependency (AFD) being the most popular one. Another formulation is the Probabilistic Functional Dependency (pFD), which we aim to support inside Desbordante - a science-intensive, high-performance and open-source data profiling tool implemented in C++. However, pFDs are relatively poorly studied, compared to AFDs.
  In this paper we study pFDs, both analytically and empirically. We start by assessing how different pFDs and AFDs are by studying cases in which pFDs have an edge over AFDs. Then, we implement the algorithm for pFD discovery, as well as study its run time and memory consumption. We also compare it with an AFD discovery algorithm. Lastly, we study the output of both algorithms to learn whether or not it is possible to use AFD discovery algorithm to get pFDs and vice versa.

</details>


### [47] [Kalypso: Relational LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23815)
*Hojae Son,Md Ashraful Islam,Huy Gia Cao,Hui Guan,Marco Serafini*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出关系型LLM服务抽象Kalypso，通过流水线执行和KV-cache重用，实现语义查询计划感知的LLM服务，在多样化工作负载下获得高达4.57倍的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义查询处理系统采用请求中心的LLM服务，对查询计划无感知，导致大量性能优化机会未被利用。

Method: 提出关系型LLM服务抽象，支持语义查询计划的API，并设计自适应内存感知调度算法，通过流水线执行实现KV-cache状态复用，同时平衡上游并行度、下游进度和GPU利用率。

Result: 与基线相比，查询完成时间提升高达4.57倍。

Conclusion: 查询感知的LLM服务能显著提升语义查询执行效率。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as semantic operators for filtering, extracting, ranking, joining, and transforming unstructured data. Existing semantic query processing systems invoke request-centric LLM serving systems that are unaware of the query plan, leaving substantial performance opportunities unused. This paper introduces relational LLM serving, an abstraction that makes LLM serving aware of semantic query structure while preserving query semantics and output accuracy. The key opportunity is pipelined execution across semantic operators: when intermediate tuples flow directly from one operator to the next, their KV-cache state can be reused instead of recomputed.
  We present Kalypso, a relational LLM serving system that exposes an API for semantic query plans and executes them using an adaptive, memory-aware scheduling algorithm. Kalypso addresses a new online scheduling problem in which pipelined operator execution is coupled with GPU memory pressure management to reuse KV-cache state in the serving engine before eviction. Its scheduler continuously adjusts memory allocations to balance upstream parallelism, downstream progress, and GPU utilization. Our evaluation shows that Kalypso improves query completion time over baselines using request-centric LLM serving, with speedups up to 4.57x across diverse workloads, demonstrating that query-aware LLM serving can substantially improve the efficiency of semantic query execution.

</details>


### [48] [Answering Conjunctive Queries with Aggregations under Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23881)
*Qichen Wang,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了在正交换半环上对合取查询进行动态维护的复杂度，揭示了插入更新下可维护类的边界从free-connex退缩到新定义的strong-connex类，并给出了完整的二分法刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分析中常见的聚合操作（如计数、求和、最短路径等）可统一为在正交换半环上评估合取查询，但聚合是否改变了可高效维护的查询类尚不清楚，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 理论方面：基于OuMv/OMv猜想和k-clique猜想证明下界；算法方面：扩展CROWN框架支持带注释的关系，实现上界匹配。

Result: 任意更新下，聚合不改变复杂度，维护难度不低于布尔半环；插入更新下，边界从free-connex退缩到strong-connex；提出了查询高度和维度的参量化下界族。

Conclusion: 给出了查询和半环参数化的二分法，恢复了布尔情形的经典结果，并精确刻画了聚合带来的额外难度。

Abstract: Dynamic query processing keeps query answers up to date during insertions and deletions. For conjunctive queries (CQs) under set semantics, the maintainable classes are known exactly: the $q$-hierarchical CQs under arbitrary updates, widening to the free-connex CQs under insertion-only updates. But modern analytics aggregates, including bag counting, SUM/COUNT, provenance, access control, and shortest paths---all captured by evaluating a CQ over a positive commutative semiring. We ask whether aggregation changes what can be maintained efficiently, and if so, when.
  Under arbitrary updates, it does not: maintenance is at least as hard as over the Boolean semiring. Under insertion-only updates, it does: the boundary retreats from free-connex to a new class we call strong-connex, with $q$-hierarchical $\subsetneq$ strong-connex $\subsetneq$ free-connex $\subsetneq$ acyclic. For every ordered semiring carrying a suitable monotone sequence (e.g., sum-product and tropical), no free-connex but non-strong-connex CQ is maintainable in $O(|D|^{1/2-ε})$ time under the OuMv and OMv conjectures. We further strengthen this lower bound into a family parameterized by the height and dimension of the query, under the combinatorial $k$-clique and generalized OuMv conjectures; these quantify how far the annotated hardness grows as the queries scale.
  On the algorithmic side, a single framework matches these boundaries by adapting CROWN to annotated relations. It maintains every strong-connex CQ in $O(1)$ amortized time under insertion-only updates, regardless of the underlying semiring. Moreover, under arbitrary updates, it maintains every $q$-hierarchical CQ in $O(1)$ amortized time if the semiring has $O(1)$-deletable aggregations. Together, the upper and lower bounds give query- and semiring-parameterized dichotomies that recover the Boolean picture and pinpoint the hardness aggregation adds.

</details>


### [49] [EviDC: A Violation-Guided Algorithm for Incremental Denial Constraint Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24021)
*Qian Zhou,Xixian Han,Xiaolong Wan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种基于违反引导的增量DC发现算法EviDC，利用前缀树结构DCTrie对证据进行剪枝，减少中间证据并提升运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量DC发现方法在证据构建时未利用已有DC的结构信息，导致产生大量中间证据，效率低下。

Method: 将已有DC组织成前缀树DCTrie，每条根到叶子的路径表示潜在违反路径；增量处理时仅沿可达违反路径扩展证据，尽早剪枝无关分支。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上，EviDC减少了中间证据量，在多数场景下提升了运行效率，且在插入比例和数据集增大时性能提升更明显。

Conclusion: 违反引导的证据构建方法具有有效性和可扩展性，适合动态数据集下的DC增量发现。

Abstract: Denial Constraints (DCs) are an important class of integrity constraints and have been widely used in data quality management. In dynamic datasets, newly inserted tuples may invalidate existing DCs and require the constraint set to be updated. Existing incremental DC discovery methods still generate a large amount of intermediate evidence because they do not exploit the structural information of existing DCs during evidence construction. We propose EviDC, a violation-guided incremental DC discovery algorithm. EviDC organizes existing DCs into a prefix tree structure called DCTrie, in which each path from the root to a leaf represents a potential violation path. During incremental processing, evidence is expanded only along reachable violation paths, while irrelevant branches are pruned as early as possible. We evaluate EviDC on real-world and synthetic datasets. The results show that EviDC reduces intermediate evidence and improves runtime efficiency in most scenarios. The performance gain becomes more pronounced as the insertion ratio and dataset size increase, showing the effectiveness and scalability of violation-guided evidence construction.

</details>


### [50] [TEmBed-T: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Table-Level Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24130)
*Ayeen Poostforoushan,Liane Vogel,Carsten Binnig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了表格级嵌入方法在不同下游任务中的表现，发现没有单一模型在所有任务中表现最佳，表明嵌入质量不能仅通过检索任务来衡量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格级嵌入在表格检索、数据湖发现和表格分类等任务中至关重要，但当前对不同嵌入方法在不同任务中的行为理解有限，缺乏系统的评估框架。

Method: 通过扩展TEmBed测试平台，引入多项互补属性评估表格级嵌入，并在多个下游任务上进行实证研究。

Result: 实验表明，TEmBed模型池中的单一模型无法在所有任务中取得最优表现，表格级嵌入质量不能仅由检索任务决定。

Conclusion: 表格级嵌入需要综合多任务评估，不能简化为单一检索任务指标。

Abstract: Tabular data is the dominant structured-data modality, and learning table representations has become a core research direction. Table-level embeddings in particular underpin a wide range of applications, including table retrieval, data lake discovery, and table classification. Despite their importance, there is still limited understanding of how different embedding approaches behave across tasks, making systematic evaluation and analysis essential. In this work, we introduce a systematic evaluation of table-level embeddings that captures several complementary properties required for downstream effectiveness. We realize this evaluation by extending TEmBed, a recently proposed testbed for tabular embeddings, whose table-level coverage is currently limited to a single retrieval task. An empirical study over the TEmBed model pool confirms that no single model excels across all tasks, demonstrating that table-level embedding quality cannot be reduced to retrieval alone.

</details>


### [51] [Domain-Specific Data Quality Analysis Using Technology-Independent Query Templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24151)
*Arno Kesper,Lukas Sebastian Hofmann,Markus Matoni,Gabriele Taentzer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出QPM框架，一种模型驱动方法，用于定义独立于特定数据库技术和应用领域的数据质量分析模板，使领域专家无需技术背景即可独立管理数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域专家缺乏查询语言专业知识，无法独立实现数据质量分析，导致需要技术专家介入，工作流程资源密集且效率低下。

Method: 提出Quality Pattern Model (QPM)框架，采用模型驱动方法定义数据质量分析模板，支持XML、RDF和Neo4j三种数据库技术，并通过定性用户研究和质量目录收集进行评估。

Result: QPM的表达能力与常见数据库查询语言相当甚至更优；领域专家能够独立使用该工具定义基于模板的质量分析，无需IT专家支持。

Conclusion: QPM框架有效消除了领域专家进行数据质量分析的技术障碍，提高了数据质量管理的效率和独立性。

Abstract: In an increasingly data-driven world, effectively working with data depends heavily on its quality. Quality analysis is a central aspect of data quality management. As data quality is typically domain- and context-specific, the definition of quality requirements is primarily the responsibility of domain experts. However, domain experts often lack the query language expertise needed to implement quality analyses. Therefore, the process of defining quality analyses results in a resource-intensive workflow that requires the involvement of technical experts, effectively excluding domain experts from independently managing data quality. To address this challenge, we present the Quality Pattern Model framework (QPM), a model-driven approach to define templates for data quality analyses that are independent of specific database technologies and application domains. QPM can eliminate the need for deep technical expertise and prevent the need for defining quality analyses several times for different database technologies. We present a proof-of-concept implementation of this approach for three database technologies: XML, RDF, and Neo4j. We evaluate the expressiveness of our approach, its applicability in the cultural heritage domain, and its usability by domain experts. For this purpose, we conducted a qualitative user study and empirically collected quality problems in a catalog. Our findings suggest that QPM matches and even exceeds the expressiveness of common database query languages. Furthermore, the results indicate that our tool enables domain experts to define template-based quality analyses independently, without requiring support of IT experts.

</details>


### [52] [Simple and Fast Algorithm for Graph-based Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (Full Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24173)
*Reon Uemura,Keito Kido,Daichi Amagata*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的过滤式近似最近邻搜索算法，能够高效处理任意查询向量和属性值组合的检索问题，实验证明了其效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术在处理过滤式近似最近邻搜索时面临搜索速度慢、难以应对任意属性组合的挑战，需要一种更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种新的算法，能够同时处理查询向量和任意属性值的组合过滤条件，具体方法细节未在摘要中详述。

Result: 通过大量实验证明，该算法在过滤式近似最近邻搜索任务上具有高效性。

Conclusion: 新算法有效解决了过滤式近似最近邻搜索中查询向量与任意属性组合的兼容性问题，性能优于现有方法。

Abstract: It has been common to represent many objects as high-dimensional vectors due to the proliferation of machine learning-based embedding techniques. One of the most important functions for analyzing high-dimensional vectors is approximate nearest neighbor search, which, given a query vector, retrieves the vector that is approximately the most similar to the query vector. In many real-world applications, such as e-commerce, objects have not only vectors but also attributes, e.g., category, color, and brand, and they require a scenario where users can specify a query vector and a value for each attribute of interest. This problem, called filtered approximate nearest neighbor search, retrieves approximate nearest neighbors from a set of objects that have the specified attribute values. Efficiently solving this problem is challenging because it has to accept arbitrary query vectors and attribute values, which are not known in advance. Existing techniques suffer from slow search performance and difficulty in dealing with arbitrary combinations of attributes. This work overcomes these challenges and proposes a new algorithm for this problem. We conduct extensive experiments, and the results demonstrate the efficiency of our algorithm.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Scale and Generation: Understanding Language Model-based Entity Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24688)
*Zeyu Zhang,Xue Li,Iacer Calixto,Paul Groth,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验（3种匹配架构×3种模型变体×3种模型规模×9个数据集，共1,215次微调运行）系统分析了实体匹配中架构、模型变体和规模的影响，发现：嵌入导向的模型变体对双编码器至关重要；交叉编码器因联合编码记录对而优于双编码器；生成式匹配器仅在分布偏移下表现更优；大模型更依赖捷径学习，不一定更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究混淆了匹配架构、模型骨干、模型变体（预训练目标不同）和模型规模等因素，难以分离性能提升的真正来源。本文旨在通过控制实验厘清这些因素的影响。

Method: 采用析因控制实验方法，系统比较3种匹配架构（双编码器、交叉编码器、生成式匹配器）、3种模型变体（来自Qwen3系列，反映不同预训练目标）和3种模型规模，在9个数据集上进行共1,215次微调运行，同时评估跨数据集迁移性和计算成本。

Result: 1）模型变体对双编码器至关重要，嵌入导向变体提供更强的初始化和更有利的表征几何结构；2）交叉编码器因联合编码记录对而始终优于双编码器，但大模型可缩小差距；3）生成式匹配器并非普遍优于交叉编码器，其优势集中在分布偏移场景（如记录模式差异、跨数据集迁移）；4）更大模型更依赖捷径学习，不一定带来更好性能。

Conclusion: 研究厘清了不同匹配架构性能差异的根源，为未来研究提出应更好解耦架构选择与模型层面因素，并明确评估分布偏移和跨数据集迁移性。

Abstract: Entity matching identifies records that refer to the same real-world entity. Language models can be adapted to this task through bi-encoder, cross-encoder, and generative matcher architectures. However, prior studies often conflate matcher architecture with differences in model backbone, model variant(reflecting different pretraining objectives), and model size, making it difficult to isolate the sources of performance gains. We address this issue through a controlled factorial study spanning three matcher architectures, three model variants and three model sizes from the Qwen3 family, and nine datasets, totaling 1,215 fine-tuning runs. We also evaluate cross-dataset transferability and computational cost.
  Our results show that model variant is critical for bi-encoders: embedding-oriented variants provide stronger initialization and more favorable representation geometry predictive of downstream matching performance. Cross-encoders retain a consistent advantage over bi-encoders because they jointly encode record pairs rather than representing each record independently, although larger models partially narrow this gap. Generative matchers do not universally outperform cross-encoders. Instead, their advantages concentrate under distribution shift, including subtle unseen differences in record schemas and cross-dataset transfer. We further find that larger models rely more heavily on shortcut learning and therefore do not necessarily perform better. These findings clarify the factors underlying performance differences across matcher architectures and motivate future research and benchmark designs that better disentangle architectural choices from model-level factors while explicitly evaluating distribution shift and cross-dataset transferability. We release our experimental results, code, training scripts, and evaluation data at https://github.com/Jantory/llm-trained-matcher.

</details>

### [54] [Optimizing Transformer Neural Network for Real-Time Outlier Detection on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22786)
*Ilia Sobakinskikh,Paul Alexander Bilokon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了如何优化Transformer神经网络在金融时间序列实时异常检测中的推理时间，并利用FPGA加速其计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列数据常含有错误或异常值，且数据量快速增长，现有数据清洗方法在精度和速度上存在不足，需要更高效的方案。

Method: 研究不同Transformer架构用于时间序列建模，并在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA板上高效实现以降低推理延迟。

Result: 成功在FPGA上实现了Transformer的高效部署，显著降低了延迟，适合实时异常检测场景。

Conclusion: 结合Transformer架构与FPGA硬件加速，可以有效提升金融时间序列实时异常检测的效率和准确性。

Abstract: In this work, we explore how the inference time of a Transformer Neural Network can be efficiently optimized with applications to real-time anomaly detection in financial time series. The financial time series are price series such as asset prices. Unfortunately, the data is often with errors or outliers that make the downstream data processing tasks useless, unstable or even harmful. Moreover, the amount of financial time-series data has been significantly increasing. Hence, there is a need for better data-cleaning methods in terms of accuracy and in terms of processing speed.
  Transformers as a neural network architecture have achieved superior performances in many tasks such as Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision. Time series modelling and especially anomaly detection tasks can benefit from the features of transformers architecture in multiple ways, including the capacity to capture long-range dependencies and interactions.
  Increasingly powerful hardware, such as field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), have seen increasing usage in recent years due to their reconfigurability and high performance. They can be efficiently utilized to speed up the computations of the Transformer architecture.
  We explore different Transformer architectures for time series modelling and how they can be efficiently implemented on an FPGA board (PYNQ-Z2). In particular, we examine the application of Transformers to detect anomalies in time series and we show how they can be efficiently implemented on an FPGA board to minimize latency.
  The code is available at https://github.com/thxi/icl_thesis

</details>


### [55] [SLA-Constrained Carbon-Aware Routing in Geo-Distributed Serverless Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22806)
*Anmol Chaudhary,Rahul Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种碳感知的无服务器路由策略，在满足SLA约束的前提下，通过优化跨地理区域的请求路由，实现高达46.8%的碳减排，且零SLA违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云路由机制仅考虑延迟，忽略了不同区域电网的碳排放强度差异，导致不必要的碳排放。

Method: 将碳感知无服务器路由问题建模为受约束的优化问题，提出SLA约束下的碳感知路由策略，在SLA可行区域内实现最优碳减排。

Result: 在5个AWS区域实测中，碳减排最高达46.8%，平均27.4%；零SLA违规；路由开销低于总延迟的0.02%；扩展到12个区域后平均碳减排达47.5%。

Conclusion: 云系统可在不牺牲用户体验的前提下实现显著碳减排，有助于实现SDG 13（气候行动）和SDG 7（可负担的清洁能源）。

Abstract: Modern cloud deployments distribute applications across multiple geographic regions, yet standard routing mechanisms prioritize latency while ignoring the fluctuating carbon intensity of local power grids. Latency-driven routing incurs avoidable carbon emissions, particularly when cleaner regions are within acceptable latency bounds. The proposed model formulates the carbon-aware serverless routing problem as a constrained optimization over geo-distributed cloud regions and introduces an SLA-constrained carbon-aware routing policy that achieves optimal carbon reduction within the SLA-feasible region, evaluated using real carbon intensity measurements across 5 primary AWS deployments. Experimental results show that the proposed policy achieves up to 46.8% carbon reduction while maintaining zero SLA violations across all evaluated thresholds. The system reduces carbon by an average of 27.4% under mixed workloads, and the routing overhead is very low (less than 0.02% of total request latency). A scalability study across 12 AWS regions spanning 6 continents demonstrates that average carbon savings increase from 27.4% to 47.5% as routing flexibility expands under mixed workloads. The proposed work contributes to SDG 13 (Climate Action) and SDG 7 (Affordable and Clean Energy) by enabling low-carbon routing decisions. These results indicate that cloud systems can achieve significant carbon savings without compromising user experience.

</details>


### [56] [Impute On-Demand: Adaptive Correlated Time Series Imputation for Changing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23503)
*Zhichen Lai,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang,Dong Gong,Lina Yao,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaCTSi，一种自适应时序插补方法，通过解耦时空特征、稀疏空间注意力和相关性加权传感器选择，在变化物联网环境下实现更优的缺失值插补，平均MAE降低33.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTS插补方法准确但缺乏对环境变化的适应性：易受传感器故障影响、不能选择性插补、架构静态不随资源变化。

Method: 结合One-shot TCN与学习型时间-传感器索引表解耦时空特征；稀疏空间注意力提取动态空间相关性；相关性加权传感器选择提供充分空间上下文。

Result: 在12种基线方法、3种适应性场景、5个基准数据集上，AdaCTSi平均MAE相对降低33.1%，支持传感器子集与资源自适应推理，可部署于MCU等设备。

Conclusion: AdaCTSi在保持高精度的同时实现了对环境变化的强适应性，内存占用小，适合在资源受限的IoT设备上部署。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) applications generate vast amounts of Correlated Time Series (CTS) data that often contain missing values and require imputation. Existing methods emphasize accuracy but often lack adaptability to changing IoT environments: they are vulnerable to sensor failures, cannot selectively impute only incomplete sensors, and use static architectures that do not adapt to resource availability. To address these limitations, we propose AdaCTSi, an adaptive CTS imputer for changing environments. AdaCTSi combines a One-shot Temporal Convolutional Network with a Learned Time-Sensor Index Table to extract and decouple complex spatio-temporal features into sensor-wise embeddings, enabling adaptation to varying sensor subsets. Sparse Spatial Attention efficiently extracts dynamic spatial correlations, while Correlation-Weighted Sensor Selection selects informative sensors to provide sufficient spatial context. Experiments with twelve baseline methods, three adaptability scenarios, and five benchmark datasets covering traffic, air quality, and trajectory data show that AdaCTSi reduces MAE by an average of 33.1% relative to the strongest baseline on each dataset. A single trained model supports sensor-subset and resource-adaptive inference, and its modest memory footprint enables deployment on commodity computing devices, including MCUs.

</details>


### [57] [Semalith v1.4: A Calibrated 184M Safety Classifier Achieving State-of-the-Art Prompt-Injection Detection at 44x Fewer Parameters than Llama-Guard-3-8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22545)
*Tejasvi C. Addagada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Semalith v1.4 是一个184M参数的DeBERTa-v3-base分类器，可在单次前向传播中同时处理提示注入、一般危害和金融合规三轴安全分类，在22项基准测试中多数优于Llama-Guard-3-8B（参数仅为其1/44），并提供了不同版本的部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源安全护栏无法在单次推理中同时处理金融和智能体场景下的提示注入、监管合规及一般危害检测需求。

Method: 基于DeBERTa-v3-base构建184M参数分类器，设计22类分类头（含9种提示注入子类、一般危害、11种BFSI标签）和4类辅助超类头，采用联合加权损失训练；使用49个公开来源的76,204行语料，并通过SHA-1去重确保零污染。

Result: 在22项基准测试中，Semalith v1.4以1/44参数在全部7项提示注入评估和18项整体基准中的11项超越Llama-Guard-3-8B；良性智能体提示FPR为0.000（对比0.063）；一般危害基准上Llama-Guard领先，形成互补。

Conclusion: v1.4推荐用于BFSI标签覆盖或对良性智能体提示零误报的场景；v1.3推荐用于对话审核部署；模型存在6个已知弱点。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in financial-services and agentic settings requires safety classifiers that simultaneously handle prompt injection, regulatory compliance, and general harm, a combination no existing open guardrail addresses in a single inference pass.
  Semalith v1.4 is a 184M-parameter DeBERTa-v3-base classifier performing simultaneous three-axis safety classification including prompt injection, general harm, and financial-services regulatory compliance, in a single forward pass. Its 22-class head (BENIGN, nine prompt-injection sub-types, general-harm, eleven BFSI labels) is trained with a 4-class auxiliary super-category head under jointly weighted loss, on a 76,204-row corpus mined from 49 public sources with SHA-1 deduplication against every held-out evaluation set, with 21 of 22 benchmarks at zero contamination (max 0.22%).
  Against Llama-Guard-3-8B on 22 held-out benchmarks, Semalith v1.4 wins every prompt-injection evaluation (7/7) and 11 of 18 benchmarks overall at 44x fewer parameters, with FPR = 0.000 on 208 benign agentic prompts vs 0.063 for Llama-Guard-3-8B. On general-harm benchmarks (WildGuardMix, HEx-PHI, HarmBench), Llama-Guard-3 leads; this complementary split is documented in Section 4. Six measured weak spots are disclosed in Section 6.
  Deployment guidance: v1.3 is recommended for conversational moderation deployments (ToxicChat F1 0.624); v1.4 is recommended when BFSI label coverage or zero-FPR on benign agentic prompts is the priority.

</details>


### [58] [CORVUS: Context Optimization and Reduction Via Underlying Synchronization for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22711)
*Mingwei Zheng,David OBrien,Siwei Cui,Pardis Pashakhanloo,Rajdeep Mukherjee,Myeongsoo Kim,Sachit Kuhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CORVUS是一种新型轨迹架构，通过解耦文件读取操作与观察结果，维护文件同步注册表，在每次推理周期注入最新文件内容，从而解决传统追加式轨迹架构中文件快照过时和冗余的问题，在多个基准测试中实现9-50%的输入token减少和高达37%的推理周期缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM编码智能体的追加式轨迹架构中，文件读取操作与观察结果紧密耦合，导致文件快照过时、推理错误以及重复读取文件造成轨迹臃肿。

Method: 提出CORVUS架构，通过维护文件同步注册表，将文件读取操作与观察结果解耦，在每个推理周期仅注入文件的当前内容。

Result: 在SWE-POLYBENCH_VERIFIED和SWE-BENCH PRO上，平均输入token减少9-50%，最终提示缩短15-32%，推理周期最多减少37%，同时保持相当的通过率。

Conclusion: CORVUS通过结构性的轨迹架构改进，有效解决了传统方法中文件快照过时和轨迹冗余的问题，实现了更轻量、更高效的LLM编码智能体。

Abstract: LLM coding agents operate by constructing trajectories that accumulate reasoning, tool calls, and results to enable multi-step decision-making. However, the conventional append-only trajectory architecture found in practice tightly couples file-read actions with their observations, capturing snapshots that become permanently fixed in the chronological history. As files change through agent edits or concurrent human modifications, these snapshots become stale, causing reasoning errors and causing agents to redundantly re-read files, with each re-read appending yet another copy to the trajectory. To mitigate this, we propose CORVUS, a novel trajectory architecture that decouples file-read actions from their observations by maintaining a synchronized registry of relevant files and injecting only their current contents at each reasoning cycle. This structural change produces significantly lighter-weight trajectories that remain synchronized with the actual codebase state by construction, eliminating redundant file copies and stale snapshots that bloat conventional trajectories. We evaluated CORVUS on SWE- POLYBENCH_VERIFIED and SWE-BENCH PRO across four LLMs, achieving 9-50% reduction in average input tokens per task, 15-32% shorter final prompts, and up to 37% fewer reasoning cycles while maintaining comparable pass rates.

</details>


### [59] [CausalGate: Causal Importance Distillation for Transformer Module Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22720)
*Kiran Nair,Smriti Regmi,Rodrigue Rizk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalGate提出了一种基于因果干预的框架，通过量化每个子层对语义输出的因果贡献来指导计算高效推理，无需运行时路由开销即可实现显著的硬件延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应推理方法依赖隐藏状态相似性或激活幅度等观察性启发式指标来丢弃冗余模块，但这些基于相关性的度量无法捕捉对语义准确性至关重要的微妙且非线性的结构计算。

Method: 在校准阶段，CausalGate隔离每个Attention和MLP子层，将其输出置零，并通过最终logit分布的KL散度精确测量语义损伤。随后，利用指数移动平均平滑目标和可微分的成对排序损失，将结构重要性层级蒸馏为一组全局静态的轻量级标量门控，消除运行时路由开销。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B、Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上的语言建模和常识推理基准测试中，CausalGate持续优于主流的动态路由和跳层基线方法，将理论计算节省转化为具体的硬件延迟降低，且零运行时开销。

Conclusion: CausalGate通过因果干预而非相关性指标来识别冗余模块，同时将重要性信息蒸馏为静态门控，在保证语义精度的前提下实现了高效推理，证明了因果推断在LLM计算优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Existing adaptive inference methods for Large Language Models rely on observational heuristics, such as hidden-state similarity or activation magnitudes, to drop redundant modules. However, these correlation-based metrics often fail to capture subtle, non-linear structural computations vital for semantic accuracy. We introduce CausalGate, an intervention-guided framework for compute-efficient transformer inference. During a calibration phase, CausalGate isolates individual Attention and MLP sub-layers, zeros out their respective outputs, and measures the exact semantic damage via the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the final logit distribution. To eliminate runtime routing overhead, this structural importance hierarchy is distilled into a global set of static, lightweight scalar gates using an Exponential Moving Average smoothing objective paired with a differentiable pairwise ranking loss. Evaluated on TinyLlama-1.1B, Qwen2.5-3B, and Llama-3.1-8B across language modeling and commonsense reasoning benchmarks, CausalGate consistently outperforms prominent dynamic routing and layer-skipping baselines, translating theoretical compute savings into concrete hardware latency reductions with zero operational overhead.

</details>


### [60] [Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22724)
*Kaibing Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Shengtian Yang,Shuo He,Yu Li,Mengyi Liu,Pengwei Chen,Jun Xu,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProGPO方法，通过首次访问观测覆盖度解决组策略优化中的信用分配陷阱问题，在困难长程任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组策略优化在长程困难任务中面临采样不平衡和信用分配陷阱：重复或低效动作主导高概率区域，导致大量全失败回放组，无法提供有效梯度信号。

Method: ProGPO在组内所有样本均获得零奖励时，利用首次访问观测覆盖度为探索更多新状态的轨迹或步骤分配更高的相对优势。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，使用Qwen2.5-1.5/7B-Instruct模型，ProGPO一致优于组策略优化基线，尤其在困难任务上提升显著。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于状态探索的代理奖励信号，ProGPO有效打破了失败主导采样循环，改善了长程任务中的策略学习。

Abstract: Group-based policy optimization has been increasingly used to train large language model (LLM) agents from sparse outcome rewards by comparing trajectories or steps within a group. However, on difficult long-horizon tasks, this comparison can suffer from a sampling imbalance: repeated or low-effect actions dominate the high-probability region of the policy while useful state-changing actions remain under-sampled. This imbalance produces many all-failed rollout groups, where outcome rewards provide no direction for correcting the policy. Together, these effects can form a self-reinforcing credit trap: failure-dominated sampling yields no outcome-based correction, allowing repeated low-effect actions to persist. To break this loop, we propose Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization (ProGPO), which uses first-visit observation coverage only when all samples in a group receive zero outcome reward. Specifically, within such groups, ProGPO assigns higher relative advantages to trajectories or steps that visit more new states since reaching new observations is a prerequisite for task success. Experiments on two challenging agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5/7B-Instruct, show that ProGPO consistently improves over group-based baselines, with particularly large gains on hard tasks.

</details>


### [61] [QFedPolyp: A Communication- and Inference-Efficient Federated Learning Framework for Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22743)
*Madan Baduwal,Priyanka Paudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QFedPolyp是一个结合量化感知训练与低精度模型通信的联邦学习框架，用于隐私保护的协作式息肉分割，实现了约4倍通信压缩和1.5倍推理加速，同时保持高分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式深度学习需要共享敏感医疗数据存在隐私风险，而联邦学习虽保护隐私但全精度模型参数的重复传输带来高通信成本，亟需一种通信高效且推理高效的联邦学习方案用于协作息肉分割。

Method: 提出QFedPolyp框架，将量化感知训练与低精度模型通信相结合。各医院在本地使用轻量级U-Net训练私有数据并模拟量化过程，客户端传输量化后的模型参数至中央服务器，通过联邦平均算法进行重建和聚合。

Result: 全精度联邦训练在Kvasir-SEG和CVC-ClinicVideoDB上分别达到0.910和0.930的Dice分数；8位均匀量化通信降低约4倍传输成本且保持竞争性分割精度；量化模型推理速度比全精度模型快达1.5倍。

Conclusion: QFedPolyp实现了隐私保护的协作式息肉分割，降低了通信开销并加速了推理，生成的轻量级模型适用于临床实时部署。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Automatic polyp segmentation supports computer-aided diagnosis and early colorectal cancer detec- tion. Centralized deep learning requires hospitals to share sensitive medical data, while federated learning preserves privacy but introduces high communication costs through repeated transmission of full-precision model parameters. We propose QFedPolyp, a communication- and inference-efficient federated learning framework for collaborative polyp segmentation.
  Methods: QFedPolyp combines quantization-aware training with low-precision model communication. Each hospital locally trains a lightweight U-Net on private data while simulating quantization during training. Clients transmit quantized model parameters to a central server, where they are reconstructed and aggregated using Federated Averaging. Evaluation is performed on Kvasir-SEG, CVC-ClinicVideoDB, PolypGen, and BKAI-IGH NeoPolyp.
  Results: Full-precision federated training achieves Dice scores of 0.910 on Kvasir-SEG and 0.930 on CVC-ClinicVideoDB. Uni- form 8-bit communication reduces transmission cost by approximately 4 times while preserving competitive segmentation accuracy. Quantized models also achieve up to 1.5 times faster inference than full-precision models.
  Conclusions: QFedPolyp enables privacy-preserving collaborative polyp segmentation with reduced communication overhead and faster inference. The resulting lightweight models are suitable for real-time clinical deployment.

</details>


### [62] [Learning to Access Computation: Accessibility Plasticity as a Principle of Adaptive Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22748)
*Zhaowen Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可访问性可塑性"（Accessibility Plasticity）原则，认为神经网络不仅可以通过修改计算能力来适应，还可以通过重新组织已有计算模块之间的交互关系来适应，并在顺序学习任务中验证了该方法能在减少能力修改的同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络主要通过修改参数来适应，虽然有了模块化、条件计算等方法，但未将"计算能力"和"计算可访问性"作为两个独立的自适应变量来区分对待。

Method: 提出可访问性可塑性原则，通过关系性操作实现，建立"复用优先"的适应层级（可访问性修改优先于更昂贵的能力和结构变化），并在顺序学习任务上进行概念验证。

Result: 可访问性适应能在减少能力修改的同时保持与基线相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: 可访问性是神经网络中一个独立的适应维度，为未来动态神经系统中计算关系随环境演变的研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Modern neural networks primarily adapt through parameter modification within predefined computational structures. While recent methods introduce modularity, conditional computation, and parameter-efficient adaptation, they generally do not distinguish computational capability from computational accessibility as separate adaptive variables. This work introduces Accessibility Plasticity, a principle of adaptive computation in which systems adapt not only by changing what computation exists, but also by reorganizing which existing computations can interact and participate. We formalize Accessibility Plasticity through a relationship-based operational realization and establish a reuse-first hierarchy of adaptation, where accessibility modification precedes more costly capability and structural changes. A proof-of-concept evaluation on sequential learning tasks shows that accessibility adaptation can reduce capability modification while maintaining comparable task performance. These results suggest accessibility as a distinct adaptive dimension and provide a foundation for future dynamic neural systems whose computational relationships evolve with changing environments.

</details>


### [63] [Hierarchical Grading in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22757)
*T. Shaska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分级大语言模型（GLLMs），通过代数框架为Transformer表示空间赋予分级结构，在保持计算复杂度和推理成本不变的前提下提升性能。最优分级可通过凸优化预训练前确定，训练后分级可被吸收为标准Transformer，实现架构兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer缺乏对表示空间的结构化分级，导致在层次化目标（如多层级数据）上存在优化效率不足的问题。作者希望通过几何不变量理论引入分级机制，在不增加计算开销的前提下提升模型对分层数据的适应能力。

Method: 构建GLLM框架：在Transformer表示空间中引入分级结构，通过分级环面（grading torus）上的Kempf-Ness泛函定义优化目标；利用Hilbert-Mumford准则确定分级方向的最优性；通过两个动量映射（moment maps）的相遇点给出闭式最优解；理论证明分级先验带来的极小最大分离（minimax separation）优势。

Result: 理论上证明了：1）最优分级构成开凸锥，其成员由Hilbert-Mumford类型准则判定；2）对于分层目标，分级先验与无分级先验之间存在指数级（以层级数为指数）的极小最大风险分离；3）训练后分级可完全吸收为参数，GLLM编译为同架构的标准Transformer。

Conclusion: GLLM在理论上证明了分级机制的有效性和计算可行性，且与现有Transformer完全兼容。未来工作可探索更复杂的分级结构及其在实际语言建模任务中的表现。

Abstract: We introduce Graded Large Language Models (GLLMs), an algebraic framework that equips the representation space of a transformer with a grading and propagates the induced weighted scalar action through embeddings, self-attention, and the training objective. The construction extends the theory of graded neural networks and graded transformers to autoregressive language models while preserving expressive power, asymptotic computational complexity, and inference cost.
  The governing geometric picture is that of geometric invariant theory. The benefit of a grading is expressed by a Kempf--Ness functional on the grading torus; the grades that improve upon the uniform architecture form an open convex cone whose membership is decided by a Hilbert--Mumford-type criterion pairing a grade direction against two measurable profiles of the target and the data; the optimal grades are the coincidence point of two moment maps, given in closed form; and the ordinary transformer appears as a semistable isotropic point on the boundary of the cone: one member of a larger graded family rather than a distinguished optimum.
  Separately, for level-stratified targets we prove a minimax separation between the graded prior and its absence: over all estimators the risks of the graded and uniform target classes separate throughout an explicit window of sample sizes, by a factor that decays exponentially in the number of levels under geometric stratification. Both profiles are estimable offline, so the optimal grades solve a convex program certified before training begins. Because the grading is absorbed into the learned parameters after training, every GLLM compiles to a standard transformer of identical architecture and inference complexity.

</details>


### [64] [Evaluating Fuzz Testing for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24577)
*Zhibin Kang,Hanmo You,Dong Wang,Haiming Zheng,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对强化学习（RL）模糊测试方法进行了全面的实证研究，从有效性、多样性、效率、实用性四个维度系统评估了五种最先进方法，揭示了不同方法的优劣，并提出了将互补策略结合等实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL模糊测试方法在评估设置、基线和指标上存在差异，导致难以可靠判断它们的相对效果和实际可用性。

Method: 在统一配置下，对五种最先进方法及随机测试在三个环境（MountainCar、BipedalWalker、CARLA）中从有效性、多样性、效率、实用性四个维度进行系统评估，并进一步评估检测到的崩溃对智能体鲁棒性提升和安全监控的实用价值。

Result: MDPFuzz等在崩溃发现上效果和效率突出；SeqDivFuzz在发现多样化崩溃行为上表现优异；模糊测试生成的崩溃可有效提升智能体鲁棒性，并实现跨方法泛化的准确安全监控。

Conclusion: 建议结合互补的模糊测试策略，并采用多层面多样性分析，以实现更全面和实用的RL测试。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains such as robotics, autonomous driving, and drone control, where unexpected behaviors may lead to severe real-world consequences. Fuzz testing has recently emerged as a promising method for exploring the vast state spaces of RL agents and exposing crashes. Although numerous RL fuzzing methods have been proposed, existing studies often differ in evaluation settings, baselines, and metrics, making it difficult to draw reliable conclusions about their relative effectiveness and practical usefulness. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive empirical study that systematically evaluates RL fuzzing methods from four complementary perspectives: effectiveness, diversity, efficiency, and practical utility. We benchmark five state-of-the-art methods alongside random testing under unified configurations across three environments of increasing complexity (MountainCar, BipedalWalker, and CARLA), and further assess the downstream usefulness of detected crashes for agent robustness improvement and safety monitoring. Our results reveal several key insights. For instance,throughput-oriented methods like MDPFuzz demonstrate superior effectiveness and efficiency in crash discovery, while methods explicitly designed to encourage exploration like SeqDivFuzz excel at uncovering diverse crash behaviors. We also show that fuzzing-generated crashes can meaningfully improve agent robustness and enable accurate safety monitoring with strong cross-method generalization. Beyond these empirical findings, we distill actionable guidance for both researchers and practitioners, highlighting the benefits of combining complementary fuzzing strategies and adopting multi-level diversity analysis to achieve more comprehensive and practical RL testing.

</details>


### [65] [An Integrated Deep Learning and Statistical Framework for Whole-Network Gene--Environment Association with Leaf Vascular Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22763)
*Geran Zhao,Yangsheng Wang,Xiaotian Dai,Guifang Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成深度学习与统计学的框架，用于从RGB图像中提取完整叶脉网络结构，并通过半参数稀疏典型相关分析识别与叶脉结构相关的基因-环境交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因-环境关联研究仅用少量低维概要性状量化叶脉结构，丢弃了原始图像中的大部分结构信息，需要更全面的方法。

Method: 1) 将完整叶脉结构表示为全网络图像表型；2) 微调EDTER模型提取叶脉结构；3) 构建新注释数据库；4) 应用SSCCA进行高维变量选择与关联建模。

Result: 两项模拟研究验证了框架性能；真实杨树数据集识别出三个与叶脉结构相关的显著基因-地理交互作用。

Conclusion: 建立了适用于高维复杂图像表型的方法学框架，为叶脉结构基因-环境关联研究提供了新见解。

Abstract: Leaf veins exhibit remarkable diversity in architecture and patterning, yet existing gene--environment association studies have primarily quantified leaf venation using a small collection of low-dimensional summary traits, thereby discarding most of the structural information contained in the original images. We propose an integrated deep learning and statistical framework. The proposed framework achieves four methodological advances. First, it represents the complete leaf vascular architecture as a whole-network image phenotype. Second, it fine-tunes the deep learning-based Edge Detection with Transformers (EDTER) model to accurately extract whole-network leaf vascular architecture from RGB images by jointly learning local and global contextual features. Third, it constructs a new annotated leaf image database by integrating edge maps generated by DiffusionEdge with the Berkeley Segmentation Database (BSDS500). Fourth, it applies Semiparametric Sparse Canonical Correlation Analysis (SSCCA) to perform variable selection and model associations between repeatedly measured high-dimensional Bivariate image responses and high-dimensional predictors while simultaneously accommodating sparse, zero-inflated data represented by edge maps through a truncated latent Gaussian copula model. Two simulation studies demonstrate the performance of the proposed framework under increasing levels of complexity. Application to a real \emph{Populus} dataset identifies three significant gene--geography interactions associated with leaf vascular architecture, providing new biological insights and establishing a broadly applicable methodological framework for high-dimensional complex image phenotypes.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Shapley: An Influence-Based Data Auditing Pipeline for LLM Alignment and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22766)
*Yunting Song,Matthew Watson,Peter Grabowski,Jun Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的、仅需推理的数据价值评估流程，通过近似Shapley值来检测LLM对齐数据中的标签错误和结构冲突，在HelpSteer2和HH-RLHF数据集中分别减少了99.1%的人工审计空间并发现了数千个隐藏的安全与事实偏好反转问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对齐性能越来越受限于数据质量，大规模偏好和指令微调数据集中存在隐藏的结构矛盾、安全风险和系统性人工标注错误，而现有审计方法（如语义去重或用LLM作为评判）难以捕捉单个样本的实际预测影响。

Method: 提出一种基于Shapley值近似的可扩展推理式数据估值流程：将语义k-NN邻域映射为有向图，通过参考LLM在零样本/单样本条件下的对数似然变化来评估数据效用，并将预测影响力分数转化为局部优势度量以隔离梯度冲突样本。

Result: 1) 在HelpSteer2数据集上将人工审计搜索空间减少99.1%，成功发现多种失效模式下的错误标注样本；2) 在HH-RLHF数据集中发现数千隐藏的安全与事实偏好反转问题；3) 揭示评估基准存在严重漏洞：模型预测正确但被错误的人类标签惩罚。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个数学上严格且高效的诊断工具，用于发现人类标注错误、清理评估基准并确保LLM对齐数据的完整性。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly bottlenecked by data quality. As datasets scale, massive preference and instruction-tuning corpora inevitably accumulate hidden structural contradictions, safety risks, and systemic human annotation errors. Standard dataset auditing methods, such as semantic deduplication or LLM-as-a-judge, struggle to capture the actual predictive impact of individual records and often miss deep functional rule clashes. To address this, we introduce a scalable, inference-only data valuation pipeline that approximates the Shapley value without iterative model retraining. By mapping semantic k-NN neighborhoods into a directed graph, our framework evaluates data utility directly through a reference LLM's probability distribution using zero-shot and one-shot conditional log-likelihood shifts. Our pipeline then translates these predictive influence scores into localized advantage metrics to isolate gradient-conflicting records. We demonstrate the pipeline's efficacy in sanitizing two heavily vetted alignment datasets. First, applying our pipeline to the HelpSteer2 dataset reduced the manual audit search space by 99.1%, successfully uncovering falsely-labeled records across diverse failure modes. Second, applying our automated audit strategy to Anthropic's HH-RLHF training and evaluation splits identified thousands of hidden safety and factual preference inversions. Crucially, by extending this audit to the evaluation split, we expose severe vulnerabilities in current benchmark integrity: highly capable models frequently predict the safer or more helpful response, only to be penalized by objectively flawed human ground-truth labels. Overall, our work provides a mathematically grounded, highly efficient diagnostic tool to uncover human label failures, sanitize evaluation benchmarks, and ensure the integrity of LLM alignment data.

</details>


### [67] [DomainPilot: Domain-Level Loss-Guided Two-Stage Data Mixture Optimization for Efficient Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22769)
*He Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DomainPilot 提出了一种基于领域级损失引导的两阶段数据混合优化框架，通过在训练框架中注入领域感知损失计算（仅需约30行适配代码），在不增加数据量或训练成本的情况下，在 Qwen3-1.7B 的 SFT 阶段取得了 MMLU-Redux +2%、AIME24 +1.8%、LiveCodeBench v5 +3.8%、BFCL v3 +3.6% 的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态数据调度方法在工业级大规模预训练和SFT中存在关键局限：数据选择在TB级语料上产生高昂的O(N)成本；混合优化方案引入严重I/O瓶颈或需要训练辅助参考模型；样本级重加权策略依赖的损失信号混淆了噪声、难度和新颖性。

Method: 提出DomainPilot框架：1) 引入token级领域损失监控，在不中断数据管道的情况下捕获每领域学习动态；2) Scaling Law引导的粗优化阶段，拟合领域特定收敛曲线，推导混合调整的先验；3) Mixing Law引导的细优化阶段，通过受控扫描实验建模跨领域交互效应，优化混合比例；4) 通过基于补丁的架构在现有训练框架中注入领域感知损失计算。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B的SFT实验中，优化后的混合相比原始混合在MMLU-Redux(+2%)、AIME24(+1.8%)、LiveCodeBench v5(+3.8%)、BFCL v3(+3.6%)上均获得提升，且未增加总数据量或训练成本。

Conclusion: 领域级训练信号能够为混合优化提供一种有效、轻量级的替代方案，避免了昂贵的数据选择或辅助模型训练。

Abstract: The training efficacy of large language models (LLMs) is fundamentally constrained by the quality and composition of training data. Existing dynamic data scheduling methods face critical limitations in industrial-scale pretraining and supervised fine-tuning (SFT): data selection incurs prohibitive O(N) costs on terabyte-scale corpora, mixture optimization schemes introduce severe I/O bottlenecks or require training auxiliary reference models, and sample-level reweighting strategies rely on loss signals that conflate noise, difficulty, and novelty.
  We present DomainPilot, a domain-level loss-guided two-stage data mixture optimization framework. DomainPilot introduces token-level domain loss monitoring to capture per-domain learning dynamics during training without halting the data pipeline. Building on these signals, we propose a Scaling Law guided coarse optimization stage that fits domain-specific convergence curves and derives a principled prior for mixture adjustment. A subsequent Mixing Law guided fine optimization stage refines the mixture by modeling cross-domain interaction effects through controlled sweep experiments. The entire mechanism is realized via a patch-based architecture that injects domain-aware loss computation into existing training frameworks (e.g., MindSpeed/Megatron-LM) with only ~30 lines of framework-specific adapter code.
  We validate DomainPilot on the Qwen3-1.7B model during SFT. Compared to the original data mixture, our optimized mixture achieves improvements of +2% on MMLU-Redux, +1.8% on AIME24, +3.8% on LiveCodeBench v5, and +3.6% on BFCL v3, without increasing total data volume or training cost. These results demonstrate that domain-level training signals provide an effective, lightweight alternative to expensive data selection or auxiliary model training for mixture optimization.

</details>


### [68] [Dementia Etiology Diagnosis via Collaborative Meta Knowledge Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22770)
*Siyuan Du,Mengxi Chen,Xinyang Jiang,Zilong Wang,Jiangchao Yao,Dongsheng Li,Ya Zhang,Lili Qiu,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种协作元知识增强（COME）框架，通过注入多中心采集语义、源标识符和模态指标作为异质性感知嵌入到统一Transformer架构中，并设计信任区域约束优化方案，在7个独立队列中实现痴呆症病因诊断的SOTA性能（平均macro-AUC 85.62%），同时保持跨中心和跨序列的优异泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在痴呆症病因诊断中面临疾病症状重叠的挑战，传统方法通过跨中心扩大数据集会引入数据异质性冲突，而现有多任务学习范式未能利用关键元信息（如中心特定采集方式和模态可用性）来应对异质性。

Method: 提出COME框架：(1) 将多中心采集语义、源标识符和模态指标作为异质性感知嵌入注入统一Transformer架构；(2) 设计信任区域约束优化方案，通过参考模型在训练过程中正则化模型以避免虚假相关性。

Result: 在7个独立队列上取得SOTA域内性能（平均macro-AUC 85.62%），比最强基线提升4.29分；在跨中心和跨序列评估中保持优异的域外泛化能力；模型预测与已建立的生物标志物（淀粉样蛋白、tau）和临床严重程度一致。

Conclusion: COME框架通过显式建模异质性实现了鲁棒且可解释的痴呆症诊断，在真实世界场景中具有较大应用潜力。

Abstract: Although artificial intelligence (AI) has shown promising performance in several medical tasks, accurate dementia etiology diagnosis with AI remains challenging due to complex overlapping symptoms among diseases. Scaling up the dataset size by combining the cross-center samples may bring a gain in the pursuit of performance, while the inherent data heterogeneity across centers or populations induces the conflict. Conventional multi-task learning paradigms offer a promising framework; however, they fail to consider critical meta information (e.g., site-specific acquisition and modality availability) to combat the heterogeneity. To address this challenge, we propose a Collaborative Meta Knowledge Enhancement (COME) framework for dementia etiology diagnosis, which injects multi-center acquisition semantics, source identifiers, and modality indicators as heterogeneity-aware embeddings into a unified Transformer architecture for scale-up training, enabling explicit modeling of heterogeneity. Besides, a trust-region constrained optimization scheme is designed to regularize the model from spurious correlations during training through a reference model. Across seven independent cohorts, our method achieves state-of-the-art in-domain performance with a mean macro-averaged AUC of 85.62% and a 4.29-point gain over the strongest baseline, while maintaining superior out-of-domain generalization under both cross-center and cross-sequence evaluations. Extensive validation also confirms the alignment between model predictions and established biomarkers (amyloid, tau) and clinical severity, highlighting the potential of COME to enable robust and interpretable dementia diagnostics in real-world settings.

</details>


### [69] [CC-AOS: Cost- and Horizon-Conditioned Amortized Backward Induction for Finite-Horizon Optimal Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22774)
*Tianwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CC-AOS，一种成本与时间范围条件化的摊销最优停止求解器，能够通过单一模型处理连续成本和多个时间范围的有限时域最优停止问题，在FordA基准上以单一检查点在六组未见过的成本-时间范围组合上平均降低了15.75%的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动后向归纳方法需要为每个成本-时间范围操作点单独求解，改变操作条件时需重复优化并维护多组模型，导致连续成本自适应和多时间范围部署效率低下。

Method: 提出CC-AOS方法，学习一个共享的继续价值模型，该模型以当前状态、绝对时间、剩余时间范围和获取成本为条件，通过联合摊销拟合后向归纳进行训练；并在模型架构中编码了价值函数关于成本的非递减、凹性和时间范围相关的Lipschitz连续性等特性。

Result: 在FordA基准上，单个CC-AOS检查点在六组未见过的成本-时间范围组合上均优于独立拟合的凸函数学习方法，平均降低15.75%的损失，同时与调优的静态阈值方法表现相当。

Conclusion: CC-AOS通过单一模型实现了对连续成本和多个时间范围的高效最优停止，避免了重复优化，并具备理论上的误差保证。

Abstract: Finite-horizon optimal stopping is a central problem in early time-series classification, where a system must decide at each sequence prefix whether the expected benefit of another observation justifies its acquisition cost. Existing data-driven backward-induction methods typically solve each cost-horizon operating point separately, so changing operating conditions requires repeated optimization and separate model stacks, making continuous cost adaptation and multi-horizon deployment inefficient. We propose CC-AOS (Cost- and Horizon-Conditioned Amortized Optimal Stopping), a structured amortized solver for a family of finite-horizon stopping problems with continuous costs and multiple horizons. CC-AOS learns a shared continuation-value model conditioned on the current state, absolute time, remaining horizon, and acquisition cost through joint amortized fitted backward induction. We establish that the exact value and continuation functions are nondecreasing, concave, and horizon-dependently Lipschitz in cost, encode these properties in the model architecture, and derive residual-based bounds on value and policy errors. Experiments on controlled Gaussian and time-varying non-Gaussian processes and the FordA engine-noise time-series benchmark compare CC-AOS with representative per-operating-point backward-induction solvers and tuned static stopping rules. At six unseen FordA cost-horizon pairs, one CC-AOS checkpoint achieved a lower terminal-risk-plus-sampling-cost objective than independently fitted Convex Function Learning at all six pairs, with an average reduction of 15.75 percent, while matching the tuned static thresholds on average.

</details>


### [70] [Predicting the Outcome of rTMS Depression Therapy using EEG Signals and CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22776)
*Wael Korani,Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Sadam AlQadi,Priyan Malarvizhi kumar,Reza Rostami,Reza Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合FBSE-ED时频图像表示与轻量级CNN模型，用于预测rTMS抑郁症疗法效果，在私有数据集上达到93.60%的分类准确率，优于DWT及其他深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rTMS是一种治疗抑郁症的非侵入性方法，但目前缺乏有效的早期预测工具来评估治疗效果，从而指导临床决策。

Method: 使用FBSE-ED和DWT两种时频方法将EEG信号转化为图像，设计轻量级自定义CNN模型，采用10折交叉验证在私有rTMS数据库上训练，并用另一个私有数据库进行鲁棒性测试。

Result: FBSE-ED表示法达到93.60%的最高分类准确率，优于DWT；相比EEG专用模型（EEGNet等）高出3.62-10.72%，相比预训练模型（Xception等）高出23.03-27.35%。

Conclusion: 将先进信号分解与深度学习相结合，能实现rTMS治疗反应的早期预测，该框架可解释、计算高效，适合部署于实际精神科诊所。

Abstract: Repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation (rTMS) is a non invasive therapy for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD). In this study, we generate images using two time frequency methods to represent EEG signals: Fourier-Bessel Series Expansion with Euclidean Distance (FBSE-ED) and Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT). We propose an efficient deep learning classifier to predict the outcome of rTMS depression therapy. In this study, we use a private rTMS databases to train a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) using 10-fold cross validation strategy in order to avoid any bias in our results. The results show that the FBSE-ED representation achieves the highest classification accuracy of 93.60\%, outperforming traditional time-frequency technique (DWT). In addition, the proposed architecture with FBSE-ED image representation technique outperforms more complex EEG-Specific deep learning models (EEGNet, DeepConvNet, SleepEEGNet) by 3.62-10.72% and pretrained models (Xception, DenseNet201, and MobileNetV2) by 23.03-27.35%. For more experiments, we utilize another private rTMS database as test database to show the robustness of the proposed model. Our results suggest that integrating advanced signal decomposition with deep learning can facilitate early prediction of rTMS treatment response and support more targeted clinical decision-making. The proposed framework is interpretable, computationally efficient, and well-suited for deployment in real-world local psychiatric clinics.

</details>


### [71] [LC-SEPLM: long-range contact-supervised adaptation for sequence-only protein representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22777)
*Chen Wang,Boming Kang,Qinghua Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LC-SEPLM通过在ESM2中引入长程残基对接触监督和LoRA适配，在仅使用序列推理的前提下显著提升了蛋白质语言模型的性能，尤其在远源同源识别任务上F1分数提升6.47个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质语言模型（如ESM2）主要基于序列上下文依赖进行训练，其训练目标未明确约束模型学习蛋白质折叠后形成的三维残基接触信息，导致结构信息缺失。

Method: 基于ESM2，采用LoRA和长程残基对接触监督进行适配；使用对特异性查询通过交叉注意力机制提取与长程空间接触相关的全局序列上下文；在50万个AlphaFold Swiss-Prot蛋白质上训练。

Result: 在全部8个蛋白质级别任务中优于ESM2；远源同源识别宏F1从0.6122提升至0.6769；在ESM-S EC基准上最大绝对增益达0.1771。

Conclusion: 残基对接触监督是向蛋白质序列表示引入结构信息的有效途径，同时保留序列推理能力。

Abstract: Protein language models learn transferable sequence representations. However, because they primarily model contextual dependencies along amino-acid sequences, their training objectives do not explicitly constrain the model to learn three-dimensional residue contacts formed after folding . Here, we introduce LC-SEPLM (Long-range Contact-supervised ESM Protein Language Model), which adapts ESM2 with LoRA and long-range residue-pair contact supervision while retaining sequence-only downstream inference. Pair-specific queries use cross-attention over the complete sequence to extract global sequence context associated with long-range spatial contacts. To expose the model to diverse structural information, we trained LC-SEPLM on 500,000 AlphaFold Swiss-Prot proteins. In downstream evaluation, LC-SEPLM improved all eight protein-level tasks relative to ESM2. The largest gain occurred in remote-homology recognition, where macro-F1 increased from 0.6122 to 0.6769 (+0.0647, or 6.47 percentage points). On the official ESM-S EC benchmark, LC-SEPLM also outperformed ESM-S with a maximum absolute gain of 0.1771. These results support residue-pair contact supervision as a bounded route for introducing structural information into protein sequence representations while preserving sequence-only inference.

</details>


### [72] [Multimodal Surface EMG Hand Gesture Recognition Using Query-Based Transformers for Prosthetic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22779)
*Federico Del Pup,Elisa Tentori,Manfredo Atzori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMG-CrossFormer，一种混合卷积-Transformer架构，用于通过表面肌电信号（sEMG）进行手部手势识别。该模型融合局部和全局特征，并支持多模态输入（如惯性信号），在四个NinaPro数据集上显著优于现有方法，多模态融合准确率最高达92.79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于sEMG的手势识别方法存在两大局限：一是纯卷积架构难以捕捉长程时序依赖，二是单一模态无法利用其他信号（如惯性、眼动数据）的互补信息，导致手势集扩大时性能下降。因此需要一种能整合局部-全局特征并支持多模态融合的架构。

Method: 提出EMG-CrossFormer，一种端到端混合卷积-Transformer架构。该模型包含多个单模态编码器，通过级联交叉注意力融合层整合不同模态的表征，并利用可学习的手势查询对融合后的表征进行解码。

Result: 在四个NinaPro数据集上，仅使用sEMG时平均准确率为72.33%、52.48%、79.16%、73.49%；融合惯性信号后提升至90.66%、80.40%、92.79%、92.06%，显著优于六种对比模型。

Conclusion: 联合局部-全局特征建模能提升纯sEMG解码性能，多模态融合可进一步放大这一优势，两种设计原则对复杂手势识别任务至关重要。

Abstract: Hand gesture recognition via surface electromyography (sEMG) is fundamental to prosthetic control. In this field, deep learning approaches have become the gold standard. However, current architectures struggle to scale; model performance typically decreases as the number of hand movements increases. Performance degradation is tied to the increased statistical complexity of decoding expanded gesture sets and compounded by the limitations of state-of-the-art methods, which primarily rely on low-latency unimodal convolutional architectures. Convolutions operate locally, limiting model's ability to capture long-range sequential patterns. Unimodal setups cannot leverage complementary information from coordinated signals characterizing movement execution, such as inertial and eye-tracking data. These limitations motivate architectures that integrate local and global features across multimodal physiological sequences. To bridge this gap, this study introduces EMG-CrossFormer, an end-to-end hybrid convolutional-transformer for seamless multimodal integration. EMG-CrossFormer combines representations from an arbitrary number of unimodal encoders through cascaded cross-attention fusion layers, and decodes the fused representations using learnable gesture queries. EMG-CrossFormer was evaluated on four NinaPro datasets (DB2, DB3, DB7, and DB10) and benchmarked against six state-of-the-art models using an increasing number of modalities. Using only sEMG, EMG-CrossFormer achieved mean accuracies of 72.33%, 52.48%, 79.16%, and 73.49% on DB2, DB3, DB7, and DB10, respectively. Incorporating inertial signals improved performance to 90.66%, 80.40%, 92.79%, and 92.06%. These results show that joint local-global feature modeling improves sEMG-only decoding and that multimodal fusion substantially amplifies this benefit, underscoring the value of both design principles for complex hand gesture recognition.

</details>


### [73] [What Softmax Throws Away: Mass-Aware Attention for Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22781)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出质量感知注意力（MAA），通过将标准L1归一化推广到Lp族，解决注意力机制中因重复模式导致的表征信息丢失问题。MAA无需额外参数或监督，在多个时序图模型和数据集上提升了未来链接预测AUC和表征可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制中，重复证据模式会导致分子分母同比例增长，使得不同证据量的输入产生相同的聚合结果，从而丢失预测相关的结构信息。作者旨在改善注意力机制的表征信息丰富度。

Method: 提出Mass-Aware Attention (MAA)，将标准L1归一化推广到Lp归一化族。在重复模式下，分子和分母以不同速率缩放，保留有效输入数量信息。p=1时退化为标准注意力。无需额外监督、参数或显式计数特征。

Result: 在4个连续时间动态图模型和3个数据集上，MAA在11/12个模型-数据集组合中提升未来链接AUC；线性恢复提升平均4.49%；优先连接恢复在所有12个组合中均有改善。在标记点过程、时序知识图谱、检索增强生成和时空点过程中也观察到一致效果。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种通用归一化原则，通过控制标准注意力中的重复不变性，有效提升面向预测器的表征信息丰富度。同时指出信息可访问性与任务效用仍存在区别（如NLL提升但排序保持等）。

Abstract: High task performance does not show whether a model retains prediction-relevant structural information in its internal representation. Temporal graph models, for example, can achieve high future-link AUC while basic graph statistics remain difficult to recover from the same representation. We identify one source of this gap in the weighted averaging used by standard attention: when an evidence pattern is repeated, the numerator and denominator grow at the same rate, so inputs with different amounts of accumulated evidence can produce the same aggregate.
  We propose Mass-Aware Attention (MAA), which generalizes standard L1 normalization to an Lp family. Under repetition, MAA makes the numerator and denominator scale at different rates, retaining the effective number of contributing inputs in the representation magnitude. It adds no supervision, parameters, hidden dimensions, or explicit count features, and recovers standard attention at p=1.
  Across four continuous-time dynamic graph models and three datasets, MAA improves future-link AUC in 11 of 12 model-dataset cells. Linear recovery from the same hidden representation increases by 4.49% on average, and preferential-attachment recovery improves in all 12 cells after family-wise correction. We also observe consistent evidence in marked temporal point processes, temporal knowledge graphs, retrieval-augmented generation, and spatio-temporal point processes. Information accessibility and task utility remain distinct: NLL improves in MTPP, ranking is largely preserved in TKG, additional information in RAG does not improve the diagnostic head, and downstream LayerNorm can erase the signal in STPP. These results position MAA as a general normalization principle for improving predictor-facing representation informativeness by controlling repetition invariance in standard attention.

</details>


### [74] [FMOPF: Latent Flow Matching with Constraint-Aware Interaction Priors for AC Optimal Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22788)
*Zhilin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMOPF框架，通过解耦压缩与生成、利用潜在流匹配和约束感知交互先验网络，实现了大规模电力系统可行近优解的高质量生成，优于现有扩散模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源渗透率加深，仅需单一最优调度的需求已不足，运营商需要可行近优解的分布特征用于风险量化、敏感性分析和多目标权衡评估。现有扩散模型在原始状态空间操作导致解质量下降且无法扩展至大规模系统，其根本原因在于将两个不同任务（压缩高维OPF解流形和学习条件映射）混入单一模型。

Method: 提出FMOPF框架，通过潜在流匹配（latent flow matching）解耦压缩与生成两个任务，并设计约束感知交互先验网络（Constraint-Aware Interaction Prior Network）显式建模负载与状态之间的耦合关系。

Result: 在四个IEEE测试系统上，FMOPF提供了最有效的牛顿-拉夫逊温启动，在生成方法中实现最低尾部风险，并且是首个能够扩展到数百节点系统且保持完全可行性的生成方法。消融实验证实潜在生成流程是物理可行性的必要条件，交互先验作为后期尾部风险控制器发挥作用。

Conclusion: 解耦压缩与生成是解决电力系统可行解生成扩展性问题的关键，潜在流匹配和约束感知交互先验设计有效提升了生成质量与规模适应性。

Abstract: AC optimal power flow determines the minimum-cost generation dispatch under nonlinear power balance constraints and is solved thousands of times daily in electricity market operations. Learning a direct mapping from load conditions to OPF solutions can accelerate this computation, yet with deepening renewable penetration, a single optimal dispatch is no longer sufficient. Operators require a characterization of the distribution of feasible near-optimal solutions for risk quantification, sensitivity analysis, and multi-objective trade-off assessment. Supervised neural networks provide fast point predictions but cannot capture this conditional distribution. Diffusion-based generative models can sample diverse solutions in principle, yet existing methods operating in the raw state space exhibit degraded solution quality and fail to scale beyond medium-sized systems. We identify the root cause as the conflation of two distinct tasks within a single model. Compressing the high-dimensional OPF solution manifold is one task, and learning the conditional mapping from loads to that manifold is another. This paper presents FMOPF, a framework that resolves this conflation by decoupling compression from generation through latent flow matching and by explicitly modeling load-state coupling through a Constraint-Aware Interaction Prior Network. Experiments on four IEEE test systems demonstrate that FMOPF provides the most effective Newton-Raphson warm starts, achieves the lowest tail risk among generative methods, and is the first such method to scale to systems with several hundred buses while preserving full feasibility. Ablation studies confirm that the latent generation pipeline is a necessary condition for physical feasibility and that the interaction prior functions as a late-stage tail-risk controller.

</details>


### [75] [Multimodal Domain Generalization for Depression Detection: An Attention-Based BiLSTM Network with Domain-Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22794)
*Ali Tabaraei,Federico Simonetta,Stavros Ntalampiras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于域泛化（DG）的患者独立多模态抑郁症检测框架，结合音频与文本模态，采用BiLSTM与注意力机制，并通过梯度反转层提升泛化能力，在Androids-Corpus数据集上达到93.2%准确率和94.2% F1分数，超越现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的抑郁症自动检测方法因说话人间域偏移（inter-speaker variability）导致泛化能力有限，亟需一种患者独立、泛化性强的多模态检测框架。

Method: 提出多模态框架，融合音频（MelSpec）和文本（ItalianBERT）特征，采用BiLSTM与模态内/跨模态注意力机制，结合段级融合决策；引入基于DANN的梯度反转层，通过对抗性训练抑制模型识别个体说话者，学习域不变表征。

Result: 在Androids-Corpus数据集上5折交叉验证，最优配置（MelSpec+ItalianBERT，30秒片段）加入DG后，准确率提升2.5%，F1提升3.3%，达到93.2%准确率、93.2%精确率、96.2%召回率、94.2% F1分数，超越所有现有基准。

Conclusion: 多模态融合、深度架构选择与域泛化技术的结合对构建鲁棒且可泛化的抑郁症检测系统具有关键贡献，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic depression detection with deep learning has shown promise but often suffers from limited generalization due to domain shift arising from inter-speaker variability.
