To address this critical issue, we present the first patient-independent multimodal depression detection framework that incorporates domain generalization (DG), jointly leveraging both acoustic and textual modalities. The proposed model integrates bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) with intra- and cross-modal attention mechanisms, accompanied by segment-level fusion for decision-making. Generalization is further enhanced by applying a gradient reversal layer inspired by Domain-Adversarial Training of Neural Networks (DANN), which promotes domain-invariant representations by adversarially limiting the model's ability to identify individual speakers, effectively reducing patient-specific bias. Conducting experiments on the Androids-Corpus dataset with a 5-fold cross-validation (CV) protocol, various pairings of audio and text feature extractors were evaluated over different segment durations, determining MelSpec and ItalianBERT as the optimal baseline at a 30-second segment duration. The addition of DG to this baseline yields a 2.5% increase in accuracy and 3.3% in F1-score, achieving 93.2% accuracy, 93.2% precision, 96.2% recall, and 94.2% F1-score, surpassing all existing benchmarks. Extensive ablation studies assess the impact of multimodal fusion, deep architectural choices, and DG, highlighting their combined contribution to robust and generalizable depression detection.

</details>


### [76] [Physically Verifiable Evidence and LLM-Based Reporting for Bearing Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22797)
*Yuntong Chen,Jianyu Liu,Guobin Zhao,Ziang Wang,Chao Chen,Ju Huang,Xitian Tian,Lijiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出诊断证据网络(DENet)，通过输出结构化证据记录(包括分类结果、特征频率和时域定位)来提升机械故障诊断的可验证性，同时利用受限语言模型将幻觉率从10-12%降至2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于AI的智能故障诊断系统缺乏可验证性：softmax置信度分数无法与物理知识对照检查，且生成式语言模型可能产生幻觉内容，影响决策可靠性。

Method: 提出编码器无关的多任务框架DENet，扩展输出为结构化证据记录（分类、可对比的特征频率、可检查的时域定位）；同时使用QLoRA适配的语言模型约束其仅翻译而非生成诊断内容。

Result: 在四个编码器和三个数据集上，DENet在无显著精度损失下实现约6Hz频率误差；预测与理论频率偏差构成无标签验证信号，AUROC达0.970和0.871；语言模型将无依据声明率从10-12%降至2%，消除编造数据。

Conclusion: 结构化证据输出和受控语言模型可有效提升AI故障诊断的可信度与可验证性，满足安全关键机械系统的部署要求。

Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of AI-based diagnosis in safety-critical mechanical systems hinges on validation: whether a prediction can be checked against physical reality before it is acted upon. Current intelligent fault diagnosers fail this standard in two ways. Their standard output, a class label with a softmax confidence score, is an internal statistic of the classifier, offering nothing checkable against independent physical knowledge; and the growing use of generative language models in maintenance reporting adds a second risk: hallucinated content entering reports on which decisions rest. Taking bearing fault diagnosis as the testbed, this work addresses both problems from the output side. The proposed Diagnostic Evidence Network (DENet) is an encoder-agnostic multi-task framework extending the output to a structured evidence record: the classification, a predicted characteristic frequency comparable against the theoretical value determined by bearing geometry and shaft speed, and a temporal localization of transient impulses inspectable on the raw waveform. Across four encoders and three public datasets, this evidence incurs no statistically significant accuracy cost, with a frequency error of about 6 Hz on 1,024-point segments where spectral estimation is structurally inapplicable. Centrally, the deviation between predicted and theoretical frequency constitutes a label-free, inference-time validation signal: it detects misclassifications with AUROC values of 0.970 and 0.871, and remains discriminative in the high-confidence regime where confidence-derived detectors are blind. Finally, a QLoRA-adapted language model is constrained to translate, but never generate, diagnostic content, reducing unsupported-claim rates from 10-12% to 2% and eliminating fabricated quantities.

</details>


### [77] [LithoFormer: A Robust Framework for Stratigraphic Inference via Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22804)
*Shwetha Salimath,Francesca Bugiotti,Sylvain Wlodarczyk,Sohaib Ouzineb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LithoFormer是一种基于Seq2Seq Transformer的框架，通过一次性处理多变量测井数据，实现地质分层和边界预测，相比传统滑动窗口方法，将边界误差中位数降低90%，消除地层顺序违规，并减少80%的人工专家工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化地质表征技术主要依赖滑动窗口分类，无法理解更广泛的地质上下文，导致地层分层错位，难以满足碳捕集与封存、地热开发等项目的需求。

Method: 采用Seq2Seq Transformer模型一次性处理完整多变量测井数据，使用通道独立的PatchTST主干网络结合旋转位置编码（RoPE）捕获长程地质依赖，通过解耦多任务头联合预测地质分层和边界概率，并设计地质信息损失函数强制物理约束（如叠覆律）。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上，边界误差中位数降低90%，消除地层顺序违规；人工专家工作量减少80%，消除地层不一致性。

Conclusion: LithoFormer为大规模地下建模提供了可扩展且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate geological characterization of subsurface reservoirs from well log data is essential to support projects such as carbon capture and storage (CCS), geothermal development, and extraction of natural resources. Existing automated techniques for geological characterization primarily use sliding-window classification, which limits their ability to understand broader geological contexts, often leading to misaligned formation layers. To overcome these limitations, we introduce LithoFormer, a robust framework for stratigraphic inference using a Seq2Seq transformer model that ingests entire multivariate well logs in a single pass. The framework utilizes a channel-independent PatchTST backbone enhanced with rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) to capture long-range geological dependencies across entire multivariate well logs. A decoupled multi-task head is employed to jointly predict geological zonation and precise boundary probabilities, while a geology-informed loss function enforces physical constraints such as the Law of Superposition. Validated and deployed on three real-world datasets, LithoFormer demonstrates a 90% reduction in median boundary error and eliminates stratigraphic order violations compared to traditional sliding-window baselines. It also achieves a 80% reduction in manual expert labor and eliminates stratigraphic inconsistencies, providing a scalable and reliable solution for large-scale subsurface modeling.

</details>


### [78] [OrchNAS: Orchestrated Neural Architecture Search Service for Personalised Federated Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22805)
*Keya Patel,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Mohammad Mostakim Fattah,Aneesh Krishna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OrchNAS框架，通过服务器端NAS服务自动设计适应异构边缘环境的能量感知个性化联邦学习模型，结合能量感知全局搜索、渐进式剪枝和原始-对偶优化，实现设备级资源约束下的个性化架构生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦边缘智能框架缺乏对异构设备能量、计算和内存约束的自动适配能力，需要一种自动化的神经架构搜索方法来为不同边缘服务生成个性化且能量高效的模型。

Method: 1) 服务器端NAS服务进行能量感知全局架构搜索；2) 通过渐进式贪婪能量感知剪枝策略为每个服务导出满足资源约束的子网络；3) 采用原始-对偶优化机制在架构适应过程中强制执行严格能量预算的个性化模型优化方案。

Result: 在真实世界和基准数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: OrchNAS框架能够自动为异构边缘环境设计能量感知的个性化模型，在满足设备资源约束的同时保持全局表示。

Abstract: We propose OrchNAS, an energy-aware, personalised, federated edge intelligence framework that leverages a Neural Architecture Search Service to automatically design service-adaptive models for heterogeneous edge environments. The framework orchestrates the architecture search process on a server-side NAS service, enabling edge services to derive personalised architectures under device-level energy, computation, and memory constraints. We introduce an energy-aware global architecture search mechanism that learns a compact global representation across heterogeneous services. We develop an energy-efficient architecture selection mechanism that enables each service to derive a personalised subnet that satisfies its resource constraints via a progressive, greedy, energy-aware pruning strategy. We propose an energy-efficient personalised model optimisation scheme that updates service-adaptive parameters while preserving global representations, where a primal-dual optimisation mechanism enforces strict energy budgets during architecture adaptation. Experiments on real-world and benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [79] [From Hybrid Mechanistic--Data-Driven Modeling Toward Neuro-Symbolic AI: What, Why, and How](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22811)
*Moein E. Samadi,Andreas Schuppert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid-to-NeSy (H2N)框架，将混合机制/数据驱动模型重新解释为神经符号AI实例，并衍生出结构违例率(SVR)和信念离散度(BD)两个指标，用于量化混合模型在机制部分的知识不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合建模设计主要通过架构和训练损失来定义，缺乏统一的语义接口用于跨域比较或验证，且对机制部分的认知不确定性关注不足。

Method: 通过H2N框架将混合模型映射到NeSy接口：机制知识放在语言侧，学习模块放在信念侧，有效域和约束放在逻辑侧，从而导出显式的NeSy推理泛函和逻辑-信念分解，并基于此定义SVR和BD指标。

Result: 在带标签噪声的二元分类结构化混合模型案例中，SVR和BD更高的模型在留出精度上表现出更大的变异性；在结构分布偏移下，H2N可在外推时量化不确定性，而测试精度只能事后揭示同一偏移。

Conclusion: H2N框架为混合模型的比较、验证和不确定性量化提供了统一的语义基础，特别是在分布偏移场景下具有预警能力。

Abstract: Hybrid mechanistic/data-driven models, which combine first-principles with learned components, are increasingly used in process engineering and scientific machine learning. Common hybrid modeling designs are specified primarily through their architectures and training losses, which offers a limited basis for a shared semantic interface to compare or verify them across domains, with comparatively little attention paid to epistemic uncertainty in the mechanistic part.
  We bridge hybrid modeling and neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI by reconstructing these designs as instances of NeSy interface. The resulting translation, Hybrid-to-NeSy (H2N), places mechanistic knowledge on the language side, learned modules on the belief side, and validity domains together with constraints on the logic side. For each design, H2N then yields an explicit NeSy inference functional and a logic-belief decomposition.
  From this decomposition we derive two metrics: structural violation rate (SVR), measuring whether the learned belief respects the mechanistic structure; and belief dispersion (BD), measuring how concentrated the learned plausibility is, serving as a hybrid model's epistemic uncertainty in its mechanistic part. We instantiate H2N on a case study of a structured hybrid model for binary classification under label noise and show that models with higher SVR and BD exhibit greater variability in held-out accuracy. Under structural distribution shift, H2N further quantifies a model's uncertainty during extrapolations, whereas test accuracy reveals the same shift only post hoc.

</details>


### [80] [MEMENTO: Memory-Guided Memetic Code-as-Policy Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22832)
*Alkis Sygkounas,Victor Aregbede,Amy Loutfi,Andreas Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMENTO框架，一种基于记忆引导的模因进化方法，用于长程具身任务中代码即策略的进化优化。该方法首先进化出一个 rollout 评估器，再通过记忆引导的爬山、宏变异和交叉操作生成策略变体，在Robosuite和AI2-THOR两个长程具身域中显著优于Eureka和REvolve基线，并成功实现仿真到真实机器人的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程具身任务需要策略执行大量依赖动作，而代码即策略的方式允许对决策逻辑进行检查和修正。现有LLM驱动的进化方法主要从独立生成的变体中选择，缺乏序列化的局部改进阶段，因此需要一种能进行局部搜索优化的进化框架。

Method: 提出MEMENTO记忆引导单精英模因进化框架：(1) 先进化出一个 rollout 评估器，将策略 rollout 映射为标量适应度和结构化反馈指标；(2) 适应度用于选择接受候选和下一精英；(3) 反馈指标用于约束策略提案生成，包括记忆引导的爬山（局部改进）、宏变异（大幅度改动）和交叉（重组）三种策略搜索分支。

Result: 在Robosuite Franka塔式汉诺塔操作和AI2-THOR家庭交互两个域中，MEMENTO在任务成功率和泛化能力上均优于Eureka和REvolve基线。消融实验表明零样本生成和未进化评估器无法解决任务，移除策略搜索分支会降低性能。最佳策略成功部署到真实Franka机器人上。

Conclusion: MEMENTO通过引入记忆引导的局部改进机制，有效提升了代码即策略进化的性能，并在仿真到真实迁移中展现出可行性。代码、提示和视频已开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks require policies that execute many dependent actions before task success can be observed. Representing policies as executable control pro- grams (code-as-policy) enables their decision logic to be inspected and revised after rollout evaluation. Revised programs can then be executed and compared by rollout performance, framing policy improvement as execution-guided program search. Evo- lutionary methods driven by large language models (LLMs) provide a natural mecha- nism for this search by generating variants and selecting high-performing candidates. However, existing approaches primarily select among independently generated vari- ants and lack a sequential local improvement phase. We introduce MEMENTO, a memory-guided single-elite memetic framework for code-as-policy evolution. ME- MENTO first evolves a rollout evaluator that maps policy rollouts to scalar fitness and structured feedback metrics. Fitness selects accepted candidates and the next elite, while feedback metrics condition policy proposals generated by memory-guided hill-climbing, macro-mutation, and crossover. We evaluate MEMENTO on two long- horizon embodied domains: Robosuite Franka Tower-of-Hanoi manipulation and AI2- THOR household interaction. MEMENTO outperforms Eureka and REvolve, adapted as code-as-policy evolutionary baselines, in task success and generalization to held- out Robosuite object configurations and unseen AI2-THOR scenes. Ablations show that zero-shot generation and unevolved evaluators fail to solve either domain, and that removing policy-search branches reduces performance. Finally, we deploy the best-evolved Robosuite policy on a physical Franka robot, demonstrating the feasibil- ity of sim-to-real transfer of the evolved code-as-policy. Code, prompts, and videos are available at: https://github.com/sygkounas/MEMENTO.

</details>


### [81] [Frustratingly Simple Black-Box Adaptation of Language Models via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22837)
*Ofek I. Cohen,Lior Shani,Aviv Rosenberg,Ankur Samanta,Tal Wagner,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级语言模型适配方法：通过学习一个固定的logit偏置向量（在每个解码步骤添加），在不修改模型权重或需要梯度的情况下，实现域内性能提升。该方法基于KL正则化强化学习目标，推导了闭式逆倾向得分估计器，并在数学和推理基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织希望将语言模型适配到内部使用场景，以提升领域特定任务性能并解决敏感数据的隐私问题。然而，现有的微调或提示优化方法操作复杂且成本高，因此需要一种更轻量、访问需求更低的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种黑盒方法，通过学习一个上下文无关的logit偏置向量，在每步解码时添加该偏置，无需修改模型权重或依赖梯度。从KL正则化强化学习目标出发，推导了固定偏置向量可近似最优前缀依赖校正的条件，并给出了基于轨迹、奖励和token概率的闭式逆倾向得分估计器。

Result: 在数学和推理基准测试上，该解码时干预方法相较于基础模型表现更好，且使用的可训练参数远少于传统微调方法。

Conclusion: 学习得到的logit偏置是一种在最小访问需求下适配语言模型的轻量级机制，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Many organizations aim to adapt language models for internal use, both to improve performance on domain-specific tasks and to address privacy concerns around sensitive data. However, such adaptation remains non-trivial: it often requires operationally challenging fine-tuning of open-source models or ad hoc prompt optimization. We study a minimal alternative based on a simple API-level control: allowing users to bias the model's logits with a user-defined vector. We develop a black-box method for learning a single context-independent logit-bias vector, added at every decoding step, without modifying model weights or requiring gradients. Starting from a KL-regularized reinforcement learning (RL) objective, we characterize when such a fixed logit-bias vector can approximate the optimal prefix-dependent correction and derive a closed-form inverse-propensity estimator from rollouts, rewards, and token probabilities. Empirically, this simple decoding-time intervention improves over base models on mathematical and reasoning benchmarks while using far fewer trainable parameters than conventional fine-tuning. Our results suggest that learned logit bias is a lightweight mechanism for adapting language models under minimal access requirements.

</details>


### [82] [Same Predictions, Different Reasons: The Effect of Quantization on Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22872)
*Kazi Kamruzzaman Rabbi,Md. Zami Al Zunaed Farabe,M. Sohel Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了静态后训练量化（PTQ）对五种常用CNN模型（VGG19、ResNet18、EfficientNet-B0、DenseNet161、MobileNetV2）在INT8和INT4精度下可解释性的影响。研究发现，分类精度不能可靠反映可解释性稳定性：DenseNet161保持良好特征一致性，而EfficientNet-B0在INT8下输入级特征归因显著退化，表明架构选择与量化策略同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去研究表明量化基本能保持分类精度，但量化是否会保持模型内部推理能力（即可解释性/可解释性）仍是一个开放问题。本研究旨在填补这一空白，系统评估PTQ对模型可解释性的影响。

Method: 采用Grad-CAM（空间注意力分析）和LIME（输入级特征归因）双可解释性框架，使用皮尔逊相关系数、结构相似性指数和Top-20% IoU三种互补指标，辅以删除/插入保真度分析，在INT8和INT4精度下对五种CNN架构进行系统比较。

Result: 分类精度不能可靠指示可解释性稳定性。DenseNet161在不同精度下保持强特征一致性；EfficientNet-B0在INT8下空间注意力和分类精度表现良好，但输入级特征归因显著退化。

Conclusion: 量化模型的可靠部署中，架构选择与量化策略同等重要，尤其在高可解释性要求的应用中需要谨慎选择模型架构。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has become a practical solution for deploying deep learning models on resource-constrained edge devices by compressing high-precision floating-point weights into low-precision representations without requiring retraining. Past research has demonstrated that quantization largely preserves classification accuracy; however, whether it also preserves the model's internal reasoning remains an open question. This study presents a systematic evaluation on how static PTQ affects the interpretability / explainability of five widely used CNN architectures: VGG19, ResNet18, EfficientNet-B0, DenseNet161, and MobileNetV2 at INT8 and INT4 precision. We employ a dual interpretability framework that combines Grad-CAM for spatial attention analysis with LIME for input-level feature attribution, and systematically compare full-precision and quantized models on two binary classification datasets. Interpretability is evaluated using three complementary metrics: the Pearson correlation coefficient, structural similarity index, and top-20% IoU to capture distributional and structural variations in model explanations, supplemented by deletion/insertion faithfulness analysis. The results show that classification accuracy is not a reliable indicator of interpretability stability under reduced precision. DenseNet161 maintains strong feature consistency across both precision levels, whereas EfficientNet-B0, despite achieving competitive spatial attention and classification accuracy at INT8 precision, exhibits a substantial degradation in input-level feature attribution. These findings have direct implications for the trustworthy deployment of quantized models in applications with high interpretability requirements, demonstrating that architecture selection is as important as the quantization strategy.

</details>


### [83] [Spatial Prediction of Soil Microplastics and Organic Matter Using Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22875)
*Anik Dev Nath,Md Al Amin,Bikash Kumar Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于图注意力网络（GAT）的深度学习方法，利用91个土壤样本的空间坐标、性质与土地利用数据建模空间依赖性。模型在微塑料（RMSE=625.06, R²=0.87）和有机质（RMSE=0.43, R²=0.91）预测中表现良好，但交叉验证显示泛化能力有限，主要受限于小样本量和稀疏图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算土壤微塑料和有机质含量对于评估生态系统健康和支持可持续土地利用至关重要，现有方法在此任务上存在不足。

Method: 采用图注意力网络（GAT）构建两层架构，将91个地理参考土壤样本的空间坐标、土壤属性及土地利用数据纳入模型，以捕捉样本间的局部空间交互依赖关系。

Result: 模型在微塑料预测上RMSE=625.06（R²=0.87），有机质预测上RMSE=0.43（R²=0.91），但交叉验证结果揭示泛化能力有限。

Conclusion: GAT在土壤空间预测中展现出潜力，但未来需要更密集的数据集和优化的图连接策略来提升模型的泛化性能。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of soil microplastics and organic matter is essential to assess ecosystem health and support sustainable land use. This study presents a graph-based deep learning approach using Graph Attention Networks (GATs) to model spatial dependencies among 91 georeferenced soil samples. By incorporating spatial coordinates, soil properties, and land use data, a two-layer GAT architecture was developed to capture local interactions. The final model showed strong performance, achieving RMSEs of 625.06 ($R^2 = 0.87$) for microplastics and 0.43 ($R^2 = 0.91$) for organic matter. However, cross-validation results revealed limited generalization, probably due to the small sample size and sparse graph structure. These findings demonstrate the potential of GATs for spatial soil prediction and underscore the need for dense datasets and improved graph connectivity.

</details>


### [84] [Efficient Learning of Truncated Boolean Product Distributions: Influence to the Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22889)
*Rohan Chauhan,Ioannis Panageas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从受限于子集S的独立样本中估计离散分布自然参数的问题，通过分析S在测度μ_z下的几何结构，改进了参数估计的样本复杂度至O(log n/ε²)，并利用布尔函数分析中的影响概念推广了fatness条件，同时证明了样本复杂度与模型宽度和集合最小距离存在指数依赖的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（Fotakis等人）在估计截断布尔乘积分布时，需要强局部连通性假设（fatness）或严格的反集中假设，且当截断集质量指数小时样本复杂度达Ω(2^n)。本文旨在规避这些限制，提供更高效的参数估计方法。

Method: 分析S在测度μ_z下的几何结构；利用布尔函数分析中的影响（influence）概念推广fatness条件；建立新的参数估计保证。

Result: 将样本复杂度改进至O(log n/ε²)（ℓ∞恢复），匹配非截断情形的极小化最优速率；给出基于影响概念的高效推断充分条件；建立指数级下界。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需强局部连通性或反集中假设，也无需在任意参数化下采样，显著推广了截断布尔分布参数估计的可行条件。

Abstract: Learning the natural parameters $z \in \mathbb{R}^n$ of discrete distributions $μ_z$ from independent samples constrained to a subset $S \subseteq \{0,1\}^n$ is a foundational challenge in high-dimensional statistics. Existing methods for efficiently estimating truncated Boolean product distributions, notably the work of [Fotakis et al' COLT'20, Algorithmica '22], require either strong local connectivity assumptions on $S$ -- a property denoted fatness -- or stringent anti-concentration assumptions and necessitate the total mass of the truncation set to be a constant with respect to $n$. Moreover, the results in [Fotakis et al' COLT'20, Algorithmica '22] suffer from sample complexities that scale as $Ω(2^n)$ if the mass of $S$ is exponentially small in $n$.
  In this work, we circumvent these limitations by analyzing the geometry of $S$ under the measure $μ_z$. We refine the existing parameter estimation guarantees under the fatness assumption, improving the prior sample complexity to $O( \log n / ε^2)$ for $\ell_\infty$-recovery, matching the untruncated minimax rate. We further generalize fatness using the notion of influence utilized in the analysis of Boolean functions and provide sufficient conditions for efficient inference. Notably, unlike previous work, our method does not require sampling at arbitrary parameterizations of the model. Lastly, we establish a theoretical lower bound demonstrating the sample complexity exhibits an intrinsic exponential dependence on the width of the model and the minimum distance between elements in the set.

</details>


### [85] [Learning from the Descent Direction: Adaptive Gradient Descent under One-Sided Hölder Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22906)
*Arzu Ahmadova,Ismail Huseynov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对连续可微（可能非凸）目标函数，提出了一种基于单侧 Hölder 曲率估计的自适应梯度下降方法，在非凸问题上证明了最佳迭代驻点收敛界，并在实验中取得了优于对标方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Lipschitz 或 Hölder 梯度假设控制的是全梯度变化，但在实际中，梯度的大幅度变化可能发生在与下降方向正交或有利的方向上，导致步长设置过于保守。本文旨在利用单侧 Hölder 曲率（仅约束下降不等式中的方向项）来设计更高效的自适应步长方法。

Method: 提出一种自适应标量步长方法：基于正的单侧 Hölder 曲率估计，结合简单的充分下降保障机制。该方法无需预设递减步长方案，而是根据局部下降几何自适应调整步长。

Result: 在二分类任务上，该方法取得了最低的交叉熵、目标值和梯度范数，以及最大的分类间隔；在非凸 Hölder 回归问题上，取得了最低的最终目标差距和梯度范数。

Conclusion: 单侧 Hölder 曲率是一种有效的自适应步长信号，尤其在全局梯度变化被不阻碍下降的方向放大时，该方法能够表现出优势。

Abstract: We study adaptive gradient descent for continuously differentiable, possibly nonconvex objectives under one-sided Hölder regularity. Unlike classical Hölder- or Lipschitz-gradient assumptions, which control the full gradient variation, our condition bounds only the directional term appearing in the descent inequality. This can allow less conservative step sizes when large gradient changes are orthogonal to, or favorable along, the update direction. We propose an adaptive scalar-step method based on an estimate of positive one-sided Hölder curvature, combined with a simple sufficient-decrease safeguard. For nonconvex objectives on a convex region containing the accepted update segments, we prove an explicit best-iterate stationarity bound with a rate determined by the Hölder exponent. Unlike predetermined diminishing step-size schemes, the method adapts to the local descent geometry. We evaluate the approach on two full-batch benchmarks designed to separate directional curvature from full gradient variation. On a binary classification problem, the method achieves the lowest final cross-entropy, objective value, and gradient norm, together with the largest classification margin among the compared scalar gradient methods. On a nonconvex Hölder regression problem, it attains the lowest final objective gap and gradient norm. These results indicate that one-sided Hölder curvature is an effective adaptive step-size signal when full-gradient variation is inflated by directions that do not hinder descent.

</details>


### [86] [Beyond Directed Acyclic Graphs: Causal Zeros and Causal Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22910)
*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Pearl结构因果模型（SCM）中预定义因果方向和禁止反馈环的两大限制，提出了扩展因果模型：通过引入激活算子形式化对称物理/经济约束中的因果零点，并利用因果微分方程（CDE）将表观瞬时循环归因于时间压缩，从而统一处理因果零点和反馈，并给出了扩展的do-calculus、可识别性条件和反事实语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearl的SCM框架要求所有关系必须预指定为有向因果边，且禁止反馈环，但现实中存在对称约束（如理想气体定律）和含反馈的动态系统，这些现象无法在SCM框架内自然表达。作者旨在扩展SCM以处理这两类异常现象。

Method: 1. 引入激活算子（activation operator），在扩展因果模型中形式化因果零点（causal zeros），受局部可解性和图可接受性条件约束。2. 使用因果微分方程（CDE）将有限传播状态空间系统中的表观瞬时循环视为时间压缩的产物，在瞬态区域中时间展开为无环因果过程，因果零点作为吸引平衡流形的定义函数出现。

Result: 给出了扩展的do-calculus、可识别性条件、反事实语义，并提出了开放性问题。为周期性吸引子和混沌吸引子提供了吸引子相对干预（attractor-relative intervention）的处理方法。

Conclusion: SCM框架可以通过引入激活算子和CDE来扩展，从而统一处理对称约束和反馈循环，进一步拓展了因果推理的适用范围。

Abstract: Pearl's structural causal model (SCM) framework, built on directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) and the do-calculus, is the dominant formal language for causal reasoning. Yet it carries two structural restrictions: every relationship must be pre-specified as a directed causal edge, and feedback cycles are forbidden. This paper examines two classes of phenomena that strain these restrictions. First, symmetric physical and economic constraints, the ideal gas law being the canonical case, carry no intrinsic causal direction. Direction emerges only under intervention, and which variable is solved for must be specified as part of the intervention. We formalize such constraints as causal zeros within an Extended Causal Model by adding an activation operator, subject to local solvability and graph-admissibility conditions. Second, for the class of finite-propagation state-space systems considered here, we treat apparent instantaneous cycles as artifacts of suppressed time and ground both causal zeros and feedback in Causal Differential Equations (CDEs). In these, the transient regime is a time-unrolled acyclic causal process, and causal zeros arise as the defining functions of attracting equilibrium manifolds; periodic and chaotic attractors define further regimes of the same dynamics, treated through attractor-relative intervention. We give the extended do-calculus, identifiability conditions, counterfactual semantics, and open problems.

</details>


### [87] [Hidden Boundary Motion in Transformer Optimization: Function-Space Orthogonalization of Affine Weight and Bias Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22927)
*Zhang Gongyue,Sheng Yixuan,Liu donghan,Wang Zhiyong,Ren Weihong,Liu honghai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现仿射层中权重更新会包含一个与偏置更新功能不可区分的分量（边界运动），并提出SBO-AdamW优化器来分离形状与边界成分，在Transformer上显著提升验证准确率（从81.68%到85.81%），但尚需稳定的中心化仿射参数化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重和偏置通常被视为独立的参数张量进行优化，但当仿射层输入均值非零时，权重更新包含一个与偏置更新功能不可区分的样本无关位移（边界运动），现有优化方法忽视了这一隐藏贡献。

Method: 将每次参数更新分解为形状（shape，中心化、样本变化）和边界（boundary，共享）两个正交分量；设计SBO-AdamW优化器，分别优化去除了偏置项后的权重梯度和原始偏置梯度，使用独立的Adam状态并补偿权重引起的边界位移。

Result: 在四层Transformer（IMDb）上，边界运动的中位范数占原始权重梯度范数的0.664；||ΔWμ||/||Δb||中位比值为134.7；SBO-AdamW将验证准确率从81.68%提升至85.81%，测试准确率从78.73%提升至82.73%，最佳验证点从3000步提前至800步。

Conclusion: 隐藏的边界运动是重要的优化机制，但当前补偿方案会导致偏置坐标漂移和边界能量降低，尚未形成最终通用优化器；下一步需要稳定的中心化仿射参数化方案。

Abstract: Weights and biases are normally optimized as separate parameter tensors, yet they do not represent separate functions when the input to an affine layer has nonzero mean. For an affine map $z=Wx+b$ with input mean $μ$, a weight update contains a sample-independent displacement $ΔWμ$ that is functionally indistinguishable from a bias update. We call this hidden contribution \emph{boundary motion} and decompose each update into a centered, sample-varying \emph{shape} component and a shared \emph{boundary} component. On a four-layer Transformer trained from scratch on IMDb, the bias-like term $g_bμ^\top$ has a median norm equal to 0.664 of the raw weight-gradient norm across affine layers and training checkpoints. More strikingly, the median ratio $\norm{ΔWμ}/\norm{Δb}$ is 134.7, while $\norm{ΔWμ}/\norm{Δb+ΔWμ}$ is 0.994. Thus, under AdamW, the observed boundary motion is almost entirely realized through the weight matrix rather than the explicit bias.
  We implement a diagnostic optimizer, Shape--Boundary Orthogonal AdamW (SBO-AdamW), that optimizes $g_W-g_bμ^\top$ and $g_b$ with independent Adam states and compensates the weight-induced boundary displacement. In a single-seed experiment, SBO-AdamW raises validation accuracy from 81.68\% to 85.81\% and validation-selected test accuracy from 78.73\% to 82.73\%, with the best validation checkpoint occurring at step 800 instead of step 3000. However, the moving-batch-center compensation produces severe bias-coordinate drift and strongly reduces boundary energy. The present evidence therefore supports hidden boundary motion as an important optimization mechanism, but it does not yet establish a final general-purpose optimizer. A stable centered-affine parameterization is identified as the required next step.

</details>


### [88] [Distribution-Specific Curvature Control with Finite-Sample Guarantees for Open-Weight Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22929)
*Domenic Rosati,Ali Dadsetan,Hong Huang,Xijie Zeng,Hassan Chowdhry,Subhabrata Majumdar,Hassan Sajjad,Frank Rudzicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HarmAlign方法，通过在估计的对比激活子空间上进行函数保持的谱变形，在阻止有害微调的同时保持良性任务的可训练性，并提供理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐方法面临两难：要么无法有效阻止有害微调，要么（如谱变形）过度限制模型导致良性适应也受阻。需要一种既能证书化抵御有害微调，又不牺牲模型良性适应能力的方法。

Method: HarmAlign首先估计对比激活子空间，然后在该子空间上进行函数保持的谱变形，推导出子空间能量的有限样本界和局部有害分布曲率下界；利用稳定性-进展二分法将证书化曲率转化为条件收敛速率控制。

Result: 在固定架构、有限预算的一阶攻击威胁模型下，HarmAlign能阻止直接微调和三种数据/目标自适应攻击，涵盖危险知识重学习和有害协助微调场景，同时良性任务保持可训练性；该保护跨优化器变体、跨分布外有害微调、以及意外退化和紧急失调场景均有效。

Conclusion: HarmAlign在理论和实践上实现了有害微调防御与良性适应的平衡，为开源模型的安全部署提供了更可靠的对齐保护方案。

Abstract: A short fine-tuning run can undo the safety guards of an open-weight model---retraining a refusal-trained assistant to aid weapons development or produce hate speech. Preventing such harmful fine-tuning while retaining benign adaptability remains difficult: the only prior method with an explicit curvature certificate, spectral deformation, inflates curvature globally and thereby obstructs benign adaptation along with harmful adaptation. We propose HarmAlign, which applies function-preserving spectral deformation along a estimated contrastive activation subspace. We derive finite-sample bounds for the estimated subspace energy and the resulting local harmful-distribution curvature lower bound. A stability--progress dichotomy for constant-step gradient descent turns the certified curvature into conditional convergence-rate control. Empirically, within a fixed-architecture, finite-budget first-order threat model, HarmAlign blocks direct fine-tuning and three data- or objective-adaptive attacks across a hazardous-knowledge relearning setting and a harmful-assistance fine-tuning setting, while the protected benign tasks remain trainable. The block persists across the tested first-order optimizer variants over every attack checkpoint, and under out-of-distribution harmful fine-tuning, and it extends to important cases in our threat model: accidental safety degradation and emergent misalignment.

</details>


### [89] [Spectral-Aware Analytic Class-Incremental Learning for Long-Tailed Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22931)
*Quyen Tran,Hai Nguyen,Quan Dao,Zhuowei Li,Nam Le,Trung Le,Dimitris Metaxas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分析持续学习（ACL）在长尾分布类增量学习中存在的频谱崩溃问题，本文提出几何频谱整流（GSR）框架，通过各向异性频谱滤波选择性膨胀尾部类坍缩特征值，实现计算效率与鲁棒泛化的最佳平衡，达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ACL方法在长尾分布的类增量学习场景中表现不佳，尾部类因自相关矩阵的病态条件导致严重频谱崩溃，而标准岭回归（L2正则化）的各向同性惩罚无法有效解决尾部不稳定与头部过度收缩之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出几何频谱整流（GSR），构造结构化的数据相关频谱摄动矩阵Δ，作为各向异性频谱滤波器选择性膨胀尾部类坍缩的特征方向，提升Gram矩阵的稳定秩。

Result: 理论证明GSR能保证Gram矩阵的稳定秩得到改善，确保数值稳定性；大量实验表明GSR在长尾场景下建立了新的类增量学习SOTA，实现了计算效率与鲁棒泛化的优越权衡。

Conclusion: GSR通过各向异性频谱正则化有效解决了长尾分布下ACL的频谱崩溃问题，相比传统各向同性正则化方法具有显著优势。

Abstract: Analytic Continual Learning (ACL) offers a computationally efficient alternative to gradient-based approaches. Recent ACL methods are based on Recursive Least Squares (RLS) and have achieved the state-of-the-art results compared to other alternatives. However, they falter significantly in Class-Incremental Learning scenarios characterized by Long-Tailed distributions. While the ill-conditioning of the autocorrelation (Gram) matrix is a known limitation of RLS, we demonstrate that class imbalance exacerbates this issue into a distinct spectral pathology: "tail" classes suffer from severe spectral collapse, rendering their subspaces numerically indistinguishable from noise. Standard Ridge Regression ($L_2$) fails to address this effectively as it applies isotropic regularization - a uniform penalty that is insufficient to stabilize the tail without over-shrinking the head. To address this, we propose Geometry-Spectral Rectification (GSR), a theoretically grounded framework that treats long-tailed learning as a spectral regularization problem. Unlike standard isotropic regularization (Ridge) which uniformly penalizes all eigenvalues, GSR acts as an anisotropic spectral filter, selectively inflating the collapsed eigenvalues of tail classes. We construct a structured, data-dependent spectral perturbation matrix $Δ$ that selectively inflates collapsed tail eigen-directions of the Gram matrix. Theoretical analysis proves that GSR guarantees an improved stable rank for the Gram matrix, ensuring numerical stability. Extensive experiments show that GSR establishes a new state-of-the-art for analytic CIL, offering a superior trade-off between computational efficiency and robust generalization in long-tailed settings.

</details>


### [90] [Discrepancy-Rounded Fair Bandits with Static and Time-Varying Exposure Floors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22935)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机bandit中满足最低曝光约束的问题，提出了块状模型BDQ-UCB算法，实现了确定性的块级曝光保证，并将遗憾从O(√(KT))降低到O(√(KR))，其中R是非强制性预算而非时间T。对于重叠组约束，采用Beck-Fiala零空间舍入方法，给出了匹配的极小化极大下界Θ(√(KR))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统、内容策展和资源分配中，每个提供者或组必须在周期内获得保证的最低曝光量，而不仅仅是累积平均。现有方法多依赖惩罚调整，无法精确保证曝光约束，且遗憾界受时间T主导，需要更严谨的理论框架。

Method: 将曝光约束建模为舍入问题：分数公平调度实现为整数拉取，曝光误差即为差异向量。提出BDQ-UCB算法（块依赖量化UCB），采用块状模型处理时变曝光下限；针对重叠组约束引入Beck-Fiala零空间舍入；设计MOSS残差变体和kl-UCB++残差规则实现最优性。

Result: BDQ-UCB遗憾为O(√(KR log(KT)))，MOSS变体达到O(√(KR))，匹配下界Θ(√(KR))。重叠组下，每臂舍入违反约束达Ω(s)，而Beck-Fiala方法将违反控制在臂度t以下。在合成数据、MovieLens-100k和压力测试中，无需惩罚调参即可精确满足约束，遗憾与调参Lagrangian基线相当。

Conclusion: 曝光约束bandit问题的核心在于将分数调度舍入为整数拉取，块状模型和零空间舍入可有效处理单层和重叠组约束。未来方向包括无条件覆盖的组计划采样。

Abstract: Minimum-exposure constraints arise in recommendation, content curation, and regulated allocation when each provider, arm, or group must receive guaranteed exposure inside a period rather than only in aggregate. We study stochastic bandits with exact exposure floors and show that the right object is a rounding problem: a fractional fair schedule is realized as integral pulls, and the exposure error is exactly a discrepancy vector. The main contribution is a blockwise model with time-varying floors. BDQ-UCB satisfies every block floor deterministically and has fair regret governed by the nonmandatory budget $R$, not the horizon $T$, with high-probability regret $O(\sqrt{KR\log(KT)})$. A MOSS residual variant attains $O(\sqrt{KR})$, and a matching lower bound gives the minimax rate $Θ(\sqrt{KR})$, even with positive mandatory exposure; a kl-UCB$^{++}$ residual rule adds instance-dependent optimality. The formulation becomes essential for overlapping group floors: per-arm rounding can violate a group constraint by $Ω(s)$ in the group size, whereas Beck--Fiala null-space rounding meets every group floor within the block budget with violation below the arm degree $t$, and composes with UCB at the same $R$-parametrized regret. For learned group plans, we close disjoint systems at $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{KT})$, give a dual-ledger decomposition explaining why naive index rules fail under overlap, and prove a plan-sampling rule that is pathwise feasible under an initial cover-slack condition and attains a conditional $\widetilde O(\sqrt{KT})$ guarantee, leaving the condition-free overlap rate open. Experiments on synthetic floors, MovieLens-100k genre exposure, and deployment stress tests show exact feasibility without penalty tuning and regret competitive with tuned Lagrangian baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Verbalized Particle Posterior: Bayesian Inference over Natural Language Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22961)
*Yan Zhang,Shikan Lian,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Verbalized Particle Posterior (VPP)方法，将语言化机器学习扩展为贝叶斯推断框架，通过粒子滤波（MH或SMC）维护自然语言假设的后验分布，实现不确定性量化和贝叶斯模型平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VML方法可解释但缺乏不确定性度量，且单次优化结果不稳定、方差大。需要一种能量化不确定性、稳定可靠的语言化学习方法。

Method: 将语言化学习视为贝叶斯推断问题，维护自然语言假设的粒子群，用Metropolis-Hastings或序贯蒙特卡洛方法更新粒子，通过贝叶斯模型平均进行预测。将LLM视为黑盒，无需访问logits或梯度。

Result: 在回归、分类和规则发现基准测试上，VPP优于单次VML运行，多数情况下达到或超过最优独立VML集成的性能，并消除了VML偶尔出现的灾难性单次运行失败。

Conclusion: VPP通过贝叶斯粒子滤波为语言化学习提供了不确定性量化、稳定性和可解释性，后验分布本身作为可读的假设集合，直观展示了数据支持或排除了哪些解释。

Abstract: Verbalized Machine Learning (VML) parameterizes a model as a natural-language prompt that an LLM evaluates as f(x; theta). The framework is interpretable, but it commits to a single hypothesis with no measure of uncertainty, and that hypothesis varies substantially across optimization runs on the same data. We propose the Verbalized Particle Posterior (VPP), which treats verbalized learning as a Bayesian inference problem: maintain a population of natural-language hypotheses as particles, update them with Metropolis-Hastings (VPP-MH) or Sequential Monte Carlo (VPP-SMC), and predict by Bayesian model averaging. Both algorithms treat the LLM as a black box, requiring no access to logits or gradients. A distinctive consequence follows. In classical Bayesian learning, model selection sits outside the posterior; in VPP both model structure and parameters share a single language space, and the posterior ranges over both. We evaluate VPP on regression, classification, and rule-discovery benchmarks. It improves over a single VML run on every benchmark and matches or exceeds an oracle-best ensemble of independent VML runs on most, while eliminating the catastrophic single-run failures that VML occasionally produces. Because each particle is a human-readable hypothesis, the posterior is itself something a reader can inspect, seeing in plain text which explanations the data supported and which it ruled out.

</details>


### [92] [Learned Interventions in Lean 4 grind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22972)
*Evan Wang,Simon Chess,Sophie Szeto,Theodore Meek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种失败触发的级联学习机制，在Lean~4的\grind{}策略失败后才调用学习到的启发式方法，确保不破坏原有证明能力。通过成本感知的\ematch{}过滤和前瞻步骤，分别提升了5%的运行效率和解决了5个超时定理。同时发现静态预测case split的正确率与随机无异，表明定理证明中学习最有效的方式是作为有限搜索的决策机制，并以可靠的符号回退为基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \grind{}策略依赖手工调优的启发式方法来决定实例化和case split，这些启发式方法很吸引学习的尝试，但因为\grind{}的搜索是非单调的，学习到的启发式方法可能帮助一个证明却破坏另一个，始终开启的替代方案通常收益接近零。

Method: 提出失败触发的级联学习机制：仅在原始\grind{}失败后才调用学习到的干预方法，通过构造保证不会丢失\grind{}已有的证明能力。应用于两个内部决策：成本感知的\ematch{}过滤和前瞻步骤。

Result: 成本感知的\ematch{}过滤解决了稍多的问题并提升了约5%的运行速度；前瞻步骤解决了5个原本会超时的定理。而静态预测case split的正确率与随机无异。

Conclusion: 定理证明策略中的学习最有效的方式是作为决定何时以及如何花费有限搜索的机制，并以可靠的符号回退作为基础。

Abstract: Lean~4's \grind{} tactic combines congruence closure, \ematch{}ing, and case-splitting into a single automated solver, and like any such solver, it relies on hand-tuned heuristics to decide what to instantiate and where to case-split. These heuristics are tempting targets for learning, but there is a catch: because \grind{}'s search is non-monotone, a learned heuristic that helps one proof can break another, and an always-on replacement usually nets out near zero. We avoid this by invoking a learned intervention only after stock \grind{} has already failed: a failure-triggered cascade that, by construction, cannot lose a proof \grind{} already had. We apply it to two of \grind{}'s internal decisions. A cost-aware \ematch{} filter solves slightly more problems and runs about 5\% faster. A lookahead step, proves five theorems it otherwise times out on. We also report the negative result that motivated the design: across four feature-based models, statically predicting the correct case split is no better than random, because whether a split explodes is a runtime property that the features do not capture. Our results suggest that learning within theorem-proving tactics is most effective as a mechanism for deciding when and how to spend bounded search, backed by a reliable symbolic fallback.

</details>


### [93] [Bayesian Complete-Pooling in Cross-Subject Classification for Motor Imagery Electroencephalogram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22980)
*Ethan Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验（20个数据集）对比贝叶斯完全池化模型与频率学派基线在跨被试运动想象EEG分类中的表现，发现贝叶斯方法在可靠性上有统计显著但无实际意义的提升，同时增加了预测不确定性，整体实用性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BCI分类器仅关注判别性能而忽略概率校准，由于EEG信号非平稳且点估计分类器在分布偏移下容易过度自信，研究者希望评估贝叶斯方法能否改善校准效果。

Method: 对比6个频率学派管道与对应6个贝叶斯管道（特征工程相同，贝叶斯模型通过MCMC后验采样拟合），主要指标为Brier分数（分解为可靠性与分辨率）、AUROC和香农熵，采用随机效应元分析（REML、Knapp-Hartung调整）及留一法影响分析。

Result: 贝叶斯完全池化在可靠性上有统计显著但实际意义轻微的提升，预测不确定性增加（锐度降低）；Brier分数、分辨率和判别性能无显著差异；研究间异质性低，但可靠性结果对留一法剔除敏感；贝叶斯管道能耗约为频率学派的13倍。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯完全池化对跨被试运动想象分类的实用价值有限，未来应探索跨被试和跨session的部分池化方法。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) have long sought calibration-free operation, but classifiers are typically benchmarked by discrimination alone, blind to whether predicted probabilities are well calibrated - a meaningful gap given nonstationary electroencephalogram (EEG) signals and the risk of overconfident point-estimate classifiers under distribution shift. We conducted a large-scale study contrasting Bayesian complete-pooling models against frequentist baselines for cross-subject, left-hand versus right-hand motor imagery EEG classification across 20 datasets. Six frequentist pipelines were each paired with an analogous Bayesian pipeline sharing identical feature engineering, fit via Markov chain Monte Carlo posterior sampling. Our primary metric was the Brier score, decomposed into reliability and resolution, alongside AUROC for discrimination and Shannon entropy for sharpness. Each metric was analyzed via random-effects meta-analysis (REML, Knapp-Hartung adjustment), verified by leave-one-out influence analysis. Bayesian complete-pooling produced statistically but not practically significant improvements in reliability and increases in predictive uncertainty (lower sharpness); Brier score, resolution, and discrimination showed no significant differences. Between-study heterogeneity was low across all metrics, though the reliability result was sensitive to leave-one-out removal. We additionally profiled computational cost, finding that Bayesian pipelines consumed roughly thirteen times more energy than their frequentist counterparts, a cost that remains modest relative to common household appliances. These results suggest that Bayesian complete-pooling alone offers limited practical benefit for cross-subject motor imagery classification, and that partial-pooling across subjects and sessions is a more promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [94] [Finite-Time Analysis of the Natural Policy Gradient in Finite-Horizon Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22982)
*Asha Barua,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域马尔可夫决策过程中精确自然策略梯度(NPG)算法的收敛性，分别证明了常数步长下的次线性收敛率$O(H^2/t)$和递增步长下的线性收敛率$O((1-1/ϑ_ρ)^t)$，并扩展到了线性MDP场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPG算法是TRPO和PPO等成功方法的基础，但此前缺乏其在有限时域、已知动力学条件下的精确形式收敛保证，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 针对有限时域MDP（含时变转移核），分别采用常数步长和递增步长（包括特定形式$η_t=η_0(H/(H-1))^t$）分析NPG收敛性，并拓展到线性MDP的精确投影-总体Oracle设置。

Result: 常数步长下NPG以$O(H^2/t)$速率次线性收敛；递增步长下达到$O((1-1/ϑ_ρ)^t)$线性收敛率，其中$ϑ_ρ>1$为问题依赖常数；线性MDP中恢复相同次线性速率。

Conclusion: 首次为有限时域NPG提供了完整的有限时间收敛保证，揭示了不同步长策略对收敛速度的影响，为理论理解NPG类算法提供了关键支撑。

Abstract: Natural Policy Gradient (NPG) is a well-established Reinforcement Learning algorithm that underlies widely used methods such as Trust Region Policy Optimization and Proximal Policy Optimization, both of which have demonstrated strong empirical success. In this paper, we study exact NPG in finite-horizon Markov Decision Processes with known dynamics and horizon-dependent transition kernels. We provide the first finite-time convergence guarantees for this algorithm in this setting, for which we consider both constant and increasing step size regimes. With a constant step size $η_t=η$, we prove that NPG converges sublinearly with a rate of $\mathcal{O}(H^{2}/t)$ after $t$ iterations, where $H$ is the horizon length. We also extend this constant step size analysis to linear MDPs in an exact population-projection oracle under a full support projection distribution, recovering the same sublinear rate as in the tabular setting. Furthermore, with increasing step sizes, we prove that this algorithm achieves a linear convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{\vartheta_ρ}\right)^t\right)$ for a problem-dependent constant $\vartheta_ρ> 1$, and the horizon-only robust schedule of the form $η_t=η_0(H/(H-1))^t$ where $η_0>0$ and $H \geq 2$, attains this same geometric rate.

</details>


### [95] [Label-free Industrial Fault Detection via Adversarial Inverse Reinforcement Learning: A System for Run-to-Failure Prognostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22987)
*Dhiraj Neupane,Mohamed Reda Bouadjenek,Richard Dazeley,Sunil Aryal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对抗逆强化学习（AIRL）的机械故障检测框架，无需故障标签和人工奖励设计，通过恢复内在的"健康度"奖励进行故障检测，在三个数据集上优于传统监督学习、上下文赌博机和重构方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督学习依赖大量故障标签，实际场景中标签稀缺；而所谓的"基于RL"的MFD方法实际上退化成了静态上下文赌博机，忽略了状态转移和时序折扣因子，本质仍是监督分类。

Method: 将MFD建模为离线逆强化学习问题，采用对抗逆强化学习（AIRL）框架，从观测的状态转移中恢复内在的"健康度"奖励函数，无需人工设计奖励或故障标签。

Result: 在HUMS2023、IMS、XJTU-SY三个全寿命失效数据集上，AIRL是唯一在所有数据集上实现检测后一致性非饱和的方法；CB基线无法检测渐变退化，重构方法则退化为全异常状态。

Conclusion: AIRL通过IRL框架有效捕捉了机械退化的时序动态特性，相比现有方法在无标签条件下具有更强的故障检测能力和一致性。

Abstract: Machinery fault detection (MFD) remains heavily reliant on supervised learning, which struggles with the scarcity of fault labels in real-world settings. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a framework to model the sequential nature of degradation, current ``RL-based'' MFD methods reduce the problem to a static contextual bandit (CB) formulation: by ignoring state transitions and discarding the temporal discount factor, they collapse to standard supervised classification. We propose an adversarial inverse reinforcement learning (AIRL) framework that treats MFD as an offline IRL problem. Unlike reconstruction-based approaches that rely on static error margins, or CBs that ignore dynamics, our method recovers an intrinsic "health" reward directly from observational state transitions, requiring neither manual reward engineering nor fault labels. On three run-to-failure benchmarks (HUMS2023, IMS, XJTU-SY), AIRL is the only method achieving non-saturated post-detection consistency across all datasets, while CB baselines fail to detect gradual degradation and reconstruction models collapse into always-anomalous states. Code and data: https://github.com/dhirajneupane/AIRL-MFD-DN.

</details>


### [96] [Recycling computational processes of dynamic programming for combinatorial optimization problems: a reservoir computing approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23009)
*Sora Todaka,Akihiro Yamamoto,Nozomi Akashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习的方法，利用动态规划的计算结果作为特征，通过线性回归辅助多个组合优化问题的求解，实现计算过程的多路复用，在旅行商和子集和问题上验证了该方法能够提高近似精度并减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算中，结果重用仅限于单一问题内部，而跨问题的计算过程共享难以通过人工设计实现，因此希望利用机器学习自动发现这种跨任务的算法。

Method: 基于储层计算（reservoir computing），将动态规划在组合优化问题上的计算结果作为特征，用于线性回归，从而辅助其他组合优化问题的求解。

Result: 在旅行商问题和子集和问题上的实验表明，多路复用动态规划过程相比通用特征提高了近似精度，相比独立求解减少了计算时间。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种新的计算范式，多个计算过程可以高效地共享和回收中间结果与状态，区别于传统的计算设计。

Abstract: Reusing previously computed results is a long-standing principle for reducing computational cost, but such reuse has largely been confined to a single problem's computation. Sharing computational processes across multiple simultaneously solved problems remains possible in principle, yet designing algorithms that exploit nontrivial cross-task relationships is difficult to do manually. Here, we use machine learning to discover such algorithms automatically. Specifically, based on reservoir computing, we propose a method that uses computation results recorded by dynamic programming for combinatorial optimization problems as features for linear regression, leveraging them to assist other combinatorial optimization computations. We validate the approach on the traveling salesman and subset sum problems. Multiplexing the dynamic programming process improves approximation accuracy over generic features and reduces computation time compared with independent solutions. These results suggest a new form of computation, distinct from conventional computational design, in which multiple processes efficiently share and recycle intermediate results and states.

</details>


### [97] [Mini-batch Noise Lowers Sharpness via Dominant-Subspace Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23012)
*Junho So,Dongwook Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新解释了SGD训练中Hessian矩阵主导子空间的作用：它并非损失下降的主要空间，而是解释mini-batch SGD锐度动力学的关键子空间。作者推导了由主导方向上的mini-batch噪声引起的锐度修正项，实验表明将该修正项加入GD可使其锐度演化更接近SGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究发现SGD梯度与Hessian矩阵的top-k特征向量主导子空间高度对齐，但该子空间内的更新对损失下降并无实质贡献，这一矛盾亟待解释。

Method: 理论分析了主导方向上梯度波动的平均效应，推导出由mini-batch噪声诱导的锐度修正项；并通过实验将修正项加入GD，比较锐度演化。

Result: 将推导的锐度修正项加入GD后，GD的锐度演化更接近SGD的锐度演化行为。

Conclusion: Hessian主导子空间的主要作用不是降低损失，而是通过mini-batch噪声驱动锐度动力学，从而解释SGD与GD在锐度演化上的差异。

Abstract: During SGD training, the gradients often align strongly with the dominant subspace spanned by the top-$k$ eigenvectors of the Hessian of the loss. While this seems to naturally imply that loss reduction mainly occurs within this space, prior work has shown that updates within this dominant subspace make no meaningful progress in reducing the loss. In this work, we argue that the dominant subspace is better understood not as the main space for loss reduction, but as a key subspace for explaining the sharpness dynamics of mini-batch SGD. To explain the role of the dominant subspace in reducing top-$k$ sharpness, we show how the averaged gradient over fluctuations in the dominant directions produces a sharpness correction term, and derive a sharpness correction term induced by mini-batch noise in the dominant directions. Experimental results show that adding the derived correction term to GD brings the sharpness evolution of GD closer to that of SGD.

</details>


### [98] [All in One: Generative Modeling as Mean-Field Game Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23026)
*Kun Zhao,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MFGLab开源库，通过均场博弈（MFG）的统一视角，将12种连续时间生成模型统一为变分问题的特例，并引入DI-Flow方法通过可微熵函数提升模式覆盖，实验证明统一API无损于手写实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间生成模型（如CNF、Score-based Models、Schrödinger Bridges等）虽可通过MFG统一视角关联，但交互项I在多数模型中未被利用，且MFG求解器从未被应用于生成建模，这两个空白亟待填补。

Method: 开发MFGLab（基于PyTorch的开源库），核心API为成本元组，通过四个可组合成本函数统一12种模型，自动共享训练循环、log-Jacobian和反向ODE采样器；同时提出DI-Flow，利用可微熵函数鼓励模式覆盖，并提供基于学习的MFG求解器。

Result: 在2个2-D基准测试上验证，统一API相比手写实现无损；基于学习的MFG求解器在随机动力学行上显著优于神经训练方法。

Conclusion: MFG统一框架有效覆盖了12种生成模型，开源库降低了新模型开发成本，DI-Flow和MFG求解器为生成模型提供了新工具。

Abstract: Mean-field games (MFGs) offer a unifying lens on continuous-time generative modeling: a cost tuple recovering twelve prominent models---Continuous Normalizing Flows, OT-Flow, Score-based Models, Schrödinger Bridges, and more---as special cases of one variational problem. Yet two dimensions of this space remain entirely unexplored: the interaction term $\mathcal{I}$ is set to zero in many existing models, and the rich family of MFG solvers has never been applied to generative modeling. We address both gaps with MFGLab an open-source PyTorch library whose primary API is the cost tuple: all twelve models are specified by four composable cost functions, and the training loop, log-Jacobian, and reverse-ODE sampler are shared automatically. We additionally propose DI-Flow, a novel cost design that uses a differentiable entropy functional to encourage mode coverage, and provide learning-based MFG solvers that substantially outperform neural training on stochastic-dynamics rows. Experiments on two 2-D benchmarks confirm that the unified API is lossless relative to hand-coded implementations.

</details>


### [99] [Multi-Agent Privacy Game in Federated Learning: A Unified Mean-Field View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23029)
*Kun Zhao,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于平均场隐私博弈的联邦学习框架，将隐私保护问题建模为多智能体博弈的均场极限，在任意客户端数量下都能得到可解的均衡解，并能实现个性化隐私保护，在多个任务上达到了与熵隐私基线相当的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习的隐私保护方法存在两个问题：一是注入噪声的方法组合保证有限，二是多智能体隐私博弈的纳什均衡在客户端数量增多时变得难以求解。本文旨在弥合这两类方法之间的差距，提出一种可扩展且能处理异构客户端偏好的隐私保护框架。

Method: 将隐私保护的联邦学习建模为平均场隐私博弈：每个客户端策略性地选择自己的隐私预算，仅通过单个平均场统计量与整体群体交互。均场极限使得任意数量客户端下的均衡可解，并能处理异构客户端偏好，通过对数Sobolev收缩继承了指数衰减的隐私保证。

Result: 在二次回归、逻辑回归和MNIST数据集上的实验表明，该框架能达到与熵隐私基线相当的隐私-效用权衡，同时能提供同质基线无法表达的个性化隐私保证。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了熵隐私基线（作为同质特例）和多智能体隐私博弈（作为有限群体特例），为联邦学习中的个性化隐私保护提供了一种可扩展的理论框架。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without centralising their data, yet privacy remains a persistent concern because the shared model updates can leak information about local datasets. Existing privacy-preserving methods either inject calibrated noise into client updates, limiting their composition guarantees, or formulate client privacy choices as a multi-agent game whose Nash equilibrium becomes intractable as the number of clients grows. We bridge these two lines of work by formulating privacy-preserving federated learning as a mean-field privacy game: each client strategically chooses its own privacy budget while interacting with the population only through a single mean-field statistic. The mean-field limit yields a tractable equilibrium for arbitrarily many clients, accommodates heterogeneous client preferences, and inherits an exponentially decaying privacy guarantee through a log-Sobolev contraction. The framework recovers the entropic privacy baseline as the homogeneous special case and the multi-agent privacy game as the finite-population case. Experiments on quadratic regression, logistic regression, and MNIST demonstrate that the proposed framework attains the privacy-utility trade-off of the entropic baseline while delivering a personalized privacy guarantee that the homogeneous baseline cannot express.

</details>


### [100] [Online Policy Evaluation for MDPs with Dynamic UBSR Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23030)
*Weikai Wang,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了在线策略评估算法UBSR-TD，用于带线性函数近似的动态效用型短缺风险马尔可夫决策过程，并证明了其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险感知强化学习的策略评估方法要么局限于特定风险度量类型，要么依赖模拟器，缺乏适用于完全在线场景的高效函数近似方法。

Method: 提出UBSR-TD算法，通过将损失函数纳入时序差分误差，将风险中性MDP的策略评估算法推广到动态UBSR设置，并开发了多种加速收敛的变体。

Result: 建立了算法几乎必然收敛的条件，数值实验和易腐库存管理案例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: UBSR-TD能够在风险感知强化学习中实现高效在线策略评估，且易于扩展和实现。

Abstract: Developing efficient function-approximation methods for policy evaluation is a fundamental challenge in risk-aware reinforcement learning. Existing approaches either focus on restrictive classes of risk measures or rely on access to a simulator, limiting their applicability in fully online settings. In this work, we propose computationally efficient online learning algorithms for policy evaluation in Markov decision processes (MDPs) with dynamic utility-based shortfall risk (UBSR) measures under linear function approximation. Specifically, we introduce the UBSR-TD algorithm, establish conditions under which it converges almost surely, and develop several variants designed to accelerate convergence. Our formulation shows that existing policy evaluation algorithms for risk-neutral MDPs can be readily adapted to dynamic UBSR settings by incorporating a loss function into the temporal-difference error. Numerical experiments support our theoretical findings, and an application to a perishable inventory management problem with shelf-life uncertainty demonstrates the practical effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [101] [MixQuant: Adaptive Mixed-Precision Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23047)
*Ashitabh Misra,Madhav Agrawal,Arham Jain,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MixQuant是一种技术无关的自适应量化框架，通过边际化层间依赖的失真得分，实现单次校准即可适应任意内存预算的混合精度量化，在多个模型和量化方法上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度量化方法针对单一固定内存预算求解分配方案，而实际部署中预算会变化且在校准时未知。自适应量化虽能服务任意预算，但现有方法在评估层敏感性时未考虑其与其他层量化水平的依赖关系。

Method: MixQuant提出：（1）边际化每层在随机量化上游配置下的失真得分，得到预算无关的评分；（2）在分配器自身产生的计划上校准量化器参数；（3）惩罚使层处于最低位宽的分配。最后通过单次贪心遍历服务任意预算。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B、Llama-2-7B、Mistral-7B上，基于AWQ和GPTQ，MixQuant在所有设置下超越自适应和混合精度基线，平均准确率提升最多8%，最紧预算下困惑度从12.43降至10.70，且以极低部署成本匹配ILP求解器。

Conclusion: 层敏感性强烈依赖于上游层的位宽，考虑这种依赖关系的自适应分配能显著提升量化效果，MixQuant以高效方式实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization improves the accuracy of post-training quantization by allocating higher bitwidths to sensitive layers, but existing methods solve the allocation for a single fixed memory budget. In practice the budget varies across deployments and is unknown at calibration time. Adaptive quantization addresses this with one offline calibration that serves any budget, yet current methods score layer sensitivity in a manner that does not consider its dependency on quantization levels of other layers. We show that a layer's sensitivity depends strongly on the bitwidths of its upstream layers and that this dependence shifts the resulting preferred bit allocation. We propose MixQuant, a technique-agnostic adaptive framework that wraps any base quantizer. MixQuant marginalizes each layer's distortion over random quantized upstream configurations to obtain budget-agnostic scores, calibrates the quantizer's parameters on plans the allocator itself produces, and penalizes allocations that leave layers at the lowest bitwidths. A single greedy pass then serves any budget at deployment. Across Llama-3.2-3B, Llama-2-7B, and Mistral-7B under AWQ and GPTQ, MixQuant outperforms adaptive and mixed-precision baselines in every setting, improving average accuracy by up to 8 points and reducing perplexity from 12.43 to 10.70 at the tightest budget, while matching an ILP solver at negligible deployment cost.

</details>


### [102] [The Entropic Bound for Transformers: Why Static Rank Fails and Attention-Native Rank Recovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23050)
*Byeong Hoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵界的Transformer容量度量方法，证明了线性注意力中token混合算子的本质秩是模型容量的紧致下界，并将该理论扩展到真实注意力机制（包括softmax注意力），引入注意力原生本质秩概念，恢复了完整的熵界结构（缺陷性、可达性、可恢复性），最后刻画了数据驱动的容量可预测性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律回答了损失随模型规模增长如何下降，但未解决一个前置问题：对于固定任务，求解所需的最小模型容量是多少？本文旨在通过熵界（Entropic Bound）这一谱系概念，定义并量化Transformer的任务内在容量需求。

Method: 首先在线性注意力代理中证明token混合算子的本质秩r*是紧致下界；其次验证梯度下降在低秩隐式偏差假设下能恢复该秩；然后通过受控插值实验定位真实注意力中理论失效的原因（输入条件化的混合算子）；最后引入注意力原生本质秩（最小查询-键核秩），并证明在线性和softmax注意力中熵界结构均成立。

Result: 线性注意力中r*是紧致下界且可恢复；真实注意力中直接迁移失败，原因在于注意力混合算子的输入条件化性质；引入注意力原生本质秩后，线性与softmax注意力的熵界结构（缺陷性、可达性、可恢复性）完全恢复；线性QK注意力的r*可在训练前精确恢复，而softmax注意力仅能部分恢复。

Conclusion: 本文将熵界从事后描述指标重新定义为注意力原生的容量度量，并精确刻画了其可预测性边界，为理解Transformer任务内在容量需求提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe how loss decreases as models, data, and compute grow, but they do not answer a prior question: for a fixed task, what is the minimum model capacity required to solve it? We study this through the Entropic Bound, a spectral notion of task-intrinsic capacity for Transformers. We first prove that, in a linear attention surrogate, the intrinsic rank $r^*$ of the token-mixing operator is a tight lower bound: any rank-deficient model incurs unavoidable excess risk, and the bound is achievable at $r^*$. We further show that gradient descent recovers this rank under standard low-rank implicit-bias assumptions, confirm all three properties empirically, and show $r^*$ is recoverable from data before training. We then ask whether this transfers to real attention. A naive transfer fails, and a controlled interpolation ladder localizes the cause precisely: it is not softmax and not a rank constraint, but the input-conditioned nature of attention's mixing operator, which a static weight kernel cannot summarize. Motivated by this, we introduce an attention-native intrinsic rank -- the minimum query-key kernel rank realizing the task within the attention class -- and show that under this definition the full Entropic Bound structure (deficiency, achievability, recovery) is restored for both linear and softmax attention, with the energy effective rank as the estimator robust to softmax distortion. Finally, we map the boundary of data-only predictability: $r^*$ is exactly recoverable for linear QK attention, even without the value map at scale, while softmax attention admits only partial pre-training recovery due to nonlinear inversion and kernel-value identifiability effects. Our results reframe the Entropic Bound from a post-hoc descriptor into an attention-native capacity measure with a precisely characterized predictability frontier.

</details>


### [103] [Through the Bottleneck: How Multi-head Latent Attention Separates Content from Position in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23054)
*Dhruvil S,Fenil Sojitra,Ravirajsinh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对DeepSeek-V2提出的多头潜注意力（MLA）进行可解释性研究，发现其通过共享低秩瓶颈（cKV）压缩KV缓存81%，该瓶颈仅保留内容信息（实体身份保持率98%）而丢弃位置信息，且诱导头聚集在单层（第12层），语义枢纽层（第15层）具有最高秩和扰动归因分数，瓶颈平均仅使用46%容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA已被大规模生产模型采用，但此前没有任何研究分析该瓶颈保留或丢弃了哪些信息，以及它如何重塑内部Transformer电路结构。

Method: 训练1.14亿参数Transformer（在网页/代码/数学混合数据上预训练，在TinyStories上微调），通过SVD、注意力头分类、线性探针和扰动归因分析进行机制可解释性研究。

Result: (1) cKV瓶颈学习纯内容表示，保留98%实体身份信息，丢弃位置信息；(2) 诱导头聚集在单层（第12层）；(3) 第15层是语义枢纽层，具有最高SVD有效秩和最强扰动归因分数；(4) 瓶颈平均仅使用46%容量，存在过度配置。

Conclusion: MLA不仅是被动压缩注意力，还重塑了模型组织内容、位置和电路结构的方式。本文仅作为初步数据点，存在范围限制。

Abstract: Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA), introduced in DeepSeek-V2, compresses key-value pairs through a shared low-rank bottleneck (cKV), achieving 81% KV-cache reduction during inference. Despite its adoption in massive production models, no prior work has studied what information this bottleneck preserves or discards, nor how it reshapes internal transformer circuits. We present the first comprehensive mechanistic interpretability study of MLA, training a 114M-parameter transformer (pretrained on a web/code/math mixture, fine-tuned on TinyStories) and analyzing its representations through SVD, attention head taxonomy, linear probing, and a disruption-attribution analysis. Our key findings are: (1) the cKV bottleneck learns a pure content representation, preserving entity identity (98% retention) while discarding positional information, validating MLA's separation of content from position via RoPE; (2) induction heads co-locate at a single layer (Layer 12), unlike their distributed formation in standard MHA; (3) a single "semantic hub" layer (Layer 15) simultaneously exhibits the highest SVD effective rank and strongest disruption-attribution score; and (4) the bottleneck is globally over-provisioned, using only 46% of its capacity on average. These findings suggest MLA does not merely compress attention passively, but reshapes how the model organizes content, position, and circuit structure. We view this as an initial data point and detail scope limitations in Section 5.

</details>


### [104] [Self-Boosting Vision-Language Models with Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23125)
*Shuai Wang,Daoan Zhang,Zhe Tang,Hao Cheng,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NOPD（噪声学生在线自蒸馏）方法，无需外部模型或人工标注，通过干净输入与噪声输入之间的预测差异产生自监督信号，提升视觉语言模型性能，在多个视觉推理任务上匹配甚至超越强化学习和外部蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM后训练方法依赖人工标注数据、外部模型蒸馏或强化学习等外部监督，限制了模型在没有外部监督下的提升能力。作者希望提出一种无需任何外部模型或真实答案的自我改进方法。

Method: 提出NOPD（Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation），核心思想是利用模型在干净输入和带噪输入下的预测差异作为自监督信号。模型从带噪输入中学习，同时以自身在干净输入下的预测作为token级别的监督信号。

Result: 在五个视觉推理任务上有效；使用Geometry3K的2.1K样本训练，Qwen2.5-VL-7B在验证集上提升20个点；在MathVista上获得7.4个点的增益；在三个模型、12个基准测试上均取得改进。

Conclusion: NOPD是一种简单有效的自蒸馏方法，可以匹配甚至超越依赖外部监督的强化学习和蒸馏方法，是一种通用的VLM增强方法。

Abstract: Post-training enables vision-language models (VLMs) to understand human instructions and perform various downstream tasks. Current post-training methods usually rely on human-annotated data, distillation from external models, reinforcement learning with human feedback, or verifiable answers. This limits their ability to improve without external supervision. To tackle this, we propose NOPD (Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation), a simple yet effective self-distillation approach that improves VLMs without any external models or ground-truth answers. Our key insight is that prediction discrepancies between clean and corrupted inputs naturally induce a self-supervision signal. In NOPD, the model learns from corrupted inputs while using its own predictions under clean inputs as token-level supervision. We show the effectiveness of NOPD on five visual reasoning tasks; it can match and even outperform reinforcement learning approaches or distillation from external models. Notably, when trained with 2.1K samples from Geometry3K, NOPD improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 20 points on its validation set. It also shows generalization on out-of-distribution test sets and achieves 7.4 point gains on MathVista. Furthermore, we demonstrate that NOPD is a general approach to enhance VLMs, achieving improvements across three models on 12 benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [Diffusion-Guided Search via Exponential Tilting (DiffTilt): An Application to Falsification of Safety-Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23134)
*Tanmay Khandait,Preetom Biswas,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Giulia Pedrielli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffTilt框架，通过扩散模型的指数倾斜来联合优化环境和系统执行分布，有效解决自动驾驶和网络-物理系统中罕见安全关键故障的发现难题，在ARCH-COMP基准测试中取得竞争性或更优的故障检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪造方法依赖条件采样策略，将环境和系统执行的联合分布进行因子分解，导致乘法稀有效应——故障诱导输入和故障诱导轨迹的同时稀缺使得穷举搜索代价高昂。需要一种能有效处理联合空间中罕见故障的新方法。

Method: 提出DiffTilt框架，通过指数倾斜扩散模型诱导的联合分布，利用扩散引导采样作为联合空间中的重要性采样，引导分数实现概率质量的KL最优重新分配，将失效概率放大的同时，联合生成模型作为可复用先验，系统仿真仅用于学习评分函数。

Result: 在ARCH-COMP基准测试和提出的拖拉机-拖车基准测试中，DiffTilt相比现有方法达到竞争性或更优的伪造性能，尤其在规范定义不限于STL公式时增益更大。

Conclusion: DiffTilt通过分布倾斜框架有效克服了乘法稀有效应，证明了倾斜可以放大失效概率并且严格优于条件采样方法，为实现自适应、高效的故障发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Discovering rare safety-critical failures in autonomous and cyber-physical systems is a fundamental challenge in verification and validation. Existing falsification approaches rely on conditional sampling strategies that factor the joint distribution over environments and system executions, and therefore suffer from multiplicative rarity effects: the simultaneous scarcity of failure-inducing inputs and failure-inducing traces makes exhaustive search prohibitively expensive.
  This paper develops DiffTilt, a distributional framework that exponentially tilts a diffusion model-induced joint distribution over environments and executions. We show that diffusion-guided sampling admits an exact interpretation as importance sampling in the joint space, where guidance scores induce a KL-optimal reallocation of probability mass towards failure-relevant behaviors. We further show that tilting provably amplifies failure probability and strictly outperforms conditional sampling, which is limited by multiplicative rarity. In this framework, the joint generative model serves as a reusable prior over scenarios and need not faithfully represent the system under test. Expensive system simulations are instead limited to learning a scoring function that characterizes scenario quality, enabling their selective and adaptive use.
  We study DiffTilt on ARCH-COMP benchmarks, and we propose an additional tractor-trailer benchmark showing the behavior of several approaches when scenario generation is guided by a well-defined specification rather than a reward. The proposed method achieves competitive or improved falsification performance compared to state-of-the-art approaches, with larger gains when specification definition is not limited to STL formulas.

</details>


### [106] [Foundation Models and Fine-Tuning: Toward a New Generation of Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23146)
*Morad Laglil,Bertrand Pracca,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于大语言模型启发的时序基础模型，探讨了其架构、预训练策略和优化方法，并通过微调实验证明该步骤能持续提升零样本预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列预测需要针对特定数据集设计模型和手动调参，缺乏通用性。受大语言模型成功启发，研究者希望构建统一的基础模型，实现零样本时间序列预测，减少人工干预。

Method: 回顾了时序基础模型的主要架构（如Transformer变体）、预训练策略（大规模多样化时序数据）和优化方法，并对预训练后的模型进行微调以适配特定数据集。

Result: 微调步骤能够持续提升模型在特定数据集上的预测精度，优于纯零样本基线。

Conclusion: 时序基础模型结合微调策略是一种有效的通用时间序列预测方案，能兼顾零样本泛化能力和特定任务适配性。

Abstract: Inspired by recent breakthroughs in large language models for natural language processing, foundation models have emerged as a promising paradigm for zero-shot time series forecasting, enabling accurate predictions on datasets never seen during pre-training. Ranging from tens to hundreds of millions of parameters, these models are pre-trained on vast and diverse collections of time series, learning generalizable representations that support both point and probabilistic forecasting. This approach alleviates the need for dataset-specific model design and manual tuning, offering a unified solution across forecasting problems. In this work, we review the main architectures, pre-training strategies, and optimization methods underpinning these models. We further investigate post-pre-training fine-tuning of selected foundation models to enhance their performance on specific datasets. Our empirical results demonstrate that this step consistently improves forecasting accuracy over the zero-shot baseline.

</details>


### [107] [XGRVFL-MV: Residual-Coupled Graph-Embedded Multi-View Random Vector Functional Link Network with FleXi Guardian Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23149)
*Yogesh Kumar,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于残差耦合图嵌入的多视图RVFL模型（XGRVFL-MV），通过图嵌入保留视图特定几何结构、使用XG损失函数限制大预测残差影响、引入残差耦合项建模视图间关系，在多个基准数据集上取得竞争性分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MV-RVFL方法难以同时保留视图特定几何结构、限制大预测残差影响以及建模多视图间关系。

Method: 为每个视图构建RVFL表示；利用局部Fisher判别分析的图嵌入保留几何结构；采用有界非对称XG损失函数进行残差学习；引入残差耦合项促进视图间残差一致性；使用基于Nesterov加速梯度下降的无逆一阶优化求解。

Result: 在UCI、KEEL、AwA、Corel5k基准数据集上，XGRVFL-MV在分类性能上优于基线方法，并通过统计分析和超参数敏感性分析验证了有效性。

Conclusion: XGRVFL-MV通过图嵌入、XG损失和残差耦合机制有效提升了多视图分类性能。

Abstract: Random Vector Functional Link (RVFL) networks provide an efficient randomized learning framework for classification. Existing multi-view RVFL methods utilize complementary information from multiple views. However, preserving view-specific geometric structure, limiting the influence of large prediction residuals, and modeling relationships between multiple views remain challenging. This paper proposes a Residual-Coupled Graph-Embedded Multi-View RVFL model with fleXi guardian loss (XGRVFL-MV) for multi-view classification. The proposed model constructs RVFL representation for each view, incorporates graph embedding with intrinsic and penalty graphs constructed using the Local Fisher Discriminant Analysis weighting scheme. It also uses the bounded and asymmetric FleXi Guardian (XG) loss for residual learning. A residual-coupling term is introduced to encourage consistency among view-specific prediction residuals while preserving view-specific representations. The resulting optimization problem is solved using an inversion-free first-order optimization procedure based on Nesterov accelerated gradient descent. We evaluate the proposed model on UCI, KEEL, AwA, and Corel5k benchmark datasets. Experimental results, together with statistical analyses and hyperparameter sensitivity analyses, show that XGRVFL-MV achieves competitive classification performance compared with the baseline methods across the evaluated benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [108] [In-Context Learning as Implicit Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23153)
*Masahiro Kaneko,Timothy Baldwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种基于分数的上下文学习（ICL）与策略梯度优化（REINFORCE算法）之间的结构对应关系，并通过理论证明和实验验证了自注意力机制可以实现类似奖励权重的聚合，且注意力权重与示例分数之间存在强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有实证发现LLM可以通过迭代生成样本和评估分数来改进输出，但其理论基础尚不清晰。本文旨在从理论层面解释这一现象的内在机理。

Method: 通过构造性证明自注意力机制在特定权重矩阵配置下可实现类似于REINFORCE算法的奖励加权聚合；推导了有界注意力更新下分布偏移的上界，建立了与KL约束策略优化的类比关系；并通过多模型实验验证理论。

Result: 证明了分数条件ICL与策略梯度优化的结构对应关系；推导了分布偏移的精确上界；实验表明LLM能有效利用分数信息将输出分布向高分示例偏移，注意力权重与示例分数呈强相关。

Conclusion: 本文为基于分数的上下文学习提供了理论基础，揭示了其与策略梯度优化的深层联系，并验证了自注意力机制在其中的关键作用。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can iteratively improve their outputs by incorporating generated samples and their corresponding evaluation scores as in-context examples. Despite these empirical findings, the theoretical foundations underlying this phenomenon remain poorly understood. In this paper, we show that score-conditioned In-Context Learning (ICL) admits a structural correspondence to policy gradient optimization. We first provide a constructive proof that self-attention mechanisms can implement reward-weighted aggregation analogous to the REINFORCE algorithm under specific weight matrix configurations, and discuss the relationship between this construction and the behavior of pretrained transformers. The correspondence is directional in hidden-state space and holds exactly only under the stated simplifying conditions; we quantify its strength empirically. Within our simplified hidden-state model, we furthermore derive an exact upper bound on the distribution shift induced by a bounded attention update, yielding a trust-region-like analogy to KL-constrained policy optimization. We validate our theory through extensive experiments across multiple LLMs, demonstrating that LLMs effectively utilize score information to shift output distributions toward high-scoring exemplars, and that attention weights exhibit a strong correlation with example scores.

</details>


### [109] [Wrong Design Intent Is Worse Than None: A Derangement-Control Diagnosis of Header Conditioning in CAD Program Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23191)
*Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过CADCON基准测试，研究发现代码LLM在参数化CAD生成中确实会读取设计意图头（header），错误头会显著降低生成质量，而正确的头带来的提升被以往指标高估。该效应通过因果控制和独立几何指标得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究在代码LLM的CAD生成中附加设计意图头（header），但缺乏独立于条件本身的因果控制指标来验证模型是否真正读取了header内容，而非仅依赖边际分布或机械混淆。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B上LoRA微调，为CadQuery风格的草图-拉伸程序添加五特征设计意图头；使用{0%, 40%}前缀 × {正确, 错误, 掩码}头的矩阵设计，通过可执行几何断言（B-rep实体）独立评分，并与正则表达式提取的代码指标对比。

Result: (i) 错误头在40%前缀下使多边形/薄几何的达标率从0.43降至0.30/0.21；(ii) 打乱header的对照组不受错误头影响，验证了因果效应；(iii) 独立几何指标显示正确头的提升被高估（正则指标+0.21 vs 几何+0.02）；(iv) 0%前缀时无条件基线无法生成有效CAD，错误头在40%前缀下是主动误导向而非噪声。

Conclusion: 模型确实会读取设计意图头，错误头会主动误导生成而非仅随机噪声，且以往使用的代码级指标存在循环性，高估了正确头的效果。研究提供了因果证据和独立几何评估框架。

Abstract: Fine-tuned code LLMs can be conditioned on a lightweight design-intent header to steer parametric CAD generation, but whether the model actually reads the header's content has not been tested under a metric independent of the conditioning itself, nor with a causal control. We study CADCON, a five-feature design-intent header prepended to CadQuery-style sketch-extrude programs during LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B, re-scored by executable geometric assertions on the produced B-rep solid, sharing no code with the header-defining regex extractor. Across three seeds and a pre-registered {0%, 40%}-prefix $\times$ {correct, wrong, masked}-header matrix we find: (i) in conditional completion (40% prefix), a semantically wrong header degrades adherence below the no-header baseline (0.43 $\to$ 0.30/0.21 text/token) on design intents the model can render unconditioned -- polygonal and thin geometries; circle and tall intents sit at a baseline generation floor for this checkpoint ($\approx$0 in both compared arms) and are uninformative for this contrast; (ii) a derangement control -- retrained with shuffled ground-truth headers, identical header marginal but destroyed content correlation -- remains competent yet is immune to wrong headers, while the standard model is not (text headers; interaction significant on 3/3 seeds, p $\leq$ $4.2\times10^{-3}$): the harm requires the learned header$\to$program mapping, excluding the marginal/mechanical distribution-shift confound; (iii) the independent metric deflates the apparent benefit of a correct header (token: +0.21 regex $\to$ +0.02 geometry), quantifying metric circularity; (iv) the harm is regime-specific -- at 0% prefix the unconditioned baseline cannot generate valid CAD at all. Wrong intent is not noise: it actively misdirects generation.

</details>


### [110] [Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling for Anomaly Detection in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23197)
*Youngseok Hwang,Joonsung Kwon,Geonwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPR-GM是一个面向小样本工业异常检测的预测框架，通过LLM从系统文档中提取物理耦合关系构建领域先验图，无需可学习参数，在SKAB基准上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的多元传感器异常检测方法在小规模物理系统中，由于标注异常稀少、正常数据有限，容易捕捉虚假相关并产生不稳定的传感器拓扑结构。

Method: 利用LLM从系统文档提取传感器间有向物理耦合，编码为二值领域邻接矩阵作为结构门控，再通过正常数据中的皮尔逊相关系数调制；异常分数进一步由变异系数推导的传感器级可靠性加权；所有图与权重组件在训练前固定，无可学习参数。

Result: 在SKAB基准上，DPR-GM在F1、AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于基于图、统计和深度学习的基线方法。

Conclusion: 在数据稀缺的CPS场景中，领域结构化的图先验可作为全学习拓扑的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection on multivariate sensor time series is critical for industrial monitoring of cyber-physical systems (CPS), where even subtle deviations from normal behavior can indicate process disruption. Recent graph-based approaches have made significant progress, but they often struggle in small-scale physical systems with scarce labeled anomalies and limited normal data. In such settings, graph-based models tend to capture spurious correlations and produce unstable sensor topologies. We propose DPR-GM (Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling), a forecasting-based framework that incorporates system design knowledge into graph construction. DPR-GM leverages a large language model (LLM) to extract directed physical couplings between sensor pairs from system documentation, which are encoded as a binary domain adjacency matrix serving as a structural gate over sensor relations. This gate is then modulated by Pearson correlations estimated from normal training data. The anomaly score is further weighted by sensor-level reliability derived from the coefficient of variation. All graph and weighting components are fixed prior to training and add no learnable parameters. On the SKAB benchmark, DPR-GM outperforms graph-based, statistical, and deep learning baselines across F1, AUROC, and AUPRC, showing that domain-structured graph priors are a practical alternative to fully learned topologies in data-scarce CPS.

</details>


### [111] [Variance-Preserving Orthogonal Selection (VPOS): Greedy Feature Selection via Orthogonal Deflation in PCA Loading Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23198)
*Baran Koseoglu,Berrin Yanikoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPOS是一种基于加权PCA加载空间的贪婪无监督特征选择框架，通过零空间投影消除已选特征方差方向，保证选择正交的协方差结构分量。在8个基准上，VPOS全部取得最低重构MSE，速度比基于图的方法快10-140倍，且消融实验证明投影操作使MSE降低10-73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一种高效的无监督特征选择方法，解决现有基于图的方法计算开销大、可扩展性差的问题，同时保证选择特征的方差保持与正交性。

Method: 在加权PCA载荷空间中进行贪婪选择，每次选择后通过零空间投影消除已选特征的方差方向，确保后续选择覆盖正交的协方差结构。超参数d通过最小化重构MSE的灵敏度扫描确定。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上全部取得最低重构MSE；运行速度比基于图的方法快10-140倍；消融实验表明投影操作使MSE降低10-73%。
